### [1] [GNN-RSMA: An Interference Management Framework for a Large-Scale HAPS Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00380)
*Afsoon Alidadi Shamsabadi,Animesh Yadav,Halim Yanikomeroglu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于UE聚类与速率分割多址（RSMA）的干扰管理方案，用于高空平台站（HAPS）与地面网络组成的垂直异构网络（vHetNets）。该方法将网络建模为异构图，利用图神经网络（GNN）高效分配RSMA的公共与私有功率，以最大化最小频谱效率，在极低计算成本下达到与传统凸优化方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAPS高海拔部署导致与UE之间存在强视距（LoS）链路，造成UE间信道高度相关；同时HAPS覆盖范围广、服务用户多，导致有限无线资源被众多UE共享，产生严重的资源块内（intra-RB）干扰，亟需高效的干扰管理方案。

Method: 1) 提出基于UE聚类与RSMA的干扰管理方案；2) 将网络建模为异构图；3) 开发图神经网络（GNN）高效分配RSMA的公共和私有功率，目标是最大化最小频谱效率（max-min SE），实现快速且可扩展的功率分配。

Result: 仿真结果表明，提出的GNN-RSMA干扰管理算法优于传统多址接入方案，其公平性和最差用户性能与基于连续凸逼近（SCA）的优化方法相当，但计算成本仅为后者的一小部分。

Conclusion: GNN-RSMA方案在HAPS与地面网络组成的vHetNets中，能够以极低的计算开销实现接近最优的频谱效率和良好的用户公平性，是一种高效且可扩展的干扰管理解决方案。

Abstract: Integrating non-terrestrial networks (NTN) with terrestrial infrastructure is a key enabler of next-generation wireless systems, providing ubiquitous connectivity while meeting stringent rate and latency requirements. In particular, high altitude platform stations (HAPS) can complement terrestrial networks and jointly form vertical heterogeneous networks (vHetNets), extending coverage while delivering high-capacity, reliable, and low-latency connectivity for user equipments (UEs) including ground users and uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs). However, the high altitude deployment of HAPS establishes strong line-of-sight (LoS) links to UEs, creating highly correlated channels among UEs. Moreover, the wide coverage footprint of HAPS enables it to serve a large number of UEs, forcing limited radio resources to be shared among many UEs and resulting in significant intra-resource block (RB) interference. To address this challenge, we propose an interference management scheme based on UE clustering and rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA). Specifically, the network is modeled as a heterogeneous graph, and a graph neural network (GNN) is developed to efficiently allocate the common and private RSMA powers, maximizing the minimum spectral efficiency (SE) in a fast and scalable manner. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed GNN-RSMA interference management algorithm outperforms conventional multiple access schemes while achieving fairness and worst-user performance comparable to successive convex approximation (SCA)-based optimization at only a fraction of its computational cost.

</details>


### [2] [Channel-Agnostic Semantic Compression for Bandwidth-Limited Visual Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00394)
*Xuanhao Luo,Ruichen Gao,Zhizhen Li,Mingzhe Chen,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出RQ-NAC，一种与信道无关的语义压缩框架，用于视觉通信。它利用残差量化生成可扩展的离散语义表示，实现对率失真权衡的细粒度可控，并结合n-gram驱动的算术编码模块利用潜在索引间的上下文依赖进行无损熵编码。实验表明RQ-NAC在未压缩视觉数据上实现超过600倍压缩，同时保持高感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉通信系统在带宽受限条件下存在两方面不足：一是联合信源信道编码将表示学习与信道模型紧密耦合，缺乏跨环境灵活性；二是基于生成的重建技术可能产生语义不一致的输出。因此需要一种与信道无关且能保证语义一致性的高效压缩方案。

Method: RQ-NAC框架包含两个核心组件：1) 残差量化（Residual Quantization）产生可扩展的离散语义表示，实现对率失真权衡的细粒度、可预测控制；2) n-gram驱动的算术编码模块，利用潜在索引间的上下文依赖实现无损熵编码，进一步提升压缩效率。

Result: 大量实验表明，RQ-NAC在未压缩视觉数据上实现了超过600倍的压缩比，同时保持较高的感知质量，能够在带宽受限条件下实现高效、灵活且可靠的语义传输。

Conclusion: RQ-NAC通过将语义压缩与信道模型解耦，结合残差量化与上下文熵编码，为带宽受限的视觉通信提供了一种高效、灵活且可靠的解决方案，在极高压缩比下保持了良好的感知质量。

Abstract: Bandwidth-limited visual communication systems require efficient transmission of high-dimensional data under dynamic wireless conditions. Existing approaches either rely on joint source-channel coding, which tightly couples representation learning with channel models and lacks flexibility across varying environments, or adopt generative reconstruction techniques that may introduce semantically inconsistent outputs. In this paper, we propose RQ-NAC, a channel-agnostic semantic compression framework for visual communication. The proposed method leverages residual quantization to produce scalable discrete semantic representations, enabling fine-grained and predictable control over the rate-distortion tradeoff. To further enhance compression efficiency, we integrate an n-gram-driven arithmetic coding module that exploits contextual dependencies among latent indices for lossless entropy coding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RQ-NAC achieves over 600$\times$ compression relative to uncompressed visual data while preserving high perceptual quality. The results show that our approach enables efficient, flexible, and reliable semantic transmission under bandwidth-constrained conditions.

</details>


### [3] [Frequency Coding over Noisy Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00539)
*Bo-Yu Su,Hsin-Po Wang,Venkatesan Guruswami*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究利用DNA分子的频率向量编码信息的问题。作者提出了一种低复杂度的编码方案，可以在无条件（不要求至少√R条不同字符串）的情况下达到每字符串log₄R比特的信息容量，并将该方案推广到存在测序噪声的场景，分析了噪声对信息容量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA分子非常小，可以利用其频率向量来编码消息。发送者将M_X份特定DNA字符串注入池中，接收者通过测序池来恢复M_X。但存在两个不确定性来源：(a) M_X太大无法精确计数，只能通过采样估计；(b) DNA测序仪可能有噪声。此前Tamir等人仅在满足至少√R条不同字符串的条件下证明低复杂度非编码方案能达到log₄R比特容量，本文目标是实现无条件达到该容量，并处理测序噪声问题。

Method: 作者设计了一种低复杂度的编码方案，在无需额外条件下达到log₄R比特的信息容量，解决了前人工作的条件限制。随后将该方案推广以处理测序噪声，通过Fourier变换分析噪声对容量的影响。噪声惩罚量为log₂detW（由Fourier变换使用带来的线性项），其中detW项与Gerzon、Shomorony和Weinberger获得的结果渐近相同，但本文方案以少量码率换取实际复杂度更低。

Result: 本文证明：(1) 低复杂度编码方案可以无条件达到每字符串log₄R比特的信息容量；(2) 推广到有噪声场景后，噪声将使总比特数减少log₂detW，外加一个由于Fourier变换使用导致的线性项；(3) log₂detW的惩罚与前人Gerzon等人的结果渐近一致，且方案在码率与复杂度之间取得了实际可行的折衷。

Conclusion: 本文提出并验证了一种不受条件限制的低复杂度编码方案，能够达到DNA频率向量编码的信息理论容量，同时有效处理了测序噪声的影响。该方案在实际复杂度与码率损失之间取得了良好平衡，为DNA信息存储和通讯提供了实用的编码策略。

Abstract: DNA molecules are so small that it might be practical to use their frequency vectors to encode messages. More precisely, a sender can inject $M_X$ copies of the string $X =$ CATCATCAT into a pool and the receiver can recover $M_X$ by sequencing the pool. There are, however, two sources of uncertainty: (a) $M_X$ is usually too big to be counted exactly, but is estimated by sampling. (b) The DNA sequencer could be noisy; it may have difficulty distinguishing CATCATCAT from CATGATCAT.
  Recently, Tamir, Weinberger, and Guillén i Fàbregas clarified the amount of information the frequency vector can carry under (a). They showed that each string can carry about $\log_4 R$ bits, where $R$ is the average number of times each string is read. They also showed that $\log_4 R$ bits can be achieved by a low-complexity uncoded scheme under the condition that there are at least $\sqrt R$ distinct strings. In this paper, we show that a low-complexity coded scheme can achieve the same $\log_4 R$ bits unconditionally. We then generalize the scheme to handle sequencing noise, (b), and show that the noise penalizes the total number of bits by $\log_2 \det W$, together with a linear term due to the use of Fourier transforms in our proof. The former penalty $\log_2 \det W$ is asymptotically the same as that obtained by Gerzon, Shomorony, and Weinberger; our scheme trades a small amount of rate for practical complexity.

</details>


### [4] [An Information Theoretic Treatment of Yager's Probability Distribution Negation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00594)
*Roberto Bruno,Ugo Vaccaro*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文对Yager提出的概率分布取反操作及其推广形式进行了全面的信息论分析，利用信息论和主化理论工具，统一、扩展并加强了此前已知的多个性质，为Yager取反作为最自然、最合理的概率分布取反定义提供了强有力的理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yager在2015年提出了概率分布取反的定义，但此前关于其性质的研究较为分散，缺乏一个统一的理论框架来系统性地理解和验证该定义的合理性与最优性。

Method: 利用信息论（information theory）和主化理论（majorization theory）的工具，对Yager取反及其推广形式进行综合性的理论分析，将此前已知的多个性质统一到一个共同的分析框架中。

Result: 成功统一、扩展并加强了Yager取反的多个已知性质，证明其在多种信息论准则下都是最自然和最优的概率分布取反定义。

Conclusion: Yager取反在各种信息论标准下都被证明是概率分布取反最自然、最有原则性的定义，本文为其提供了坚实的理论依据。

Abstract: In the seminal paper (Yager 2015), Yager defined the negation of a probability distribution $\mathbf{p}=(p_1,\dots,p_n)$, as the distribution $\overline{\mathbf{p}} = (\overline{p}_1,\dots,\overline{p}_n)$, where $\overline{p}_i = ({1-p_i})/({n-1}),$ for $ i=1, \ldots , n.$ In this paper, we present a comprehensive information-theoretic analysis of Yager's negation and its generalizations. Using tools from information theory and majorization theory, we unify, extend, and strengthen a number of previously known properties of Yager's negation within a common framework. Overall, our results offer strong theoretical justification for Yager's negation as the most natural and principled definition of probability distribution negation under various information theoretic criteria.

</details>


### [5] [Symplectic Barnes-Wall GKP Codes: Deterministic $O(N \log^2 N)$ Decoding and Logarithmic Rate Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00601)
*Shanxiang Lyu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文构建了Barnes-Wall格子的显式辛实现，由此生成一族多模GKP码，编码率R = ½log₂N，并配备确定性O(N log²N)有界距离解码器。通过递归生成器G_{m+1} = [[G_m, 0], [G_m, R_m G_m]]（其中R_m = I + Ω）同时保证量子稳定子的辛整数性和Barnes-Wall解码结构的保持。码距离恒为Δ² = 1（以2π为单位），体现了显式的距离-速率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模GKP码在容错量子计算中具有重要作用，但如何构造既具有高编码率又具备高效解码的GKP码仍具挑战。Barnes-Wall格子具有良好结构性质，但其辛实现和与GKP稳定子结构的对齐尚未被系统探索。

Method: 提出递归生成器G_{m+1} = [[G_m, 0], [G_m, R_m G_m]]，R_m = I + Ω，它同时满足两个关键条件：(1) 辛整数性，确保生成的算符是合法量子稳定子；(2) 通过等距同构链保留精确的Barnes-Wall解码结构。由此构建显式辛实现，得到编码率R = ½log₂N的多模GKP码。

Result: 所得GKP码具有编码率R = ½log₂N和确定性O(N log²N)有界距离解码器；码距离恒为Δ² = 1（以2π为单位），即保护能力不随码长扩展，实现了对数编码效率与非扩展保护之间的显式权衡。

Conclusion: 该构造为支持非局部模连接性的平台提供了一种确定性、空间高效的多模GKP纠错范式，展示了在距离-速率权衡下实现高编码率多模GKP码的可行路径。

Abstract: We construct an explicit symplectic realization of the Barnes-Wall lattice that yields a family of multimode Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes with encoding rate $R = \frac{1}{2}\log_2 N$ and a deterministic $O(N\log^2 N)$ bounded-distance decoder. The recursive generator $G_{m+1} = \bigl(\begin{smallmatrix} G_m & 0 \\ G_m & R_m G_m \end{smallmatrix}\bigr)$ with $R_m = I + Ω$ simultaneously guarantees symplectic integrality for valid quantum stabilizers and preserves the exact Barnes-Wall decoding structure through a chain of isometric isomorphisms. The code distance is constant at $Δ^2 = 1$ (in units of $2π$), representing an explicit distance--rate tradeoff in which logarithmic encoding efficiency is achieved at the cost of non-scaling protection. This construction provides a deterministic, space-efficient paradigm for GKP error correction in platforms supporting non-local modular connectivity.

</details>


### [6] [New Results Towards the Characterization of Service Rate Region of Reed-Muller Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00810)
*Chenyang Yu,Luopeng Sun,Yiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对高阶Reed-Muller码的服务速率区域(SRR)问题，通过显式刻画恢复集的交集模式，推导出m=r+1情况下的精确SRR区域，并提出了新的更紧约束来缩小现有近似与精确SRR多面体之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务速率区域(SRR)是评估分布式存储系统并发服务能力的关键指标。虽然MDS码和一阶Reed-Muller码的SRR已被刻画，但高阶Reed-Muller码的SRR分析因复杂度更高而极具挑战性，现有工作（Ly, Soljanin, and Lalitha）仅给出了部分结果。

Method: 本文通过显式刻画高阶Reed-Muller码恢复集的交集模式来精细化SRR分析，推导出m=r+1情况下的精确区域，并构造新型更紧约束以弥补现有近似与精确SRR多面体之间的差距。

Result: 获得了m=r+1情形下高阶Reed-Muller码的精确SRR区域，并提出了比现有结果更严格的新约束类型。

Conclusion: 本文推进了高阶Reed-Muller码SRR的精确刻画，为分布式存储系统中并发服务能力分析提供了更精确的理论工具。

Abstract: The Service Rate Region (SRR) serves as a critical metric for evaluating the concurrent service capacity of distributed storage systems. While several works have characterized the SRR for MDS codes and first order Reed-Muller codes, for high-order Reed-Muller codes the problem becomes way more complicated and only partial results were given by Ly, Soljanin, and Lalitha [IEEE ISIT 2025]. In this paper, we refine the SRR analysis by explicitly characterizing the intersection patterns of recovery sets for high-order Reed-Muller codes, deriving the exact region for the case m=r+1 and providing new types of strictly tighter constraints to bridge the gap between existing approximations and the exact SRR polytope.

</details>


### [7] [Partially-Observable Transmission Control for UAV-Enabled Federated Learning in IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00855)
*Masoud Ghazikor,Zhou Ni,Morteza Hashemi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于无人机辅助联邦学习的包级传输框架，通过公平性-共识双层（FCB）优化来联合控制传输阈值和传输功率，以提高数据包投递率（PDR）并保证物联网学习器之间的公平性，从而提升联邦学习的聚合和训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机辅助联邦学习在共享非授权频段运行时，上行链路更新传输会受到干扰且不可靠，导致缓冲溢出、延迟违规和传输错误等问题，影响联邦学习的部分更新接收和整体训练性能。

Method: 1) 开发包级传输框架，通过包投递率（PDR）和伯努利掩码的联邦学习聚合过程表示部分更新接收；2) 提出公平性-共识双层优化（FCB），同时优化传输阈值（最大化平均PDR并达成共识）和传输功率（改善最差PDR并保证公平性）；3) 设计交替FCB优化器，包含基于共识的阈值控制器（CTC）和基于公平性的功率控制器（FPC）。

Result: 在CNN联邦学习任务上的数值结果表明，FCB优化器通过增强包级更新传输改善了联邦学习的聚合和训练性能，始终优于基线传输策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的FCB优化框架能够有效提升无人机辅助联邦学习在不可靠无线环境下的更新传输效率和公平性，从而改善整体联邦学习性能。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV)-enabled federated learning (FL) can provide flexible, on-demand edge intelligence for large-scale IoT deployments, but operating in shared unlicensed bands makes uplink update delivery interference-coupled and unreliable. In this paper, we develop a packet-level transmission framework that captures buffer overflow, delay violations, and transmission errors, and uses the resulting packet delivery ratio (PDR) to represent partial-update reception through a packetized, Bernoulli-masked FL aggregation process. We then formulate a fairness-consensus bilevel (FCB) optimization that jointly controls (i) transmission thresholds to maximize the average PDR while reaching consensus under partial observability and (ii) transmission powers to improve the worst PDR and enforce fairness across IoT learners. To solve this problem, we propose an alternating FCB optimizer composed of a consensus-based threshold controller (CTC), which drives the IoT learners toward a PDR-efficient consensus on transmission thresholds, and a fairness-based power controller (FPC), which updates transmission powers to improve the worst PDR and ensure fairness under the resulting consensus thresholds. Numerical results on CNN-based FL tasks show that the FCB optimizer improves FL aggregation and training performance by enhancing packet-level update delivery, consistently outperforming baseline transmission policies.

</details>


### [8] [Goal-Oriented Logic-based Semantic Communication for Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning with Applications onto Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00878)
*Ahmet Faruk Saz,Duo Xu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于一阶逻辑（FOL）的语义通信框架，用于协作环境（如自动驾驶网络）中的神经符号决策。每辆CAV将局部观测转化为自然语言场景描述和FOL证据，在上行带宽限制下选择最具信息量的观测传至RSU，RSU融合证据、评估规则并进行逻辑演绎，将车辆安全与路权信息下行传输给各CAV。CAV结合本地描述与演绎结果，通过本地LLM智能体选择高层驾驶动作。方法基于随机支持Dirichlet-Categorical归纳逻辑概率模型（对Carnap和Hintikka系统的现代统计算法重释），推导出面向目标的语义信息瓶颈公式，优先传输能最大程度降低任务目标不确定性的证据。在CARLA模拟器MDrive上，使用加州驾驶手册152条交通规则评估，在相同通信预算下，语义证据选择零事故完成所有场景，而均匀证据选择产生碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作环境（如自动驾驶网络）中，通信带宽有限，传统通信方式无法高效传输所有感知数据。需要一种语义通信方法，能够识别并传输对决策任务最关键的信息，而非均匀传输所有数据。同时，需要一个可验证的理论框架来保证决策的可靠性与安全性。

Method: 1) 每辆CAV将传感器数据转为自然语言场景描述和grounded FOL证据；2) 基于随机支持Dirichlet-Categorical模型对归纳逻辑概率进行建模，作为Carnap和Hintikka系统的现代统计重释；3) 由此推导面向目标的语义信息瓶颈公式，量化每条证据对任务目标不确定性的减少程度，指导语义编码器的证据选择；4) RSU融合多车证据，评估交通规则并进行逻辑演绎；5) 车辆结合RSU演绎结果与本地描述，由LLM智能体选择驾驶动作。

Result: 在CARLA的MDrive模拟器中，使用加州驾驶手册中的152条交通规则进行评测。在相同通信预算下，基于语义的证据选择方法能无安全隐患地完成所有场景，而均匀证据选择方法产生碰撞，证明语义通信在有限带宽条件下具有显著优越性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了语义通信（结合神经符号方法与归纳逻辑概率模型）能够在有限通信资源下显著优于均匀传输策略，为自动驾驶等安全关键型协作系统中的高效通信与可靠决策提供了可行的理论框架和工程验证。

Abstract: We consider First-Order Logic (FOL)-based semantic communication for neuro-symbolic decision-making in collaborative environments such as autonomous driving networks. Each connected autonomous vehicle (CAV) converts its partial sensor observations into a natural-language scene description and corresponding grounded FOL evidence. Under an uplink budget, a semantic encoder at each car selects the observations most informative for evaluating traffic rules and transmit to a Road Side Unit (RSU). The RSU fuses all received evidence, evaluates collaborative rules, performs logical deduction for vehicle-specific safety and right-of-way information for constrained downlink transmission. Each CAV combines the received deductions with its local description, enabling a local LLM agent to select a high-level driving action. We develop a principled, verifiable semantic communication method using a random-support Dirichlet--Categorical model of inductive logical probability, providing a modern statistical reinterpretation of Carnap's and Hintikka's systems. From this model, we derive a goal-oriented semantic information-bottleneck formulation that prioritizes evidence transmission by its reduction of uncertainty over task goals. Using 152 traffic rules extracted from the California Driver Handbook, we evaluate the framework on MDrive simulator in CARLA. Under identical communication budgets, semantic evidence selection completes every scenario without safety hazards, whereas uniform evidence selection produces collisions, showcasing semantic communication's superiority.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的信息年龄（AoI）指标仅关注最新更新的年龄，无法全面衡量状态更新系统中信息的新鲜度分布和时间一致性。作者希望提出一个新的度量指标来捕捉更新之间的时间间隔差异，从而更全面地评估系统性能。

Method: 本文在M/G/1/1排队系统框架下分析年龄离散度及其k阶扩展。通过数学建模和理论推导，研究了年龄离散度的统计特性，并建立了其与k阶AoI之间的理论联系。

Result: 在M/G/1/1排队系统中完成了对年龄离散度的完整分析，并成功推导出了k阶年龄离散度与k阶AoI之间的数学关系。

Conclusion: 年龄离散度提供了一种新的视角来度量状态更新系统的时间一致性，补充了传统AoI指标的不足，且与高阶AoI之间存在可量化的理论关联。

Abstract: We introduce and characterize \emph{age dispersion} as a measure of temporal consistency in status update systems. Age dispersion is defined as the difference between the ages of the two most recently received updates, and its higher-order extension, the {$k$-th} order age dispersion, captures the difference between the ages of the most recent update and the $(k+1)$-th most recent one. We analyze age dispersion in an M/G/1/1 queueing system. Furthermore, we establish connections between the {$k$-th} order age dispersion and the {$k$-th} order age of information (AoI), where the latter quantifies the age of the {$k$-th} most recently received update.

</details>


### [10] [Spatial Prefix Caching for Wireless Edge LLM Inference: A Stochastic-Geometry and Queueing Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01126)
*Le Yang,Zhouyong Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究无线边缘网络中LLM推理的prefix caching问题，建立了一个结合随机几何和排队论的框架，来分析空间通信-缓存-计算三者之间的权衡。核心发现是最优节点不一定是最近节点，且TTFT随缓存前缀深度呈非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prefix caching能显著降低LLM推理的TTFT，但在无线边缘网络中，前缀状态分布在空间分离的GPU节点上。近距离节点无线路径短但可能复用率低，远距离节点可能缓存更长的匹配前缀但通信和排队延迟更高。此外，持久前缀与活跃请求的KV状态竞争GPU内存，激进缓存会降低推理并发度并造成排队热点。这是一个关键的通信-缓存-计算三维权衡问题，此前缺乏系统的分析框架。

Method: 论文构建了一个随机几何与排队论相结合的框架：1) 用前缀森林表示提示词工作负载，定义包含多个可复用前缀的祖先封闭缓存配置；2) 将边缘GPU节点建模为带有独立缓存配置标记的泊松点过程，形成可解析的空间层级；3) 推导了配置关联概率、条件服务距离分布、令牌级计算卸载比以及负载感知关联策略下的TTFT覆盖概率；4) 通过不动点公式化空间关联与多服务器GPU队列之间的耦合，外层优化在静态内存和稳定性约束下选择缓存配置分布。

Result: 解析结果与蒙特卡洛仿真结果高度一致。研究结果表明：延迟最优节点不一定是最近的节点；由于GPU内存耦合效应，TTFT随缓存前缀深度呈非单调变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为无线边缘网络中prefix caching的设计提供了理论指导，揭示了空间维度上通信、缓存与计算资源之间的复杂权衡关系，为实际的缓存策略优化提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Prefix caching reuses the key--value (KV) states of shared prompt prefixes and can substantially reduce the time to first token (TTFT) of large language model (LLM) inference. In a wireless edge network, however, prefix states are distributed across geographically separated GPU nodes. A nearby node offers a short radio path but may provide little reuse, whereas a more distant node may cache a longer matching prefix but incur additional communication and queueing delay. Moreover, persistent prefixes and active-request KV states compete for the same GPU memory, so aggressive caching can reduce inference concurrency and create queueing hotspots. This paper develops a stochastic-geometry and queueing framework for this spatial communication--caching--computation tradeoff. We represent the prompt workload by a prefix forest and define an ancestor-closed cache profile that may contain multiple reusable prefixes. Edge GPU nodes form a Poisson point process and are independently marked by cache profile, yielding analytically tractable spatial tiers. We derive the profile-association probability, conditional serving-distance distribution, token-level computation-offloading ratio, and TTFT coverage probability under a load-aware association policy. A fixed-point formulation captures the coupling between spatial association and multi-server GPU queues, while an outer optimization selects the cache-profile distribution subject to static-memory and stability constraints. Analytical and Monte Carlo results agree closely. The results show that the latency-optimal node need not be the nearest node, that TTFT can be non-monotonic in cached-prefix depth because of GPU-memory coupling.

</details>


### [11] [Perfect codes as exact minimizers of quadratic discrepancy in q-ary Hamming spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01134)
*Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了有限字母表上的Stolarsky不变原理：当Hamming空间和基数允许存在完美码时，完美码恰好是二次球面差异（quadratic ball discrepancy）的最小化者。作者进一步给出了仅依赖参数的显式下界基准，并证明在这些参数条件下，完美码的存在性等价于达到显式变分目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Stolarsky不变原理从二进制Hamming空间推广到任意有限字母表，并建立完美码与变异最小化（discrepancy minimization）之间的精确等价关系，为完美码的存在性问题提供新的分析工具。

Method: 作者采用Stolarsky不变原理将二次差异转化为能量最小化问题，使用Fourier-Krawtchouk分析和显式多项式证书（polynomial certificates）来证明下界基准，而非通过数值线性规划求解。对于一纠错参数集（算术可允许）和二纠错参数集（满足球填充和积分Lloyd根条件），证明等式成立等价于完美填充（perfect tiling）。

Result: 证明了在固定Hamming空间和基数允许完美码存在的情况下，完美码精确地是最小化二次球面差异的码。同时给出了参数性的显式下界基准，在覆盖的参数范围内，完美码的存在性与达到显式变分目标等价。

Conclusion: 该结果将完美码的存在性问题转化为一个显式的变分最小化问题，通过Fourier-Krawtchouk和多项式证书给出了解析证明，为完美码的研究提供了新的理论框架和判别标准。

Abstract: Stolarsky's invariance principle converts quadratic discrepancy into an energy-minimization problem. Barg developed its form for binary Hamming space and proved that binary perfect codes minimize total quadratic ball discrepancy among binary codes of the same length and cardinality. We prove an exact finite-alphabet counterpart: whenever a fixed Hamming space and cardinality admit a perfect code, the perfect codes are precisely the discrepancy minimizers. The competitors are arbitrary subsets of the prescribed cardinality, with no linearity or minimum-distance assumption.
  More sharply, we give explicit parameter-only lower benchmarks before existence is known. For every arithmetically admissible one-error parameter set, and for every nontrivial two-error parameter set satisfying the sphere-packing and integral Lloyd-root conditions, equality in the corresponding benchmark is equivalent to perfect tiling. The bounds are proved by explicit Fourier--Krawtchouk and polynomial certificates rather than by solving a numerical linear program. Thus, in every covered parameter regime, perfect-code existence is equivalent to attainment of an explicit variational target.

</details>


### [12] [Schur--Plucker Geometry of the MDS Locus for Principal-Ideal Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01146)
*Yangcheng Li,Pingzhi Yuan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究系数向量码的MDS轨迹（MDS locus），建立了普适MDS多项式D_{n,r}的显式描述——由Schur多项式乘积构成，并利用该描述得到非MDS边界的平展性、度数与有限域估计、长度过滤以及坏特征判据等多种结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解系数向量码（coefficient-vector codes）中MDS码的结构是一个基本问题。作者希望精确刻画MDS轨迹（即使得码字达到Singleton界的多项式集合），并探究其代数几何性质。

Method: 利用伴随构造（companion construction）将系数空间与循环矩阵-向量对的模空间等同，再通过余数轨道映射将其嵌入格拉斯曼流形Gr(r,n)的大胞腔中，作为光滑完全交集。在此基础上，作者证明每个归一化的极大Plücker坐标拉回后（至多差一个符号）等于A_0的幂乘以Schur多项式S_κ(g)=s_κ(Λ_g)，从而得到普适MDS多项式的乘积公式。

Result: 给出了普适MDS多项式D_{n,r}=A_0∏S_κ的显式公式；由此得到：非MDS边界在Z上平展、显式的度数与有限域估计、由稀疏倍数控制的长过滤、根重数层上的坏特征判据、不可约层上的密度一结果，以及代数闭域上首失败层中稠密开非GRS轨迹的存在性。

Conclusion: 该工作为系数向量码的MDS轨迹提供了完整的代数几何刻画，将普适MDS问题归结为Schur多项式的乘积结构，并为后续在编码理论、格拉斯曼簇与表示论交叉方向的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Let \(g\) be a monic polynomial of degree \(r<n\), and let \(C_g(n)\) be the coefficient-vector code formed by multiples \(ug\) with \(°(ug)<n\). We study the coefficient-space MDS locus \(M_{n,r}\). The companion construction identifies coefficient space with the moduli of cyclic matrix-vector pairs, and the remainder-orbit map embeds it as a smooth complete intersection in the standard big cell of \(\operatorname{Gr}(r,n)\).
  We prove that every normalized maximal Plucker coordinate pulls back, up to sign, to a power of the constant coefficient \(A_0\) times a Schur polynomial \(S_κ(g)=s_κ(Λ_g)\), where \(κ\subseteq (n-r)^{r-1}\). Hence the universal MDS polynomial is \[D_{n,r}=A_0\prod_{κ\subseteq (n-r)^{r-1}}S_κ.\] This description yields a flat non-MDS boundary over \(\mathbb{Z}\), explicit degree and finite-field estimates, and a length filtration governed by sparse multiples. It also gives bad-characteristic criteria on root-multiplicity strata and density-one results on the irreducible stratum. Finally, for \(r\ge 3\) and \(N\ge r+3\), every nonempty first-failure layer over an algebraically closed field has a dense open non-GRS locus.

</details>


### [13] [Bridging Differential Privacy and Random Triangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01412)
*Tianxi Ji*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了高斯机制在差分隐私中的两种几何表示方法，将隐私损失从标量随机变量扩展为高维随机三角形结构。第一种表示将归一化边长平方映射到单纯形，推导了精确联合密度、椭圆支撑，并重建了经典隐私损失；第二种表示将归一化三角形的谱形状映射到半球面，推导了相应密度和坐标映射，同样恢复隐私损失，并刻画了维度增加时的赤道漂移和带状集中现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的差分隐私高斯机制分析将隐私损失归结为一个标量随机变量，虽然这对隐私核算足够，但忽略了每次扰动实例所产生的高维几何结构（由敏感性向量和两个噪声向量构成的随机三角形）。作者希望从几何角度更深入地理解隐私损失的机制，并建立差分隐私与随机形状概率分析之间的联系。

Method: 本文提出了两种互补的几何表示：(1) 将归一化的三角形边长平方映射到单纯形（simplex）上，推导精确的联合密度、刻画椭圆支撑，并从单纯形坐标重建经典隐私损失随机变量；(2) 将归一化三角形的谱形状映射到半球面，推导对应密度和坐标映射，同样恢复隐私损失，并分析高维下赤道漂移与带状集中的现象。

Result: 两种几何表示均能精确恢复经典的高斯机制隐私损失随机变量，同时提供了额外的高维几何信息。第二种表示揭示了高维空间中的赤道漂移（equatorial drift）和带状集中（band concentration）现象，表明随机三角形在维度增加时其谱形状趋向于某种集中分布。

Conclusion: 本文提供了两种精确的几何坐标系来补充标量隐私损失，将差分隐私与随机形状的概率分析（probabilistic analysis of random shapes）联系起来，为理解高斯机制的隐私属性提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: The classical analysis of the Gaussian mechanism in differential privacy reduces privacy loss for a pair of neighboring datasets to a scalar random variable. While this scalar characterization is sufficient for privacy accounting, each perturbation instance also induces a high-dimensional random triangle formed by the sensitivity vector and the two corresponding noise vectors. In this work, we develop two complementary geometric representations of these random triangles.
  The first representation maps the normalized squared edge lengths to a simplex. We derive its exact joint density, characterize its elliptical support, and reconstruct the classical privacy loss random variable from the simplex coordinates. The second representation maps the spectral shape of each normalized triangle to a hemisphere. We derive the corresponding density and coordinate mappings, recover the same privacy loss, and characterize an equatorial drift together with band concentration as the dimension increases. These results provide two exact geometric coordinate systems that complement the scalar privacy loss and connect differential privacy with the probabilistic analysis of random shapes.

</details>


### [14] [Capacity of Additive-Noise Sticky Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01433)
*Cécile Bouette,Samuel Pearson,Roni Con,João Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了加性噪声粘性信道（additive-noise sticky channels）的容量问题。这类信道通过固定噪声分布独立地扩展每个输入游程长度，其容量对应非负整数上加性噪声无记忆信道的单位成本容量。作者在Bernoulli加性噪声场景下发现了超越数值方法的精确行为（如黄金分割比处的容量恒定性），并推广到更一般的噪声分布，给出了容量下界和零误差游程长度约束编码的最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘性信道（sticky channels）是同步错误信道（如删除、插入和复制）中最简单的类型，它们从不破坏或创建游程。尽管结构简单，目前文献中对这类信道容量的了解仅限于纯数值方法的结果。作者希望超越数值模拟，对这类信道进行更广泛、更系统的理论探索。

Method: 1. 将加性噪声粘性信道的容量转化为非负整数上加性噪声无记忆信道的单位成本容量问题；2. 对Bernoulli加性噪声（参数p）场景，推导出所有p≤1/2时的精确容量，并通过解析界描述了p→1时的渐近行为；3. 证明了当p∈[1/φ²,1/2]（φ为黄金分割比）时容量恒定，由零误差游程长度约束编码实现；4. 推广到一般加性噪声分布，对给定均值和支撑的噪声分布导出容量下界，并刻画零误差游程长度约束编码非最优的区间。

Result: 1. 对Bernoulli加性噪声，确定了所有p≤1/2时的精确容量，并给出了1/2<p<1时的解析界以刻画p→1的渐近行为；2. 发现容量在p∈[1/φ²,1/2]区间为常数（φ为黄金分割比），由零误差游程长度约束编码实现；3. 将结果扩展到有界游程长度的输入字符串（面向DNA数据存储）；4. 对一般加性噪声分布，推导出给定均值和支撑下的容量下界，并刻画了零误差游程长度约束编码不再最优的区间。

Conclusion: 该研究为粘性信道的容量分析提供了超越纯数值方法的理论框架，揭示了Bernoulli加性噪声下容量行为的奇特结构（如黄金分割比处的常数容量区间），并建立了从DNA测序同聚物长度误判到同步丢失的理论联系，为DNA数据存储等实际应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Sticky channels, which never destroy nor create runs, are some of the simplest types of channels with synchronization errors (such as deletions, insertions, and replications). Despite their simplicity, we know little about the capacity of the sticky channels considered in the literature so far beyond what can be gleaned from purely numerical methods. Towards a broader and systematic exploration of these channels, we initiate the study of additive-noise sticky channels, a basic family of sticky channels that extend each input run by an amount independently sampled from a fixed noise distribution. The capacity of these channels corresponds to the capacity per unit cost of additive-noise memoryless channels over the non-negative integers, and they capture some aspects of the loss of synchronization caused by homopolymer length miscalls in DNA sequencing.
  We first focus on the setting of Bernoulli additive noise with parameter $p$, and uncover curious behavior of the capacity beyond what numerical methods can tell us. For example, the capacity is constant when $p\in [1/\varphi^2,1/2]$ with $\varphi\approx 1.618$ the golden ratio, achieved by zero-error runlength-constrained coding, and behaves differently when $p\approx 0$ vs. when $p\approx 1$. More generally, we determine the capacity exactly for all $p\leq 1/2$, and when $1/2<p<1$ we give analytical bounds that allow us to characterize the asymptotic behavior of the capacity as $p\to 1$. We also extend our analysis to the setting where input strings have bounded runlengths, motivated by DNA-based data storage.
  Then, we study additive-noise sticky channels beyond Bernoulli noise. We derive capacity lower bounds for all additive-noise sticky channels whose noise distributions have a given mean and support. These lower bounds allow us to characterize the regime where zero-error runlength-constrained coding is never optimal.

</details>

*Min Xu,Gennian Ge*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了三层网络中向量线性函数计算问题，提出了一种支持约束行空间框架，用全局行空间来表示线性计算码，并给出了其可实现性的充要条件，同时确定了中间节点的最小均匀通信负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决三层网络中线性函数计算问题，需要一种统一的表示框架来刻画线性计算码的存在性和容量。现有方法缺乏对全局行空间与局部支持约束之间关系的系统理解，特别是在MDS目标函数和循环网络场景下。

Method: 提出支持约束行空间框架：将线性计算码表示为一个全局行空间，该空间必须包含目标行空间，并由满足网络局部支持约束的行生成。证明了该表示与线性计算码存在性等价；对任意给定的全局行空间，给出了其可实现性的充要条件（以源访问集上局部子空间的秩表达），并确定了中间节点的最小均匀通信负载。条件将局部实现问题与全局行空间设计问题分离，得到线性计算容量的变分刻画。将该框架应用于循环网络上的MDS目标。

Result: 识别了目标行空间单独是否足够的情况以及何时需要辅助行。确定了密集情形和割集界为整数时的稀疏情形的容量。对剩余稀疏参数，给出了可达率等于割集界整数部分的通用线性构造。

Conclusion: 支持约束行空间框架提供了一种统一且系统的工具来分析三层网络中的线性函数计算，将问题分解为局部可实现性与全局行空间设计两个层面，并对循环网络上MDS目标给出了完整的容量刻画。

Abstract: We study vector-linear function computation over three-layer networks with a fixed target function and a fixed source-access pattern. We develop a support-constrained row-space framework that represents a linear computing code by a global row space. This space must contain the target row space and be generated by rows satisfying the local support-constraints of the network. We prove that this representation is equivalent to the existence of a linear computing code. For any prescribed global row space, we give a necessary and sufficient condition for its realization and determine the minimum uniform communication load at the middle nodes. The condition is expressed in terms of the ranks of the local subspaces supported on the source-access sets. It separates the exact local realization problem from the outer problem of designing the global row space and yields a variational characterization of the linear computing capacity. We then apply the framework to MDS targets over cyclic networks. We identify when the target row space alone is sufficient and when auxiliary rows are required. We determine the capacity in the dense regime and in the sparse regime whenever the cut-set bound is integral. For the remaining sparse parameters, we give a general linear construction whose achievable rate equals the integer part of the cut-set bound.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC系统需要同时兼顾感知性能和通信性能。传统固定阵列受限于孔径和信道建模的局限性，而可移动子阵列（MSAs）通过物理位置优化可以提升感知精度。此外，现有研究多采用单一近场或远场信道模型，但实际场景中感知目标和通信用户可能处于混合近远场区域，亟需更精确的混合场信道模型。

Method: 1) 建立混合近远场信道模型，其中感知目标和通信用户位于整个MSA孔径的近场但每个子阵列的远场区域。2) 推导等效费舍尔信息矩阵和CRB用于联合距离、仰角和方位角估计。3) 联合优化发射波束成形矩阵和子阵列位置，目标为最小化CRB迹，约束条件包括最小通信SINR、最大发射功率和子阵列移动范围限制。4) 提出交替优化算法，结合迭代秩一惩罚半定松弛（SDR）与投影有限差分块下降和回溯方法。

Result: 数值结果表明：1) 所提混合场模型与精确的球面波模型高度吻合，验证了建模的准确性；2) 可移动子阵列（MSAs）相比固定子阵列能显著降低CRB，有效提升感知精度。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了可移动子阵列在ISAC系统中通过位置优化能够大幅提升感知性能，同时所提出的混合近远场信道模型为更实际的ISAC场景提供了有效的理论基础和优化框架。

Abstract: This letter investigates an integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) system aided by movable subarrays (MSAs) using a hybrid near-far field channel model. The sensing target and communication users are assumed to lie in the near field of the overall MSA aperture but in the far-field region of each subarray. Accordingly, a hybrid near-far field channel model is established, and the equivalent Fisher information matrix and Cramér-Rao bound (CRB) for joint range, elevation, and azimuth estimation are derived. The transmit beamforming matrix and subarray positions are jointly optimized to minimize the trace CRB subject to minimum communication signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR), maximum transmit power and subarray movement constraints. An alternating optimization algorithm is developed combining iterative rank-one-penalized semidefinite relaxation with projected finite-difference block descent and backtracking. Numerical results show that the hybrid-field model closely matches the spherical-wave model, while MSAs substantially reduce the CRB.

</details>


### [17] [CARNet: Channel-Adaptive Receiver Network for Robust NextG Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02172)
*Chao Jiang,Zhuo Xu,Yongli Yan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合专家（MoE）框架的信道自适应神经接收机网络（CARNet），通过多个专家网络和高效路由机制，实现跨多样信道场景的鲁棒信号检测，解决了传统神经接收机泛化能力不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经接收机依赖针对特定信道条件优化的静态网络，在面对多样化信道场景时泛化能力不足，这是阻碍其应用于下一代通信（NextG）的关键挑战。

Method: CARNet采用MoE架构，包含多个基于堆叠ResNet块构建的专家网络，每个专家专门处理特定信道条件下的信号检测。路由机制包含轻量级表示学习模块，将粗信道估计投影为低维潜在嵌入，用以刻画任务相关的信道条件并指导准确的专家选择。

Result: 链路级仿真实验表明，CARNet在多样化的信道条件下均取得了优越的性能。

Conclusion: 基于MoE框架的信道自适应设计能够有效提升神经接收机在不同信道条件下的泛化能力和检测性能，为NextG通信中的智能接收机提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Neural receivers have been recognized as a promising paradigm for the next-generation (NextG) communications. However, due to the reliance on a static network optimized for specific channel conditions, their generalization capability across diverse scenarios remains a significant challenge. To address this issue, this paper proposes a novel channel-adaptive neural receiver network (CARNet) based on the mixture-of-experts (MoE) framework. The proposed architecture employs multiple expert networks together with an efficient routing mechanism to enable signal detection in various scenarios. The experts are constructed via stacked ResNet blocks and specialize in robust signal detection within specific channel conditions, while the routing mechanism incorporates a lightweight representation learning module, which projects the coarse channel estimate into a low-dimensional latent embedding. The learned embedding characterizes task-relevant channel conditions and provides efficient guidance for accurate expert selection. Link-level simulation experiments demonstrate that the proposed CARNet achieves superior performance across diverse channel conditions.

</details>


### [18] [Open-Loop Transmission with Discrete Blocklengths: Characterization of AoI-Optimal Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02231)
*Mojan Wegener,Eduard A. Jorswieck*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究无反馈信道的状态更新系统中，如何构造确定性传输调度以最小化平均信息年龄（AoI）和平均指数AoI。结果表明静态块长通常不是最优的，论文推导了单块长最优性的条件，并通过案例分析验证了实际适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无ACK反馈的状态更新系统中，需要设计确定性调度方案来优化信息新鲜度。现有工作缺乏对多种块长（对应不同调制编码方案）下调度策略最优性的深入分析，特别是静态块长策略是否最优的问题尚未得到充分回答。

Method: 将问题建模为一个无限状态的暂态平均成本马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），采用类似Dinkelbach的方法进行重构，并利用精细的界限技术推导最优性条件。对于逆问题，通过分析平稳随机化策略来界定非平凡确定性调度优于恒定调度的参数区域。方法还扩展到多于两种传输长度的情形。

Result: 证明静态块长在一般情况下是次优的；平均AoI下非平凡周期调度的增益较小，但在平均指数AoI下增益显著。推导了双块长下单块长最优性的充分条件，这些条件覆盖了大部分参数空间，且数值结果表明条件可能也是必要的。通过小LDPC码本的年龄最优传输案例验证了实际可行性。

Conclusion: 在无反馈系统中，动态调度可显著改善指数AoI性能；所提最优性条件具有较高的覆盖率和必要性倾向，为实际系统选择恒定或周期调度提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a general status update system, consisting of a source at which a physical process is monitored and a receiver. For transmission of status updates, a discrete set of blocklength error probability tuples is available. A typical example is a communication system with a fixed set of modulation and coding schemes. We assume that no feedback channel for acknowledgments exists and thus investigate the problem of constructing deterministic schedules for the status updating system. The average Age of Information (AoI) and average exponential AoI are appropriate performance metrics to capture the freshness of information. Interestingly, a static blocklength is shown to be suboptimal in general. While for the average AoI the gains that can be achieved by using a non-trivial periodic schedule are relatively small, they are significant for the average exponential AoI. Motivated by this, we prove conditions for single-blocklength optimality when two blocklengths are available. To achieve this, we use a Dinkelbach-like approach to reformulate the problem as an infinite-state transient average-cost Markov Decision Process and apply careful bounding techniques to derive the optimality conditions. As a converse, we analyze stationary randomized policies to certify the parameter regions where a non-trivial deterministic schedule outperforms the best constant schedule. Extensions to the case with more than two transmission lengths are provided. Numerical results show that the derived conditions cover a large portion of the parameter space and suggest that the optimality conditions for a constant schedule with the shorter blocklength, while sufficient, may also be necessary. As an example, we apply the results to the problem of age-optimal transmission with a small LDPC codebook, which illustrates the practical applicability.

</details>


### [19] [A Spatio-Temporal Model for Information Freshness in Massive Random Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02286)
*Andrea Munari,Alessandro Buratto,Federico Chiariotti,Leonardo Badia,Petar Popovski*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种捕捉大规模IoT设备通过随机接入信道发送信息时空新鲜度的模型，通过条件熵量化接收器的不确定性，并在遗忘接收器和全历史接收器两种模型下优化传输概率以最小化期望不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模连接是5G及下一代无线系统的关键构建块，现有信息新鲜度指标（如AoI）仅表征时间维度，忽略了传感器空间分布带来的相关性，无法全面刻画接收器的实际不确定性。

Method: 提出一个包含空间和时间维度的信息新鲜度模型，使用条件熵作为不确定性度量，考虑多个空间分布的可靠传感器通过时隙ALOHA信道观测同一过程，分别分析简单的遗忘接收器（仅依赖最新更新）和完整模型（考虑全部历史观测），并优化节点传输概率。

Result: 推导了遗忘接收器和全历史接收器的性能表达式，并通过优化传输概率最小化接收器的期望不确定性。

Conclusion: 该模型通过简单的标量参数（成功概率和更新准确性）将时空信息新鲜度纳入统一框架，为大规模IoT网络中的信息新鲜度研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Massive connectivity, a key building block of 5G, is expected to play an important role in the next generation of wireless systems, and its expected requirements are being revolutionized through the modeling of the information dynamics related to the vast numbers of Internet of things (IoT) devices. Motivated by this, the present paper introduces a model that captures the spatio-temporal nature of freshness of information sent via random access channel policies from an extremely large set of IoT devices via simple scalar parameters, i.e., the probability of success and accuracy of received updates. There are many information freshness metrics, starting from the age of information (AoI), all of which are proxies for the actual application performance, characterized over the temporal dimension. Our model adds the spatial dimension to this picture, observing that sensors distributed over the same area may have a strong correlation, and information from multiple close-by sensors may improve the overall accuracy of the receiver. We focus on characterizing the uncertainty of the receiver, expressed through the conditional entropy, considering a network of partially reliable, spatially distributed sensors observing the same process and reporting their measurements over a slotted ALOHA channel. We consider a simple forgetful receiver and a more complete model which accounts for the full history of past observations, deriving their performance, and optimizing the transmission probability of nodes to minimize the expected uncertainty.

</details>


### [20] [Minimum Distances of Binary Goppa Codes and Constructions with Prescribed Alternating Automorphism Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02436)
*Tianni He,Kangquan Li,Longjiang Qu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了二元可分Goppa码的最小距离问题，针对两类特殊生成多项式给出了达到设计距离的判据，并据此确定了许多无限族Goppa码的最小距离。此外，构造了具有A₄或A₅自同构群的二元Goppa码及其相关码，由此自然得到二元准循环Goppa码，并利用上述最小距离判据确定了其中一类A₄不变Goppa码的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goppa码是一类重要的线性码，在密码学中有重要应用。确定Goppa码的最小距离以及构造具有给定自同构群的Goppa码，是编码理论中既有意义又具挑战性的问题，本文致力于解决这两个方面的难题。

Method: 本文首先针对两类生成多项式g(X)=f(X^t)和g(X)=A(X)h(φ(X))，给出二元可分Goppa码达到设计距离的判据，并推导出多个最小距离被完全确定的无限族。随后构造具有A₄或A₅自同构群的二元Goppa码及其相关码，并由此自然地得到二元准循环Goppa码，最后应用上述最小距离判据确定所构造的A₄不变Goppa码中一类的参数。

Result: 给出了两类特殊生成多项式下Goppa码达到设计距离的判定准则，推导出若干最小距离完全确定的无限族；成功构造了具有A₄或A₅自同构群的二元Goppa码及其相关码，并自然得到二元准循环Goppa码；确定了所构造的A₄不变Goppa码中一类的具体参数。

Conclusion: 本文在Goppa码的最小距离确定和自同构群构造两方面取得了进展，既提供了新的理论判据，又给出了具有特定对称结构的Goppa码的构造方法，对编码理论和密码学应用具有参考价值。

Abstract: Goppa codes are a well-known class of linear codes with important applications in cryptography. Determining the minimum distance of Goppa codes and constructing Goppa codes with prescribed automorphism groups are both meaningful and challenging problems in coding theory. In this paper, we first study the minimum distance of binary separable Goppa codes. For the two classes $g(X)=f(X^t)$ and $g(X)=A(X)h(φ(X))$, we give criteria for attaining the designed distance and derive several infinite families whose minimum distances are determined. We then construct binary Goppa codes and their related codes with $A_4$ or $A_5$ automorphism groups. These constructions also naturally yield binary quasi-cyclic Goppa codes and their related codes. Moreover, by applying the minimum-distance criteria developed above, we determine the parameters of one class of the constructed $A_4$-invariant Goppa codes.

</details>


### [21] [PhaseLift for Coded Diffraction Patterns: Optimal Sampling Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02450)
*Gao Huang,Song Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了从编码衍射图案（CDP）中恢复复值信号的相位恢复问题，证明了PhaseLift可行性规划在随机掩模模型下，仅需O(log n)个随机掩模（即m=O(n log n)个标量强度测量）即可实现精确恢复，达到最优采样率，并给出了多项式衰减的失败概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码衍射图案中的结构化相位恢复问题在衍射成像等领域有重要实际应用，但理论上分析仍然缺乏。在标准随机掩模模型下，计算上可处理的恢复方法所能达到的最优采样率一直悬而未决，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者基于近似对偶证书构造的精细化高尔夫方案（refined golfing scheme），该方案结合了自适应掩模分配与维度无关的截断阈值，从而证明PhaseLift可行性规划能以多项式衰减失败概率实现精确恢复。

Result: PhaseLift仅需O(log n)个随机掩模即可精确恢复未知信号（模全局相位），同时由于在擦除掩模系综下Ω(log n)个掩模是必要的，该结果达到了最优掩模复杂度，等价于最优总采样率m=O(n log n)。

Conclusion: 本文首次为PhaseLift在随机掩模模型下建立了最优采样率，证明其达到了信息论意义上的最优掩模复杂度，为编码衍射图案相位恢复的理论分析提供了关键进展。

Abstract: Recovering a complex-valued signal from coded diffraction patterns, namely the Fourier intensities obtained after modulating the signal with a collection of masks, is a fundamental structured phase retrieval problem arising in diffraction imaging and related applications. Despite its practical importance, the theoretical analysis of this structured framework remains scarce. In the standard random mask model, the optimal sampling rate achievable by computationally tractable recovery methods has remained open.
  In this paper, we establish the optimal sampling rate for the PhaseLift feasibility program. More precisely, PhaseLift achieves exact recovery of an unknown signal $\pmb{x}_0\in\mathbb{C}^n$, up to a global phase, from $\mathcal{O}(\log n)$ random masks, with polynomially decaying failure probability. Since $Ω(\log n)$ masks are necessary to identify certain signals under the erasure mask ensemble, our result thereby achieves the optimal mask complexity. Equivalently, PhaseLift attains the optimal total sampling rate of $m=\mathcal{O}( n\log n)$ scalar intensity measurements. The proof is based on an approximate dual certificate construction via a refined golfing scheme that combines adaptive mask allocation with a dimension-independent truncation threshold.

</details>

### [22] [EmergencyBias: Bias in Text-to-Image Models under Emergency Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00598)
*Haibo Tang,Linqi Zhang,Hongxin Huan,Chenwei Lin,Xian Xu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文定义了文本到图像（T2I）生成模型中的"紧急偏差"（EmergencyBias），涵盖人口统计偏差和行为偏差。作者构建了一个评估框架，在七个主流T2I模型、六种典型紧急场景和三个维度的检验下，揭示了模型在紧急场景中的系统性偏差，并提出了一种轻量级的提示嵌入校准方法（ActionAlign）来缓解行为偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2I偏差研究主要关注静态和显式形式的偏差（如性别、种族、地理文化表征差异），但忽略了一种重要偏差——行为偏差，即不同群体在行动、反应和社会角色分配上的差异。紧急场景为研究此类偏差提供了理想条件，因为它要求模型不仅描绘"谁在场"，还描绘"谁处于风险中""谁介入""责任如何分配"。

Method: 1) 定义EmergencyBias概念框架，包括人口统计偏差和行为偏差两个维度；2) 构建系统的评估框架，覆盖七个领先T2I模型、六种代表性紧急场景和三个人口统计维度；3) 设计两类实验：空白提示（不加人口统计说明）和受控提示（指定人口统计特征）；4) 提出ActionAlign方法，一种轻量级的提示嵌入校准方法，用于减少行为偏差。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）在无人口统计说明的空白提示下，T2I模型在紧急场景中表现出明显的人口统计偏差，体现在性别、年龄和肤色分布上；（2）在受控提示下，行为偏差与人口统计变异系统相关，性别维度上尤为突出，且不同模型间差异显著；（3）ActionAlign在减少行为差异方面优于代表性的基于提示的基线方法，同时基本保持图像质量。

Conclusion: 该研究将紧急场景确立为T2I模型偏差评估的重要场景，揭示了现有模型在紧急情境下同时存在人口统计偏差和行为偏差的问题，并为社会后果重大的场景中实现更公平的视觉生成提供了实用的改进方向。

Abstract: Bias in Text-to-Image (T2I) generation has become an important problem in multimedia content creation and communication. However, existing studies have primarily focused on relatively static and explicit forms of bias, such as disparities in the representation of gender, race, and geo-cultural attributes. Less attention has been paid to behavioral bias in how different groups are portrayed acting, reacting, and occupying social roles. Emergency scenarios provide a revealing setting for studying such bias because they require models to depict not only who is present, but also who is at risk, who intervenes, and how responsibility is allocated. In this paper, we define EmergencyBias, a form of bias in T2I generation under emergency scenarios that includes both demographic bias and behavioral bias. We construct an evaluation framework to systematically study EmergencyBias across seven leading T2I models, six representative emergency scenarios, and three demographic dimensions. Our experimental results reveal three main findings. First, under blank prompts without demographic specification, T2I models exhibit clear demographic bias in emergency scenarios, reflected in the distributions of portrayed individuals across gender, age, and skin tone. Second, under controlled prompts, behavioral bias in emergency responses remains systematically associated with demographic variation, with particularly pronounced disparities along gender and substantial differences across models. Third, we introduce ActionAlign, a lightweight prompt-embedding calibration method that outperforms a representative prompt-based baseline in reducing behavioral disparities while largely preserving image quality. Overall, our work identifies emergency scenarios as an important setting for bias evaluation in T2I models and offers a practical direction toward fairer visual generation in socially consequential contexts.

</details>


### [23] [FATE: Frame-Level Audio-Visual Temporal Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01310)
*Kaisi Guan,Bingzi Zhang,Xihua Wang,Ying Ba,Xin Cheng,Yijing Chen,Ruihua Song*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出FATE（Frame-level Audio-visual Temporal Embedding），一种帧级音视频时间嵌入方法，同时捕捉音频-视觉内容的语义信息与时间对齐信息。FATE保留帧级序列并在物理时间线上对齐，通过联合优化跨视频语义对比学习和视频内时间对比学习，在时间检索、语义检索、事件定位和生成评估等多个任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的音视频模型要么只匹配语义但丢失时间信息（embedding模型），要么只捕捉时间偏移但缺乏语义理解（synchronization模型），无法同时兼顾二者。作者希望构建一个既能理解"发生了什么声音"又能知道"何时发生"的统一表征。

Method: FATE采用帧级表示而非池化的单一嵌入：1）保留每个帧的特征序列而非压缩成单个向量；2）在物理时间线上对齐帧对并计算严格对齐帧间的相似度；3）通过联合目标函数训练，包含跨视频语义对比学习和视频内时间对比学习，从而同时编码语义信息与同步信息。

Result: 在三个任务上，FATE在时间检索和语义检索上大幅超越最强基线；在零样本事件定位任务上与全监督方法性能相当；作为生成评估指标时与人类判断的相关性最佳。

Conclusion: 帧级保留与严格时间对齐是同时捕捉音视频语义和时间信息的有效途径，FATE证明了将同步信息编码到可复用的嵌入空间中是可行的，为多模态时间对齐研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: When a dog opens its mouth and barks, humans naturally recognize what the sound is and when it occurs. Building audio-visual models with this same ability requires representations that capture both semantic and temporal alignment. Current approaches fall short on one side or the other: embedding models match semantic but lose temporal information; synchronization models capture temporal offsets but lack semantic understanding. To bridge this gap, we propose FATE, Frame-level Audio-visual Temporal Embedding. Unlike prior embedding models that pool each modality into a single embedding and discard temporal information, FATE retains frame-level sequences, aligns them on the physical timeline, and computes similarity over strictly aligned frame pairs. Unlike synchronization models that output only an offset prediction, FATE encodes synchronization in a reusable embedding space, trained with a joint objective combining cross-video semantic and within-video temporal contrastive learning to capture both what sounds and when it occurs. Across three tasks, FATE surpasses the strongest baseline on temporal and semantic retrieval by a large margin, matches fully supervised methods on event localization in a zero-shot setting, and achieves the best correlation with human judgments as a generation evaluation metric. The source code can be found at \texttt{https://github.com/guankaisi/FATE}.

</details>


### [24] [Hear, Invoke, and Understand: A Skill-Calling Multimodal Agent for Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01881)
*Yuwen Wang,Tian-Hao Zhang,Minghao Cai,Yilin Ren,Ziyang Jiang,Xin Wang,Zhichao Wang,Pan Zhou,Kun Zhan,Xinyuan Qian*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeechAgent-R，一个支持工具交互式音频推理的音频智能体，并构建了包含65,492条交互轨迹和507.6小时音频的HIU-Corpus训练语料以及包含1,395个样本的HIU-Bench评估基准。该智能体通过轨迹监督微调学习结构化交互行为，再通过多轮强化学习优化决策，在分布内任务上达到84.17分，在分布外任务上达到70.94分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂的声学问题可能需要模型执行声学操作、与外部工具交互，并基于工具返回的文本或处理后的音频观察进行推理，而不是直接从固定音频输入中给出答案。现有音频模型缺乏这种工具交互式音频推理能力。

Method: SpeechAgent-R采用两阶段训练策略：首先通过基于轨迹的监督微调（SFT）学习结构化的工具交互行为，然后通过多轮强化学习（RL）进一步提升决策能力。同时构建HIU-Corpus训练语料和HIU-Bench评估基准，覆盖24个任务、8种技能和9种工具。

Result: SpeechAgent-R在分布内（ID）任务上达到84.17分，在分布外（OOD）任务上达到70.94分，相比相同智能体框架下的基础模型分别提升了15.40分和14.23分。

Conclusion: 学习技能和工具的协调能力能够显著提升音频智能体在多样化任务场景和自适应工具交互中的表现，证明了工具交互式训练对于音频推理智能体的重要性。

Abstract: Complex acoustic problems may require models to perform acoustic operations, interact with external tools and reason over the resulting textual or processed-audio observations rather than answer directly from a fixed audio input. We study such problems as tool-interactive audio reasoning and develop SpeechAgent-R, an audio agent that coordinates its intrinsic multimodal understanding with external skills and tools. To support this capability, we construct HIU-Corpus, comprising 65,492 interaction trajectories and 507.6 hours of audio across 24 tasks, 8 skills and 9 tools. SpeechAgent-R first learns structured interaction behaviors through trajectory-based supervised fine-tuning and then improves its decisions through multi-turn reinforcement learning. We further introduce HIU-Bench to jointly evaluate task performance, interaction quality and generalization to diverse task settings. It contains 1,395 samples across 56 tasks, including in-distribution (ID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) splits with substantial shifts in tool usage and workflow composition. SpeechAgent-R achieves 84.17 on ID tasks and 70.94 on OOD tasks, improving over the base model under the same agent harness by 15.40 and 14.23 points. These results demonstrate that learning skill and tool coordination improves audio agents' ability to handle diverse task settings and adaptive tool interactions.

</details>


### [25] [AcoustiTrace: When Plausible Sound Violates Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02035)
*Shiyang Li,Yuewen Cao,Yihao Liu,Yuandong Pu,Baochang Zhang,Xiaofei Li,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: AcoustiTrace是一个用于诊断音视频生成模型声学物理真实性的基准测试工具，通过八个声学维度（声源生成、传播环境、声学接收）对T2AV和I2AV模型进行评估，并发现即使最先进的生成器在基本声学物理过程上仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频生成模型虽然能产生语义合理且看似同步的声音，但常违反可见事件和环境所暗示的声学物理过程。现有基准测试无法将这些违规归因到特定的声学过程并量化其严重程度，这阻碍了对模型缺陷的精确诊断和改进。

Method: AcoustiTrace构建了一个基于声学机制的基准框架：1）将评估组织为声学过程的三个环节（声源生成、传播环境、声学接收）共八个维度，每个维度都基于可测量的声学量；2）构建包含真实音视频记录和声学标注的RGB-D观测的大规模数据集；3）开发针对性的提示套件和经过验证的评估器，用于系统诊断模型的声学物理真实性问题。

Result: 实验表明，即使领先的生成器在产生看似合理的声事件时，仍难以处理基本的声学物理过程。此外，AcoustiTrace提供的针对特定声学关系的诊断结果可用于指导模型改进，使其生成更符合物理真实的音频。

Conclusion: AcoustiTrace为音视频生成提供了系统化的声学物理真实性评估框架，其细粒度诊断能力能够指导模型优化，并将声学原理引入训练目标、奖励建模和候选选择等环节，为音视频生成模型的物理真实性改进开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Recent audio-video generators can produce semantically plausible and apparently synchronized sound, yet may still violate the acoustic processes implied by visible events and environments. Existing benchmarks provide limited support for attributing such violations to particular acoustic processes and quantifying their severity. We introduce AcoustiTrace, a diagnostic benchmark that formalizes acoustic physical realism in audio-video generation. AcoustiTrace organizes text-to-audio-video (T2AV) and image-to-audio-video (I2AV) evaluation around the acoustic process, covering sound generation, propagation environment, and acoustic reception through eight dimensions grounded in measurable acoustic quantities. Based on these evaluation dimensions, we construct a large-scale dataset organized around acoustic mechanisms, comprising real-world audio-video recordings and acoustically annotated RGB-D observations, and use it to develop targeted prompt suites and validated evaluators. Experiments reveal that even leading generators still struggle with fundamental acoustic processes despite producing plausible sound events. Finally, we show that the diagnostics AcoustiTrace provides for specific acoustic relations can guide model refinement toward more physically faithful audio and open new directions for incorporating acoustic principles into training objectives, reward modeling, and candidate selection.

</details>


### [26] [Estimating SSIM from MSE for DCT-Based Compressed Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02549)
*Luc Trudeau,Maria G. Martini*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从全局PSNR/MSE近似SSIM的方法，通过仅使用参考图像的局部统计信息（方差或标准差加权）重分配全局MSE到局部，从而在不访问失真图像局部信息的情况下高效估算SSIM，在Kodak和Xiph Subset1数据集上验证了其准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像和视频处理、压缩及流媒体系统需要高效且感知上有意义的质量评估。传统SSIM计算需要完整的参考图像和失真图像信息，计算成本较高。已有工作假设可以访问局部MSE，但实际中往往只能获得全局MSE（如PSNR）。因此，如何仅从全局MSE和参考图像的局部统计信息来近似SSIM是一个值得研究的问题。

Method: 提出两种方法来用全局MSE近似局部MSE：(1) 基于方差的加权重分配；(2) 基于标准差（即局部对比度）的加权重分配。通过将全局MSE按照参考图像的局部统计信息重新分配到各个局部块，再结合SSIM公式计算近似的SSIM值。该框架可自然扩展到视频场景，参考图像派生的统计信息可在同一内容多次编码中重复使用。

Result: 在Kodak和Xiph Subset1数据集上，跨越多个JPEG质量级别进行实验，两种方法均提供了准确且稳健的SSIM近似，显著优于全局MSE基线。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，仅利用参考图像的局部方差或标准差统计信息，就可以从全局PSNR/MSE准确近似SSIM，为DCT-based压缩图像提供了一种高效且感知相关的质量评估途径，并具备扩展到视频应用的潜力。

Abstract: Efficient and perceptually meaningful quality assessment is a fundamental requirement for image and video processing, compression, and streaming systems. This article shows that, in the context of Discrete Cosine Transform ( DCT)-based compressed images, Structural Similarity Index ( SSIM ) can be approximated from global Peak Signal to Noise Ratio (PSNR) or Mean Square Error ( MSE) using local statistics derived only from the reference image. While prior work assumes access to local MSE, we propose two approaches to approximate local MSE by redistributing the global MSE using variance or standard-deviation-based weighting. Experiments on the Kodak and Xiph Subset1 datasets across a range of JPEG quality levels demonstrate that both approaches provide accurate and robust SSIM approximations, substantially outperforming the global MSE baseline. The proposed framework is designed to extend naturally to video, where reference-derived statistics can be amortized across multiple encodes of the same content.

</details>

Abstract: The Brazilian DTV system standards have been recently revised in order to address new use cases related to deeper integration be- tween broadcast services and broadband services. Such an evolution could not disrupt the current DTV services since Brazil and many ISDB-T countries are still under the switch-off process from ana- log to digital. The middleware layer is the best candidate for such an incremental (yet powerful) evolution. In the case of Ginga, the Brazilian DTV middleware, as an open platform based on receiver profiles, the results were impressive. In this paper, we focus on the advances and new use cases addressed by the D-profile Ginga DTV receiver specification carried out in the Brazilian DTV Forum under a collaborative effort led by the Academia

</details>

### [28] [A Subsampling Theorem for Constraint Satisfaction Problems with Large Arity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00203)
*Martino Bernasconi,Matteo Castiglioni,Andrea Celli,Gabriele Farina,Giulio Malavolta*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次提出一个子采样定理（subsampling theorem）用于约束满足问题（CSP），其所需样本量在arity k和误差ε上为多项式，在字母表大小q上为多对数级别，显著优于以往的结果。该定理在交互式证明和性质测试中有重要应用，包括证明AM(poly)=AM以及构建首个样本量在k和ε上为多项式、在q上为多对数的单侧测试器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSP的子采样定理保证在将CSP限制在小规模随机变量子集后，其值近似保持。此前Barak等人（SODA '11）的结果仅在常数arity情形下实现多项式依赖ε和多对数依赖q。作者希望获得一个在arity k和误差ε上多项式、在字母表q上多对数的新定理，以突破常数arity的限制，并填补交互式证明中AM(poly)=AM证明的关键缺失环节。

Method: 作者提出了一种新的子采样定理，其样本复杂度在arity k和误差ε上呈多项式增长，而在字母表大小q上仅呈多对数增长。该定理改进了Barak等人（SODA '11）的工作，将多项式依赖推广到非恒定arity的一般情形，并证明该定理在交互式证明（AM协议）和性质测试（单侧测试器）中都具有直接应用。

Result: 1. 获得了首个满足所要求样本复杂度的CSP子采样定理，样本量在k和ε上是多项式的，在q上是多对数的。2. 在交互式证明领域，为该定理提供了Aaronson、Impagliazzo和Moshkovitz（CCC '14）证明AM(poly)=AM所需的关键缺失成分。3. 在性质测试领域，首次获得了样本量在arity k和误差ε^{-1}上多项式、在字母表大小q上多对数的单侧可满足性测试器。

Conclusion: 该子采样定理在样本复杂度上实现了质的提升，将多项式依赖从常数arity推广到一般arity，并在交互式证明和性质测试两个重要领域产生了直接影响，证明了该理论工具的强大应用价值。

Abstract: Subsampling theorems for constraint satisfaction problems (CSPs) guarantee that the value of the CSP is approximately preserved after restricting it to small random subsets of variables. We provide the first subsampling theorem for CSPs, which requires a sample size that is polynomial in the arity $k$ and error $\varepsilon$, and polylogarithmic in the alphabet size $q$. This improves upon the subsampling theorem of Barak, Hardt, Holenstein, and Steurer (SODA '11), which achieves a polynomial dependency on $\varepsilon$ and polylogarithmic in $q$ only in the constant-arity regime. Our subsampling theorem has applications in interactive proofs and property testing. In interactive proofs, it provides a key missing ingredient for the proof of Aaronson, Impagliazzo, and Moshkovitz (CCC '14) that $\textsf{AM}(\textsf{poly})=\textsf{AM}$ (where $\textsf{AM}(k)$ is the class of languages decidable by Arthur-Merlin protocols with $k$ non-communicating Merlins with independent questions). In property testing, it yields the first one-sided tester for satisfiability with sample size polynomial in the arity $k$ and the error $\varepsilon^{-1}$, and polylogarithmic in the alphabet size $q$.

</details>


### [29] [Robust Polynomial Freiman-Ruzsa from Corrupted Set Observations via a Sharp Persistent-Subset BSG Compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00451)
*Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究在F_2^n上对抗性损坏集合观测下的结构推断问题。核心贡献是一个无承诺、锐利的持久子集BSG编译器：给定采样和查询访问S，输出一个固定子集Y⊆S，满足|Y|≥c√α|S|且|Y+Y|≤Cα^{-4}|Y|。与算法PFR和确定性提升结合，得到η=O(K^{-1/2})下的随机化FPT算法，输出V满足|V|≤|A|且N_V(A)≤K^{O(1)}。迭代残差算法对任意η<1输出覆盖所有兼容隐藏集的公共列表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典BSG引理和算法PFR在干净输入下有效，但损坏可能破坏集合B的加倍性质，使得二者无法直接组合于对抗性损坏模型。需要开发一种能够在损坏存在下仍保持锐利质量保证、无承诺有效性的编译器，以统一处理结构推断问题。

Method: 提出一个无承诺的锐利持久子集BSG编译器：通过采样和查询访问S，返回FAIL或定义固定子集Y的描述符。编译器利用能量条件保证成功，不满足时仍以规定概率输出有效结果。结合认证的大小无关算法PFR和确定性提升技术，以及迭代残差算法和精确两子空间构造，形成完整的推断框架。

Result: 编译器输出满足|Y|≥c√α|S|和|Y+Y|≤Cα^{-4}|Y|的子集，提供自适应查询下的持久成员资格和条件精确乘积采样。其在η=O(K^{-1/2})下产生随机化FPT算法，输出满足|V|≤|A|且N_V(A)≤K^{O(1)}的子空间V。对任意η<1，迭代残差算法输出多项式覆盖预算的公共列表，采样复杂度为多项式，而查询和运行时间为XP。精确两子空间构造的公共覆盖代价为Θ((1-η)^{-1/2})。

Conclusion: 锐利保留质量、无承诺有效性、持久性和精确有限采样构成一个可组合的统一接口，为对抗性损坏集合下的结构推断提供了完整的理论框架，并在复杂度上给出了FPT和XP界限以及最优覆盖代价的刻画。

Abstract: We study structural inference from an exact, adversarially corrupted set observation over $\mathbb F_2^n$. A hidden nonempty set $A$ satisfies $|A+A|\leq K|A|$, while the algorithm receives deterministic membership and exact uniform-sampling access only to $B$, where $|A\triangle B|\leqη|A|$. Since corruption can destroy the doubling of $B$, sharp existential BSG and clean-input Algorithmic PFR do not directly compose in this model.
  Our main result is a promise-free sharp persistent-subset BSG compiler. Given sample-and-query access to $S$ and $α$, it returns either $\mathsf{FAIL}$ or a descriptor defining one fixed subset $Y\subseteq S$ with $|Y|\geq c\sqrtα\,|S|$ and $|Y+Y|\leq Cα^{-4}|Y|$. Without an energy promise, every nonfailure output is valid except with the prescribed soundness probability; high energy guarantees success with high probability. The descriptor gives persistent membership under adaptive queries, and a finite-horizon bridge gives conditionally exact product samples. Thus sharp retained mass, promise-free validity, persistence, and exact finite sampling form one composable interface.
  Combined with certified size-oblivious Algorithmic PFR and deterministic lifting, the compiler yields a randomized FPT-form algorithm for $η=O(K^{-1/2})$, outputting $V$ with $|V|\leq|A|$ and $\mathcal N_V(A)\leq K^{O(1)}$. For every supplied $η<1$, an iterated residual algorithm outputs a common list serving every compatible hidden set with polynomial covering budget; samples are polynomial, while membership-query and running-time complexity are XP. Finally, every nonempty compatibility class admits one common subspace nonconstructively, whereas an exact two-subspace construction forces common covering cost $Θ((1-η)^{-1/2})$.

</details>


### [30] [An Analysis of Brent's Insertion Method for Hash Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00762)
*William Kuszmaul*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文对Brent在1968年提出的哈希表构建方法进行了首次正式分析。Brent方法即使哈希表填满到100%，查询表中随机键的期望时间仍为O(1)，但该方法的保证从未被正式证明，主要困难在于处理"损坏的随机性"问题。作者给出了一个简单且正式的分析，可在研究生随机算法课程中讲授。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brent哈希表方法自1968年提出以来，虽然在经验上表现出色（即使表满100%，查询期望时间仍为O(1)），但从未被正式分析过。根本困难在于"损坏的随机性"问题：算法在探测过程中可能对先前已探测过的键重复使用哈希函数，此时不能再将这些哈希函数视为随机的，因为其随机位已经影响了表的状态。这使得形式化推理变得异常微妙。

Method: 作者给出了一个简单且形式化的分析方法来处理Brent哈希表。该方法专门解决了"损坏的随机性"这一微妙问题，提供了一种在概率分析中处理此类困难的典范方法。分析简洁到可以在研究生随机算法课程中作为教学内容。

Result: 文章给出了Brent哈希表的简单形式化分析，证明了即使在表填满100%的情况下，查询随机键的期望时间为O(1)。这是该算法首次获得正式的理论保证。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于：(1)首次为Brent哈希表提供了正式的理论分析；(2)展示了如何处理概率分析中"损坏的随机性"这一微妙问题，为类似算法的分析提供了可借鉴的教学范例。

Abstract: In 1968, Richard P.~Brent introduced a new way of building a hash table that, at least empirically, achieves a remarkable property: Even if the hash table is filled to 100\% full, the expected time to query a \emph{random key out of those present} is $O(1)$.
  Despite the simplicity of Brent's method, the guarantees of the method have never been formally analyzed. This is due to the subtle issue of handling \emph{spoiled randomness}. The algorithm will sometimes try to use hash functions $h_j$ on keys $y$ that it has already probed in the past. When the algorithm does this, we cannot treat the hash function as random, because its random bits have already affected the state of the table. This issue makes Brent's method surprisingly subtle to reason about formally.
  In this note, we give a simple and formal analysis of Brent's hash table. The analysis can be taught in a graduate randomized algorithms course, and provides a nice example of how to deal with subtle issues in a probabilistic analysis (namely, the issue of spoiled randomness).

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 核心算法是对称矩阵 A 进行均匀列采样（约 O(log n/ε⁴) 列），利用采样列张成近似特征向量空间。对于最大幅值特征向量，只需采样 O(log n/ε²) 列。当可按平方范数比例采样行列时，误差界改进为 ε‖A‖_F。技术基础建立在 Bhattacharjee et al. '22 和 Swartworth & Woodruff '25 的随机采样特征值工作之上，并首次扩展至特征向量估计。此外还提出基于截断 Nyström 方法的替代方案，在一般对称矩阵上达到近最优采样复杂度。

Result: 算法输出特征向量由少量 A 的列张成，每个条目可在 poly(log n, 1/ε) 时间内计算。在量子启发算法框架中首次给出了特征向量近似的亚线性时间经典算法（加性误差 ε‖A‖_F）。对于最大特征向量近似，证明采样复杂度在对数项意义下是紧的。截断 Nyström 方法在一般对称矩阵上达到近最优采样复杂度，对半正定矩阵有改进界。

Conclusion: 本文成功将亚线性时间随机采样技术从特征值估计扩展到特征向量估计，在量子启发算法框架中实现了首个亚线性时间经典特征向量近似算法，并建立了相应的最优性下界，为该方向未来的研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study sublinear time sampling methods for approximating the outlying eigenvectors of large matrices. Our main result is an algorithm that uniformly samples just $\tilde{O}(\log n/ε^4)$ columns of a symmetric matrix $A \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times n}$ with entries bounded in magnitude by $1$, and, for any eigenvalue $λ$ of $A$ with $|λ| \ge εn$, outputs an approximate eigenvector $v$ satisfying $\|Av - λv\|_2 \le εn$. For approximating just the eigenvector of the largest magnitude eigenvalue, our algorithm samples only $\tilde{O}(\log n/ε^2)$ columns. Given the ability to sample rows and columns of $A$ proportional to their squared norms, we give a similar result with an improved error bound of $ε\|A\|_F$. For top eigenvector approximation, we show our bound is tight up to logarithmic terms.
  A key feature of our algorithms is that the output eigenvectors are spanned by a small number of $A$'s columns, and individual entries can be computed rapidly, in poly(log n, 1/epsilon) time per entry. This makes them applicable in the quantum-inspired algorithms framework of [Tang, STOC 2019], where we give the first sublinear time classical algorithms for eigenvector approximation with additive error $ε\|A\|_F$.
  Finally, we present an alternative approach, based on a truncated Nystrom method, that, while not allowing poly(log n, 1/epsilon) time entrywise computation of the approximate eigenvectors, achieves near optimal sample complexity for general symmetric matrices, and improved bounds for positive semidefinite matrices.
  Technically, our bounds build on recent work on approximating the outlying eigenvalues of symmetric matrices via random sampling in [Bhattacharjee et al. '22] and [Swartworth and Woodruff '25]. We demonstrate for the first time that these approaches extend to the problem of eigenvector estimation.

</details>


### [32] [A Consistency-Robustness Framework for Robust Optimization: Integrating Predictions into Robust Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00848)
*Yasser Alghouass,Eric Balkanski,Nicole Megow,Vineet Goyal*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预测的鲁棒优化框架，将预测场景作为独立基准与经典min-max鲁棒最优平衡，并在不确定处理时间的调度问题中系统研究了不同不确定性模型与机器环境下的consistency-robustness权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典鲁棒优化针对最坏情况保护，但当地预测（如历史数据或学习预测）指示更可能场景时可能过于保守。现有学习增强算法中预测仅估计实际输入，而本文提出预测应创建一个独立的基准（预测最优），需与min-max鲁棒最优进行权衡，这是一个新的研究角度。

Method: 针对makespan调度问题，在不同不确定性模型（区间不确定性、预算不确定性、任意不确定性）和机器环境（受限分配、相关机器、无关机器、相同机器）下，利用对偶归约到区间型上包络的框架，以及支撑函数块构造等技术，给出consistency-robustness权衡的理论结果。

Result: 1) 区间不确定性：受限分配和相关机器得到平滑的(1+1/λ, 1+λ)权衡；无关机器证明不存在常数权衡。2) 预算不确定性：受限分配得到(1+1/λ, 2+λ)权衡。3) 任意不确定性：相同机器通过支撑函数块构造得到常数权衡；受限分配证明不可行。另外证明即使只有一个作业偏离，相关机器也不存在常数权衡。

Conclusion: 一致性（consistency）与鲁棒性（robustness）能否结合的可行性关键取决于不确定性模型与机器环境的相互作用，本文给出了系统的结构分类。

Abstract: Robust optimization protects against uncertainty by optimizing for the worst case over a prescribed uncertainty set. This protection can be overly conservative when forecasts, historical data, or learned predictions indicate a more likely scenario. We introduce a framework for robust optimization with predictions. The input consists of an uncertainty set together with a distinguished predicted scenario, and the goal is to compute a single solution that is both consistent, meaning near-optimal for the predicted scenario, and robust, meaning competitive with the classical min-max robust optimum. Unlike in standard learning-augmented algorithms, the prediction does not merely estimate the realized input; it creates a separate benchmark, the predicted optimum, which must be balanced against the min-max robust optimum. We study this framework for makespan scheduling with uncertain processing times and give a structural classification across standard uncertainty models and machine environments. For interval uncertainty, we obtain a smooth $(1+1/λ,1+λ)$ consistency-robustness tradeoff for restricted-assignment and related machines. Furthermore, we prove that unrelated machines admit no constant tradeoff. For budgeted uncertainty, we obtain a $(1+1/λ,2+λ)$ tradeoff for restricted assignment. Our analysis is based on a duality-based reduction to an interval-like upper envelope. We complement this with a lower bound showing that related machines admit no constant tradeoff even when only one job may deviate. For arbitrary uncertainty sets, we obtain constant tradeoffs for identical machines via a support-function block construction, and prove impossibility for restricted assignment. Our results show that the possibility of combining consistency and robustness in robust scheduling depends critically on the interaction between the uncertainty model and the machine environment.

</details>


### [33] [Testing Full Quartet Consistency: Adaptive Reconstruction, Random Verification, and Constant-Query Testability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00987)
*Chuang-Chieh Lin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究系统发育四分体拓扑（quartet topologies）的稠密性质测试问题，判断一个系统是否由系统发育树诱导。主要结果是显式的多项式时间自适应单侧误差测试器，通过锚定四分体查询重构候选树，并用随机查询验证，查询复杂度为O(n log n + ε⁻¹log(1/δ))。还给出了非自适应变体和基于超图编码的测试器，并证明了匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育树是进化生物学中重建物种或基因演化关系的基本模型，四分体拓扑（四分体四元组）是推断系统发育树的核心约束信息。判断一个给定的完整四分体系统是否与某棵树兼容（即树诱导）是重要的验证问题。以往显式测试器复杂度为O(n³/ε)，本文寻求更高效的测试方法，并探究该问题的查询复杂度下界。

Method: 1. 提出自适应单侧误差测试器：先通过锚定四分体查询（anchored quartet queries）重构一棵候选树，再用均匀随机四分体查询验证候选树是否与输入的绝大部分条目一致。2. 给出非自适应缓存锚点变体，使用组合查询。3. 将完整四分体系统在重标号等变意义下编码为有向三色4元结构，利用遗传有向超图测试获得n无关的测试器。4. 建立下界：通过随机化测试器的信息论分析，证明普通性质测试和单侧误差测试的查询下界。

Result: 自适应测试器查询复杂度为O(n log n + ε⁻¹log(1/δ))，非自适应变体为C(n-1,3)+O(ε⁻¹log(1/δ))次查询，均优于此前显式的O(n³/ε)上界。对于固定ε和δ，两者均使用o(n⁴)次查询。下界方面，普通性质测试自适应随机测试器至少需F(ln((1-δ)/δ)/ln(1/(1-ε))))次查询，单侧误差测试器至少需ln(1/δ)/ln(1/(1-ε))次查询，与随机验证项匹配。对更强的重构或拒绝任务，上下界最优至常数因子：自适应和非自适应复杂度分别为Θ(n log n+ε⁻¹log(1/δ))和Θ(n³+ε⁻¹log(1/δ))。

Conclusion: 本文给出了系统发育四分体系统性质测试的几乎最优自适应和非自适应测试器，首次实现了亚四次方查询复杂度，并建立了匹配的下界，证明其最优性。超图编码方法展示了与hereditary超图测试的联系，尽管实用性有限。整体工作完善了该问题从测试到重构的复杂度刻画。

Abstract: We study dense property testing for full systems of resolved quartet topologies on $n$ taxa: determining whether a system is induced by a phylogenetic tree or is $\varepsilon$-far from every tree-induced system. Our main result is an explicit polynomial-time adaptive one-sided-error tester. It reconstructs a candidate tree through anchored quartet queries and verifies the candidate using uniformly random quartet queries. With error probability $δ$, it uses $O\!\left(n\log n+\varepsilon^{-1}\log(1/δ)\right)$ queries. We also give a non-adaptive cached-anchor variant using $\binom{n-1}{3}+O\!\left(\varepsilon^{-1}\log(1/δ)\right)$ queries. Both improve the previous explicit $O(n^3/\varepsilon)$ query bound. Since the input contains $\binom{n}{4}=Θ(n^4)$ quartet entries, both testers use $o\!\left(\binom{n}{4}\right)$ queries for fixed~$\varepsilon$ and~$δ$.
  We additionally encode full quartet systems, equivariantly under relabeling, as directed, three-colored $4$-ary structures. Hereditary directed-hypergraph testing then yields an $n$-independent one-sided-error tester, although its dependence on $\varepsilon$ is quantitatively impractical.
  Finally, we prove lower bounds. In ordinary property testing, every adaptive randomized tester, even with two-sided error, requires asymptotically at least $\ln((1-δ)/δ)/\ln(1/(1-\varepsilon))$ queries as $n\to\infty$. Every one-sided-error tester requires $\ln(1/δ)/\ln(1/(1-\varepsilon))$ queries, matching the random-verification term up to rounding. For the stronger reconstruct-or-reject task, our upper bounds are optimal up to constant factors: the adaptive and non-adaptive complexities are $Θ(n\log n+\varepsilon^{-1}\log(1/δ))$ and $Θ(n^3+\varepsilon^{-1}\log(1/δ))$, respectively.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 先前工作对于已知链接函数的所有p≥1情形和未知链接函数的p=2情形已有研究成果，但对于未知链接函数和一般p≥1的组合尚未解决，这是更具挑战性的设定。

Method: 提出了一个非自适应采样算法。该算法对设计矩阵A有完全访问权限，但对响应向量b仅能通过坐标查询访问，通过精心设计的采样策略来估计f(Ax)，从而在未知链接函数下实现(1+ε)近似。

Result: 算法达到O(d^{p/2∨1}/ε^{p∨2} poly log(n/ε))的查询复杂度；同时为p>2建立了近乎紧的下界，有效缩小了单指标模型主动ℓ_p回归中剩余的理论差距。

Conclusion: 这项工作完成了单指标模型主动回归问题在未知链接函数和一般p≥1情形下的系统研究，上界与下界相互印证，为该领域提供了近乎完整的理论刻画。

Abstract: This paper studies active regression for single-index models under general $\ell_p$-loss with an unknown $1$-Lipschitz link function $f$, formulated as $\min_{f,x} \|f(Ax)-b\|_p^p$ with full access to $A$ but coordinate-query access to $b$. Prior work established upper bounds for known link functions for all $p\geq 1$ and for unknown link functions only in the $p=2$ case, together with lower bounds for $p\leq 2$. This work addresses the more challenging setting of unknown link functions and general $p \geq 1$. A non-adaptive sampling algorithm is presented that achieves a $(1+ε)$-approximation using $O(d^{p/2\vee 1}/ε^{p\vee 2}\operatorname{poly}\log(n/ε))$ queries. Nearly tight lower bounds are also established for $p>2$. These results close much of the remaining gap in active $\ell_p$-regression for single-index models.

</details>

Main category: cs.DS

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言极限生成是研究生成器如何从正例流中学习生成新有效字符串的理论框架。Kleinberg和Wei证明了确定性算法的最优下密度保证为1/2，但现有分析较为复杂。本文旨在提供一个更简单、统一的框架来获得最优下密度保证，并探索随机化和多排序场景下的扩展。

Method: 本文首先设计了一个更简单的确定性算法，通过统一的分析框架恢复最优的1/2下密度保证。然后利用该框架的灵活性进行两个扩展：(1) 针对不知情对手，引入随机化将最优保证提升到1-1/e；(2) 对任意有限多个重要性排序，证明可以同时对所有排序达到最优的确定性和随机化保证，即多排序场景下无需牺牲最优性。

Result: 给出了一个显著简化分析的最优确定性算法（达到1/2下密度保证）；随机化算法在不知情对手下达到1-1/e的最优保证；对有限多个排序可同时达到各自的最优保证，表明支持多重重要性概念不损失最优性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个简洁统一的框架来解决语言极限生成中的最优下密度问题，不仅简化了已有结果的证明，还通过随机化和多排序扩展展示了该框架的通用性和灵活性，为后续研究提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: Language generation in the limit is a theoretical framework for studying how a generator can learn to produce new valid strings from a stream of positive examples. In this model, an adversary chooses an unknown language from a countable family and enumerates its elements in an arbitrary order, while the generator must eventually output only elements of the language that have not yet appeared in the enumeration. Reliable generation is thus formalized through two eventual guarantees: validity and novelty relative to the observed data. To further quantify the breadth of the generator's outputs, Kleinberg and Wei (FOCS 2025, STOC 2026) introduced lower density as a measure of output coverage. Given an order representing the importance or relevance of possible outputs, lower density is the asymptotic lower bound, as $n$ grows, on the fraction of the first $n$ elements of the target language that the generator outputs before they appear in the data. Kleinberg and Wei showed that $1/2$ is the optimal lower-density guarantee for deterministic algorithms.
  We develop a simple and unified framework for obtaining optimal lower-density guarantees. We first give a deterministic algorithm that recovers the optimal guarantee of $1/2$ with a significantly simpler analysis than prior work. We then demonstrate the flexibility of our framework through two extensions. First, against an oblivious adversary, randomization raises the optimal guarantee to $1-1/e$. Second, for any finite collection of orders, the optimal deterministic and randomized guarantees can be achieved simultaneously with respect to every order, so accommodating multiple notions of importance or relevance entails no loss in the optimal guarantee.

</details>


### [36] [Context Compaction Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01326)
*Hayder Tirmazi,Sam Markelon,Allison Bishop,Michael Mitzenmacher*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM代理中的上下文压缩（context compaction）进行形式化研究，提出了两个博弈框架（Context Selection Game和Context Generation Game）来建模当代AI代理的两种压缩策略，并证明了上下文压缩与单向通信复杂度之间的等价关系，从而将通信复杂度的已知界直接迁移到上下文压缩领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然上下文压缩是AI代理在调用LLM时普遍依赖的核心过程（用于将代理状态压缩到有限的上下文窗口内），但这一过程从未经过严格的形式化分析。作者希望通过建立形式化框架来理解上下文压缩的理论基础和最优策略。

Method: 作者提出了两个博弈框架：（1）上下文选择博弈（Context Selection Game），建模从累积状态中选择子集保留的压缩算法；（2）上下文生成博弈（Context Generation Game），建模通过任意有界长度消息总结状态的压缩算法。然后证明上下文生成博弈与单向通信复杂度等价，表明最小压缩预算等于诱导通信问题在相同误差下的单向通信复杂度。同时证明上下文选择博弈对应单向通信协议的一个受限类别，并证明存在查询集合使生成比选择需要严格更少的预算。

Result: 证明了上下文生成博弈与单向通信复杂度之间的等价性；证明了上下文选择博弈是一类受限的单向通信协议；证明了存在一组查询使得上下文生成比上下文选择需要的预算严格更少；并以Anthropic的上下文压缩端点为案例，展示了如何衡量部署算法的性能与最优策略之间的差距。

Conclusion: 通过将上下文压缩与成熟的通信复杂度理论建立联系，本文为上下文压缩提供了形式化的分析基础，使得通信复杂度中已有的界限和工具可以直接应用于上下文压缩问题，并为评估实际部署的压缩算法提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have a bounded context window. The context window is the maximum input size an LLM can consume for a single inference. AI agents rely on a process called context compaction to fit their state within the context window when calling an LLM. Despite its ubiquity, context compaction has received essentially no formal analysis. In this paper, we initiate a formal study of context compaction. We first introduce a framework consisting of two games that capture the two algorithmic strategies for context compaction used by contemporary AI agents in practice. The Context Selection Game models context compaction algorithms that select a subset of an agent's accumulated state to retain. The Context Generation Game models context compaction algorithms that summarize an agent's state by an arbitrary message of bounded length. We then prove an equivalence between the Context Generation Game and one-way communication complexity. The minimum context compaction budget for answering a set of queries within a target error is equal to the one-way communication complexity of the induced communication problem at the same error. Known bounds from communication complexity therefore transfer directly to context compaction. We also show that the Context Selection Game corresponds to a restricted class of one-way communication protocols. Any gap between selection and generation is therefore a gap between two classes of communication protocols. We prove that there exists a set of queries for which generation needs strictly less budget than selection. The equivalence between the Context Generation Game and one-way communication also lets us measure how well a deployed context compaction algorithm performs on a query relative to the optimal strategy. As an example, we present a case study that evaluates Anthropic's context compaction endpoint on set membership queries.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将RLE压缩与suffixient set结合可以达到比传统后缀树更紧凑的空间占用，同时保持对run编码模式的高效MEM/SMEM查询能力，在生物信息学（如单倍型面板比对）中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We show how to store a text $T [1..n]$ consisting of $ρ_T$ runs in $O (ρ_T + χ)$ space, where $χ$ is the size of the smallest suffixient set for $T$, such that when we are given a pattern $P [1..m]$ consisting of $ρ_P$ runs we can find the maximal exact matches (MEMs) of $P$ with respect to $T$ in $O (ρ_P \log m)$ time plus constant time for each edge we would fully or partially descend in the suffix tree for $T$ while finding those MEMs. We then adapt and optimize our result to finding set-maximal exact matches (SMEMs) of query haplotypes with respect to stored haplotype panels.

</details>
