TL;DR: 本文研究由局部因子指定的两个指数大规模量子态（乘积态）之间的迹距离近似计算问题，给出了一个确定性常数因子近似算法，其运行时间在因子数、局部维度和输入比特长度上均为多项式；同时证明即使对于对角量子比特态，精确计算该问题是#P-难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态之间的迹距离是量子信息中最基本的度量之一，但直接计算涉及指数规模矩阵的1-范数，在实际中不可行。当量子态由局部因子（张量积结构）指定时，能否高效近似迹距离是一个基础且重要的问题，对量子态层析、量子信道容量估计等应用有直接意义。

Method: 论文采用多步技术路线：(1) 利用局部Uhlmann最优纯化将迹距离问题约化为乘积保真度缺陷（product-fidelity defect）的估计；(2) 将问题进一步转化为结构化一阶算子迹范数的计算；(3) 构造一个局部凸代理（surrogate），该代理可通过多项式规模的经典锥规划（conic formulation）求解；(4) 用平方函数估计（square-function estimate）证明代理是迹范数的上界；(5) 通过对偶性和局部去相干（local dephasing）将反向比较归约为独立中心随机变量的头尾不等式（head-tail inequality），证明代理最多是迹范数的常数倍。

Result: (1) 对于有理数乘积态输入，给出了通用常数因子内的确定性近似算法，运行时间为多项式； (2) 证明了对角量子比特态情形下精确计算迹距离（等价于乘积分布之间的全变差距离）是#P-难的，说明近似与精确计算之间计算复杂度存在根本鸿沟。

Conclusion: 论文确立了乘积量子态迹距离的近似计算可高效求解（多项式时间常数因子近似）而精确计算不可行（#P-难）的复杂度图景，其技术核心在于用局部凸代理和概率不等式逼近指数规模算子的迹范数，为量子态距离的高效估计提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the trace distance \[D_{\mathrm{tr}}(ρ,σ)
  =\frac12\|ρ-σ\|_1,
  ρ=\bigotimes_{i=1}^nρ_i,\quad
  σ=\bigotimes_{i=1}^nσ_i, \] when the two exponentially large states are specified by their local factors. We give a deterministic approximation within a universal constant factor for rational product inputs. Its running time is polynomial in the number of factors, the local dimension, and the input bit length. In the opposite direction, exact computation is $\#\mathsf P$-hard even for diagonal qubit states, by the corresponding hardness of total variation distance between product distributions.
  The proof uses local Uhlmann-optimal purifications to reduce the problem to estimating the product-fidelity defect and the trace norm of a structured first-order operator. Although this operator acts on an exponentially large space, we approximate its trace norm by a local convex surrogate that admits a polynomial-size classical conic formulation. A square-function estimate shows that the surrogate upper-bounds this trace norm. Conversely, duality and local dephasing reduce the reverse comparison to a head--tail inequality for independent centered random variables, showing that the surrogate is at most a dimension-free constant times the same norm.

</details>

Result: 证明了MMCS算法不是输出多项式的，通过下界构造揭示了其最坏情况性能缺陷。对启发式选择进行了深入分析，并提出了新的扩展启发式，在大量实验证明其在实际运行时间上优于现有MMCS。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了MMCS算法在最坏情况复杂度分析上的空白，同时通过理论分析与实践结合的方式，在保持或提升实际性能的同时推进了对该经典枚举问题算法复杂度的理解。

Abstract: The Transversal Hypergraph problem is to enumerate (list) all inclusion-wise minimal hitting sets of a given hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$. It is the most important open question in enumeration whether this problem admits an output-polynomial algorithm whose running time scales polynomially with the size of $\mathcal{H}$ and the number of solutions. Currently, Minimal-to-Maximal Conversion Search (MMCS) by Murakami and Uno [DAM 2014] is the most efficient algorithm for real-world instances, but there are no worst-case performance guarantees known for it. We prove that MMCS is in fact not output-polynomial. The lower bound construction motivates a more detailed analysis of how certain heuristic choices in the algorithm design affect the running time. We conduct a thorough analysis of those heuristics and, based on this, propose new extension. We then show in extensive running time experiments that this new heuristic further improves practical performance.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本算法虽然查询复杂度最优（n|P|次），但高度串行化。Black、Mazumdar和Saha [COLT 2025]给出了确定性算法的紧致轮/查询权衡，但他们将随机化下界证明留作开放问题。本研究旨在探索随机化是否能显著改变该问题的轮复杂度图景，并填补确定性结果中未知划分数情形下的空白。

Method: 作者设计了多个随机化算法：1) 当k已知时，利用随机化分组策略，通过3轮交互实现O(nk log n)次查询的高概率成功；2) 当k未知时，设计了4轮随机化算法。对于下界，通过构造对抗性论证证明2轮随机化算法需要Ω(n^{4/3}k^{2/3})次查询，以及3轮无法在未知k的情况下达到近似最优查询复杂度。

Result: 1) 当k已知时：3轮随机化算法仅需O(nk log n)次查询，而2轮则需要Ω(n^{4/3}k^{2/3})次查询（与确定性相同）。2) 当k未知时：4轮随机化算法可用O(n|P|log²n)次查询，但3轮无法实现近似最优。3) 在k未知场景下，随机化与确定性算法存在更大分离：确定性算法需要Θ(log n/log log n)轮才能达到近似最优查询复杂度，而随机化只需常数轮。

Conclusion: 随机化显著降低了学习隐藏划分问题的轮复杂度：在k已知时从Θ(log log n)轮降至常数3轮；在k未知时确定性需要Θ(log n/log log n)轮而随机化仅需4轮。这一结果表明随机化在该问题中带来了本质性的计算优势，同时也回答了Black等人的开放问题。

Abstract: We study the round complexity of learning a hidden partition $\mathcal{P}$ of an $n$-element universe using PAIR queries: PAIR($x,y$) tells us whether $x$ and $y$ belong to the same part of the partition or not. While it is easy to learn using $n|\mathcal{P}|$ queries using a basic algorithm and this query complexity is optimal, this basic algorithm is highly sequential. Black, Mazumdar, and Saha [COLT 2025] recently gave tight deterministic round/query tradeoffs when the number of parts of $\mathcal{P}$ is known. In particular they prove $Θ(\log\log n)$ rounds are sufficient and necessary to limit the number of queries to $n|\mathcal{P}|$. They leave proving a randomized lower bound as an open direction.
  We show that randomization dramatically changes the picture. When the number of parts $k = |\mathcal{P}|$ is known, we give a simple 3-round randomized algorithm using $O(nk\log n)$ queries with high probability, and prove that 2 rounds require $Ω(n^{4/3}k^{2/3})$ queries -- the same as deterministic algorithms. We also study a more general setting where the number of parts is unknown. In this case, we give a 4-round randomized algorithm using $O(n|\mathcal P|\log^2 n)$ queries with high probability, and prove that 3-rounds cannot achieve near-optimal query complexity. Furthermore, we show an even bigger separation in this regime between randomized and deterministic algorithms: for the latter, $Θ(\log n/\log\log n)$ rounds are necessary and sufficient to obtain near-optimal query complexity.

</details>


### [41] [Reconfiguration of Temporal Networks under Reachability Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02227)
*Othon Michail,George Skretas,Georg Tennigkeit,Shaily Verma*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了时序网络中的时间标签重配置问题（time label reconfiguration problem）。给定一个静态图和两个边的可用时间标签函数，目标是通过每次修改一条边的标签，从一个标签配置转换到另一个配置，同时始终保持源节点的时序可达性。研究发现该问题的复杂性存在显著转变：单源时可在多项式时间内求解，双源时变为PSPACE-hard。此外，若静态图是无环图或近似锦标赛图，则所有有效标签配置之间都可以互相重配置，且证明是构造性的，可产生多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序网络广泛存在于交通运输、通信和工业控制系统等实际应用中。在许多场景下，需要将一个已有的时序网络通过一系列原子修改转换为目标网络，同时保持其核心功能（如时序可达性）不中断。然而，此前缺乏对这一变换过程的形式化理论框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，为时序网络的标签重配置问题提供理论基础，并揭示其计算复杂性。

Method: 本文形式化定义了时间标签重配置问题，并针对有向时序图上的时序可达性约束进行研究。作者分析了不同条件下的复杂性：分别考虑单源和双源情形，以及静态图拓扑结构（一般图、无环图、几乎锦标赛图）对可重配置性的影响。证明采用构造性方法，通过设计具体的重配置序列来证明可达性并推导多项式时间的算法。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）单源情形下问题可在多项式时间内求解；（2）双源情形下问题变为PSPACE-hard，呈现尖锐的复杂性转变；（3）当静态图是无环图或近似锦标赛图时，所有有效标签配置之间均可互相重配置。所有证明均为构造性的，提供了多项式时间的算法。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统地研究了时序网络中的时间标签重配置问题，揭示了源节点数量对计算复杂性的关键影响（1个源到2个源之间从P到PSPACE-hard的跳跃），并给出了特定图类上的完全可重配置性结果。这些结果为时序网络的安全转换与维护提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Temporal networks model dynamic systems in which edges represent interactions and labels specify when these interactions occur. Examples include transportation networks, time-sensitive communication networks, and industrial control systems. In many such applications, an existing temporal network must be transformed into a desired one through a sequence of atomic modifications while maintaining essential functionality throughout the transformation. We formalize the time label reconfiguration problem and provide a theoretical framework for reasoning about such transformation processes.
  As temporal reachability is a central functionality in many temporal networks, we study a reconfiguration problem on directed temporal graphs subject to temporal reachability constraints. We are given a static graph with a designated set of sources, along with two labeling functions indicating an availability time for every edge. The goal is to transform one labeling into the other by changing the label of a single edge at a time while maintaining temporal reachability of the sources throughout. Our results reveal a sharp complexity transition: the problem is polynomial-time solvable for a single source but becomes PSPACE-hard with two sources. We also show that if the static graph is acyclic or an almost-tournament graph, then all valid labelings can be reconfigured into each other. Our proofs are constructive and yield polynomial-time algorithms.

</details>


### [42] [Noisy k-means++ is Not too Noisy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02318)
*Poojan Shah*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明ε-噪声k-means++算法能达到经典k-means++的渐近最优保证，即期望近似比8(ln k+2)((1+ε)/(1-ε))^4 = (1+O(ε))·8(ln k+2)，并给出两个下界分离结果：噪声的线性依赖是必要的，且逐点乘性噪声控制是本质条件（换成逐轮总变差接近则完全无有限近似保证）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bhattacharya等人引入ε-噪声k-means++仅获得O(log²k)保证，Grunau等人虽恢复O(log k)但常数因子高达147,638，即使ε→0也不退化。这引发了核心问题：k-means++对微小噪声是否高度敏感？能否达到(1+O(ε))倍经典保证的界？解决该开放问题对理解k-means++算法的鲁棒性至关重要。

Method: 采用精细的势函数分析，对噪声下的D²采样过程进行更紧的期望递推推导，将噪声乘性因子(1±ε)的影响通过(1+ε)/(1-ε)⁴的幂次形式精确刻画，从而消除先前分析中的巨大常数损耗，得到单常数8的干净上界。同时构造两个分离实例来证明下界。

Result: 1) 上界：期望近似比为8(ln k+2)·((1+ε)/(1-ε))⁴ = (1+O(ε))·8(ln k+2)，当ε→0时收敛到经典k-means++的常数倍。2) 下界分离一：存在噪声实例使近似比损失1+Ω(ε)，说明对噪声的线性依赖不可消除。3) 下界分离二：若将逐点乘性噪声替换为逐轮总变差接近，即使k=2也不存在有限近似保证，证明该质量控制方式的本质必要性。

Conclusion: 本文肯定地回答了Grunau等人的开放问题：k-means++对ε-噪声并非高度敏感，可获得与经典情况仅差(1+O(ε))因子的最优期望近似保证。同时也严格刻画了该结果的紧性边界——噪声线性依赖不可避免，且逐点乘性控制是保证算法可用的关键条件。

Abstract: The celebrated $k$-means++ algorithm of Arthur and Vassilvitskii (SODA 2007) achieves an $O(\log k)$ expected approximation for the classical $k$-means problem using $D^2$-sampling, a technique now ubiquitous in clustering algorithm design. Bhattacharya et al. (ESA 2020) introduced $\varepsilon$-noisy $k$-means++, where sampling probabilities may incur an adversarial multiplicative error of $(1\pm\varepsilon)$, but obtained only an $O(\log^2 k)$ guarantee. Grunau et al. (ESA 2023) recovered the asymptotic $O(\log k)$ guarantee, but their analysis loses a constant factor of roughly $147{,}638$ even as $\varepsilon\to0$, leaving open whether $k$-means++ is highly sensitive to even a small amount of noise. They asked whether a bound within $1+O(\varepsilon)$ of the classical guarantee is possible. We resolve this affirmatively, proving an expected approximation guarantee of $8(\ln k+2)\left(\frac{1+\varepsilon}{1-\varepsilon}\right)^4 = (1+O(\varepsilon))\,8(\ln k+2)$. We complement the upper bound with two separations. First, a noisy version of the Arthur and Vassilvitskii lower-bound instance incurs a $1+Ω(\varepsilon)$ loss over exact $k$-means++, so linear dependence on the noise is necessary. Second, pointwise multiplicative control is qualitatively essential: replacing it with per-round total variation closeness admits no finite approximation guarantee, even for $k=2$.

</details>


### [43] [Recovering Planted Colorings in Sublinear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02338)
*Weronika Wrzos-Kaminska*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次为对抗性种植k染色问题提出了亚线性时间算法。给定一个带有种植染色的d-正则谱λ-扩张图，算法通过预处理时间和空间以及查询时间均为Õ(n^(1/2+O(1/log(d/λ))))的数据结构，能够在至多O(√(λ/d))比例的顶点上出错（模k种颜色的排列）的情况下恢复种植染色。该算法通过实现归一化邻接矩阵底部特征空间的亚线性时间内积访问，将Alon和Kahale的经典谱方法适配到亚线性时间框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 种植k染色问题作为组合优化和图论中的重要问题，此前仅在随机图模型（如Blum和Spencer、Alon和Kahale的工作）中有高效算法。David和Feige提出了更一般的对抗性种植模型，但现有的算法复杂度较高（接近线性或多项式时间）。作者的目标是探索在亚线性时间内能否恢复种植染色，这不仅是理论上的重要突破，也对于大规模图数据处理具有重要意义。

Method: 算法采用经典的谱方法框架（Alon和Kahale的方法），但关键创新在于构建一个支持亚线性时间内积访问的数据结构，从而能够高效地访问归一化邻接矩阵的底部特征空间。预处理阶段构造该数据结构，耗时和空间均为Õ(n^(1/2+O(1/log(d/λ))))；查询阶段利用该数据结构回答给定顶点的颜色查询，查询时间同样为亚线性。算法结果保证与真实种植染色在除O(√(λ/d))比例顶点外一致（模颜色置换）。

Result: 这是首个在对抗性种植模型中恢复种植k染色的亚线性时间算法。预处理和查询时间均为Õ(n^(1/2+O(1/log(d/λ))))，错误率至多为O(√(λ/d))比例的顶点。该结果显著改善了此前在该模型中的算法复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了即使在对对抗性设定的种植问题中，亚线性时间的恢复也是可行的。通过亚线性时间内积访问底部特征空间这一技术，经典谱算法可以被有效地扩展到大规模场景。这为后续在亚线性时间内解决更多结构化图问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: We give a sublinear algorithm for the planted $k$-coloring problem. Given an expander $G$ with a planted coloring, the goal is to efficiently determine the color class of a given vertex.
  We work in the adversarial planted coloring model of David and Feige [STOC 2016], where an adversary chooses a $d$-regular spectral $λ$-expander $G$ on $n$ vertices and plants a balanced $k$-coloring by partitioning the vertices into $k$ equal parts and deleting all edges within each part. This model generalizes the earlier random graph models studied by Blum and Spencer [J. Algorithms 1995] and Alon and Kahale [STOC 1994].
  We give the first sublinear-time algorithm for recovering planted colorings in this model. The algorithm has preprocessing time and space $\widetilde O\left(n^{1/2+O(1/\log(d/λ))}\right)$, and produces a data structure that answers color queries in time $\widetilde O\left(n^{1/2+O(1/\log(d/λ))}\right)$, such that the resulting labeling agrees with the planted coloring on all but an $O(\sqrt{λ/d})$ fraction of vertices, up to a permutation of the $k$ colors.
  The algorithm gives sublinear-time inner product access to the bottom eigenspace of the normalized adjacency matrix, which allows us to adapt the classical spectral approach of Alon and Kahale in sublinear time.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出用于最短向量问题(SVP)的随机化算法，在经典计算下时间复杂度为2^{0.6039n+o(n)}，量子计算下为2^{0.5411n+o(n)}，空间复杂度为2^{0.5n+o(n)}，显著改进了之前AGRS'15的2^{n+o(n)}时间与空间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVP是格密码学中最核心的困难问题之一，其高效算法的设计对理解格问题的计算复杂度和密码分析具有重要意义。此前最佳算法的复杂度为2^{n+o(n)}时间与空间，存在显著的改进空间。

Method: 算法核心利用周期高斯函数在半个最短向量处的Hessian矩阵性质：对于最短向量v，Hessian在v/2处具有接近v的特征向量，可通过(预处理)有界距离解码算法恢复v。由于模L的周期性，候选中点由L/2L中的奇偶类索引，算法通过离散高斯采样估计对应Hessian来搜索最短向量的奇偶类。进一步通过随机子格陪集和多种采样技术优化算法复杂度。

Result: 经典时间2^{0.6039n+o(n)}，量子时间2^{0.5411n+o(n)}，空间2^{0.5n+o(n)}，均优于此前AGRS'15的最佳2^{n+o(n)}算法。

Conclusion: 该工作大幅提升了SVP算法的复杂度上界，证明了利用周期高斯函数Hessian的结构性质可以高效求解SVP，所提出的优化技术（随机子格陪集和采样技巧）可能具有独立的研究价值和应用前景。

Abstract: We present randomized algorithms for the shortest vector problem (SVP). For the $n$-dimensional lattice $\mathcal L$, our algorithms solve SVP in time $2^{0.6039n+o(n)}$ classically and $2^{0.5411n+o(n)}$ quantumly and space $2^{0.5n+o(n)}$, improving the previous best algorithm running in $2^{n+o(n)}$ time and space of Aggarwal, Dadush, Regev, and Stephens-Davidowitz [STOC'15].
  Our algorithms heavily use the property of the Hessian of the periodic Gaussian function at the half shortest vector: For a shortest vector $v \in \mathcal L$, the Hessian at $v/2$ has the eigenvector close to $v$, which can be used to recover $v$ using the (preprocessing) bounded distance decoding algorithm. Given the periodicity modulo $\mathcal L$, the candidate midpoints are indexed by the parity classes in $\mathcal L/2\mathcal L$. Our algorithm searches for the class of a shortest vector by estimating the corresponding Hessians using discrete Gaussian samples.
  We optimize the algorithm using random sublattice cosets and various sampling technique, achieving the final complexity. The optimization techniques may be of independent interest.

</details>

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了二分图独立集固定切片问题的近似计数与采样的最坏情况可计算性。作者证明：当总体密度α位于(1/Δ, 1/2)区间且两侧密度比随机Δ-正则二分图的典型相密度更平衡时，不存在FPRAS或高效采样方案（除非NP=RP）。此外，论文还研究了平衡硬核模型（要求|I∩L|=|I∩R|），证明了其计算阈值与一般有界度图上的硬核模型相同：当λ<λ_c(Δ)时有FPTAS和高效采样，当λ>λ_c(Δ)时则不存在（除非NP=RP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期Kocurek等人通过将随机正则二分图分解为固定大小的切片，给出了硬核模型的高效采样算法。本文旨在研究这种固定切片问题的更一般最坏情况可计算性，即对于任意二分图上的固定切片独立集采样问题，其计算复杂度边界在哪里。同时，作者还希望理解平衡约束（两侧密度相等）如何影响硬核模型的计算阈值。

Method: 论文采用计算复杂性归约方法。对于固定切片问题，作者证明了当密度条件满足特定平衡性要求时，该问题与已知的#P-hard问题归约等价，从而得出不存在FPRAS的结论。对于平衡硬核模型，作者通过将该模型与一般有界度图上的硬核模型建立计算等价关系，利用已知的硬核模型相变阈值λ_c(Δ)来确定平衡模型的计算相变点。

Result: 主要结果有两个：(1) 当总体密度α∈(1/Δ, 1/2)且两侧密度比随机Δ-正则二分图的典型相密度更平衡时，固定切片问题不存在FPRAS或高效采样方案（除非NP=RP）。(2) 平衡硬核模型（要求|I∩L|=|I∩R|）在一般有界度二分图上具有与硬核模型完全相同的计算阈值：λ<λ_c(Δ)时存在FPTAS和高效采样，λ>λ_c(Δ)时不存在FPRAS或高效采样器（除非NP=RP），适用于所有固定Δ≥3。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了二分图独立集固定切片问题的硬性边界：当密度处于特定区间且两侧平衡性超过随机图典型水平时，问题变得计算困难。同时，平衡约束本身（要求两侧相等）并不会改变硬核模型的计算阈值，说明这种对称约束在计算复杂性意义上不引入额外的可计算性变化。这些结果加深了对硬核模型在受约束设置下计算复杂性的理解。

Abstract: Motivated by recent work of Kocurek, Oveis Gharan, and Tjowasi, which gives an efficient sampling algorithm for the hard-core model on random regular bipartite graphs by decomposing into fixed-size slices, we study the worst-case tractability of approximate counting and sampling of fixed-size slices for bipartite independent set problems. Let $G=(L\sqcup R,E)$ be a bipartite graph with $|L|=|R|=n$ and maximum degree $Δ$. The fixed-slice problem asks to sample uniformly from independent sets satisfying $|I\cap L|=α_L n$ and $|I\cap R|=α_R n$. We show that if the overall density $α$ lies in the interval $(\frac{1}Δ, \tfrac{1}{2})$, and the densities on the two sides are more balanced than the typical phase densities of a random $Δ$-regular bipartite graph, then there is no FPRAS or efficient sampling scheme unless $\mathbf{NP}=\mathbf{RP}$.
  We then study a related fugacity model in which the densities are not fixed, but the independent set is required to be balanced between the two sides of the bipartition. For $λ>0$, the balanced hard-core model is the ordinary hard-core model with fugacity $λ$, conditioned on the event $|I\cap L|=|I\cap R|$. We prove that this model has the same computational threshold as the hard-core model on general bounded-degree graphs. That is, for every fixed $Δ\ge 3$, if $λ<λ_c(Δ)$, then the balanced partition function admits an FPTAS and the balanced hard-core distribution admits an efficient sampling scheme. Conversely, if $λ>λ_c(Δ)$, then no FPRAS or efficient sampler exists on this graph class unless $\mathbf{NP}=\mathbf{RP}$.

</details>

Abstract: We present a fully polynomial-time randomised approximation scheme (FPRAS) for the two-terminal reliability problem on general graphs, both directed and undirected. We also show that the complementary unreliability question is \BIS-hard. The key idea of the algorithm was discovered by GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra.

### [47] [Pairwise-Independent Dithering for Single-Stage Hadamard Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02564)
*Honghao Lin,Vahab Mirrokni,David P. Woodruff*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种单阶段的无偏抖动量化器，基于随机Hadamard变换和跨坐标成对独立抖动，省略了Feng等人两阶段构造中的残差量化阶段，在保持无偏性的同时将每个坐标的通信开销降至b比特，并将前导上界常数降低了约5.93倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维向量的量化是相似性搜索、分布式学习和模型压缩的基础。Feng等人提出的无偏抖动量化器虽然具有尖锐的保证，但其1/d尺度内积估计器需要第二阶段随机变换和残差量化，增加了通信开销和证明中前导常数。作者旨在证明这一额外阶段是不必要的。

Method: 作者展示了跨Hadamard坐标的成对独立抖动足以替代Feng等人的两阶段构造。具体地，使用单阶段无偏估计器，每个坐标用b比特量化，内积估计误差满足给定上界。证明最初由一个Google内部开发的基于Gemini的全自动智能体系统获得，作者随后验证并编辑了证明。

Result: 单阶段估计器实现了E[|⟨y,x̂−x⟩|²] ≤ (3π√3/2 + o(1))·‖y‖₂²/(d·4^b)的界（b→∞），其中o(1)项是与维度无关的，对单位输入和固定查询一致成立。相比Feng等人的两阶段构造，消除了残差阶段O(d)比特的负载，并将前导上界常数减少约5.93倍。

Conclusion: 跨坐标成对独立的抖动足以达到与两阶段Hadamard抖动量化相当的保证，同时显著降低通信开销和常数因子。此外，该论文还展示了使用自动化智能体系统（基于Gemini）辅助完成数学证明的可行性和潜力。

Abstract: Quantizing high-dimensional vectors is fundamental to similarity search, distributed learning, and model compression. Feng, Indyk, Kapralov, Krachun, and Prokhorov established sharp guarantees for an unbiased dithered quantizer based on a randomized Hadamard transform [FIK+26]. Their $1/d$-scale inner-product estimator, however, uses a second randomized transform and residual quantization, increasing both communication and the leading constant in the proved bound. We show that this extra stage is unnecessary: pairwise-independent dithers across Hadamard coordinates suffice. The resulting unbiased single-stage estimator uses $b$ bits per coordinate and achieves \[ \mathbb{E}\!\left[
  \left|\left\langle y,\widehat{x}-x\right\rangle\right|^2 \right] \leq \left(\frac{3π\sqrt{3}}{2}+o(1)\right) \frac{\lVert y\rVert_2^2}{d\,4^b}, \] as $b\to\infty$, with a dimension-free $o(1)$ term uniform over unit inputs and fixed queries. Compared with the two-stage construction of Feng et al., it eliminates the residual-stage $O(d)$-bit payload and reduces the leading upper-bound constant by a factor of approximately $5.93$.
  The proof was first obtained using a fully automated Gemini-based agentic system developed internally at Google. The authors have verified the proof and edited it for clarity of presentation.

</details>


### [48] [The Condition-Number Barrier in Sparse Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02588)
*Honghao Lin,Vahab Mirrokni,David P. Woodruff*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文在随机化精确体积小集合扩张假设（SSEH）下，证明了稀疏凸优化中受限条件数的线性依赖无法被多项式时间算法改进的下界猜想（即[AS21]猜想）。具体而言，对于每个固定的γ∈(0,1]，不存在随机化多项式时间算法能以至少2/3的概率返回一个x，使其在稀疏度s=O(k·κ_{s+k}^{1-γ})的情况下达到最优稀疏解的ε近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Axiotis和Sviridenko在[AS21]中猜想，稀疏凸优化中受限条件数的线性依赖无法被多项式时间算法改进，但该猜想此前未得到证明。建立这一下界对于理解稀疏优化问题的计算复杂性具有重要意义。

Method: 该证明依赖于随机化精确体积小集合扩张假设（SSEH）的加权正则图形式（由Raghavendra, Steurer和Tulsiani提出）。通过该复杂性假设建立了最小二乘目标函数的下界，证明对任意固定的γ∈(0,1]，不存在随机化多项式时间算法能够改进受限条件数的线性依赖。

Result: 成功证明了[AS21]关于最小二乘目标函数的猜想，建立了条件性下界。结果在有理数实例且A具有满列秩的情况下依然成立。值得注意的是，证明最初是通过Google内部开发的基于Gemini的全自动化代理系统获得的，随后由作者验证并编辑。

Conclusion: 本文在SSEH假设下确认了稀疏凸优化中受限条件数线性依赖的不可改进性，为稀疏优化算法的计算复杂度提供了坚实的理论下界。

Abstract: In [AS21], Axiotis and Sviridenko conjectured that the linear dependence on the restricted condition number in sparse convex optimization cannot be improved by a polynomial-time algorithm. We establish their conjectured lower bound for least-squares objectives, conditional on the randomized exact-volume Small-Set Expansion Hypothesis in the weighted regular-graph formulation of Raghavendra, Steurer, and Tulsiani [RST12]. Concretely, for every fixed $γ\in(0,1]$, there is no randomized polynomial-time algorithm that, with probability at least $2/3$, returns a vector $x$ such that, writing $s=\lVert x\rVert_0$, \[
  \lVert Ax-b\rVert_2^2
  \leq
  \min_{\lVert z\rVert_0\leq k}\lVert Az-b\rVert_2^2+\varepsilon
  \quad\text{and}\quad
  s=O\!\left(k\,κ_{s+k}^{\,1-γ}\right), \] where $κ_r$ is the restricted condition number at sparsity level $r$. The result holds even on rational instances with $A$ of full column rank.
  The proof was first obtained using a fully automated Gemini-based agentic system developed internally at Google. The authors have verified the proof and edited it for clarity of presentation.

</details>

### [49] [CITBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Interactive Tabular Data Processing with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00018)
*Zihan Nan,Yang Gu,Wei Liu,Xi Yan,Zhou Liu,Hao Liang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CITBench，一个用于评估LLM在交互式表格数据处理任务上能力的综合基准。该基准涵盖4大类（表格匹配、清洗、增强、转换）、18种任务类型和1296个实例，支持离线和在线评估两种模式。实验发现现有LLM在简单表格和规则下表现良好，但在表格复杂度增加、规则依赖性增强和交互噪声增大的情况下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格处理基准主要关注单轮、完全指定的指令下的表格推理，未能充分反映真实场景中通过多轮交互、需求不断演变的复杂表格处理任务，因此需要一个新的基准来弥补这一差距。

Method: CITBench包含：（1）一个全面的分类体系，覆盖表格匹配、清洗、增强、转换四大类，共18种任务类型和1296个实例；（2）支持离线和在线两种评估模式，其中在线模式模拟多轮交互，包含受限操作流程和结构化任务脚本，捕捉用户参与式表格处理的关键行为特征。

Result: 对多种开源和闭源LLM的评估显示一致趋势：当前模型在简单表格和规则上表现良好，但随着表格复杂度增加、规则依赖性增强以及多轮交互模拟中噪声增多，模型性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 研究发现LLM在扩展交互式数据处理场景中仍面临理解、规划和表格结构感知方面的持续挑战，表明当前模型距离实际可用的交互式表格处理助手仍有差距。

Abstract: Tabular data processing is central to data work, and LLM-based assistants have recently shown promising capabilities in supporting such tasks. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on table reasoning under single-turn, fully specified instructions, underrepresenting complex table processing that unfolds through multi-turn interactions with evolving user requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce CITBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating LLMs on interactive tabular data processing. CITBench features a comprehensive taxonomy across four high-level categories--table matching, cleaning, augmentation, and transformation--spanning 18 task types and 1,296 instances curated from datasets across diverse domains. The benchmark supports both offline and online evaluation, where the online setting models multi-turn interactions under constrained operation procedures and structured task scripts, capturing key potential behavioral characteristics of user-in-the-loop tabular data processing. We evaluate a broad suite of open-source and closed-source LLMs on CITBench, revealing a consistent trend: while current models perform well on simple tables and rules, their performance degrades significantly with increasing table complexity, tighter rule dependencies, and noisy multi-turn interaction simulations. These results highlight persistent challenges in understanding, planning, and table-structure awareness for LLMs in extended interactive data processing scenarios.

</details>


### [50] [Machine-Checked Dual-Write Recovery from a Committed Log](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00501)
*Andreas Andreakis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在Isabelle/HOL中机器验证的理论，精确刻画了事务性发件箱和变更数据捕获中"恰好一次投递"恢复保证的边界条件。核心是一个信息界定理：崩溃后的恢复决策仅凭本地持久状态无法同时避免重复投递和漏投递，必须读取接收方的已接受记录才能精确逃逸此界限。论文还证明了对陈旧答案的防护栅栏（fencing）以及保证的时效性（去重内存有界性和源历史截断都会以可证明的方式使保证失效）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事务性发件箱和变更数据捕获解决了应用的双写问题，但它们引入的中继层将投递和记录进度作为两个独立的持久化操作，导致"另一方是否已收到"的决策在系统崩溃后仍然存在。实践者用重试、检查点、幂等键和栅栏处理了这个边界十年，但缺少一个精确的理论来说明这些机制何时有效、需要什么证据、证据能持续多久。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在Isabelle/HOL中构建机器检查的分布式系统理论。核心方法包括：(1) 定义一个信息界定理，证明两个可达的崩溃后状态可以在崩溃方持久所知完全一致的情况下，对接收方接受的内容存在分歧，从而任何仅基于该侧信息的恢复决策必然导致重复或遗漏投递；(2) 证明读取接收方已接受记录可以精确逃逸此信息界；(3) 对每个危险性（旧请求仍滞留、第二个恢复者竞争）证明在接收方接受边界处的栅栏；(4) 证明栅栏本身的成本也是一个定理；(5) 证明去重内存有界性和源历史截断会以可证明方式使保证失效。

Result: 论文给出了一个短测试标准来衡量任何"恰好一次"恢复声明：接收方接受了什么、什么还能改变这个答案、证据能存活多久。证明了单一确定性的"先投递后检查点"协议，即使其持久游标被恢复读取，也会仅因崩溃时序而被击败。同时证明了读取接收方的接受记录是精确逃逸信息界的方法，并为每个风险提供了在接收方接受边界处的定理化栅栏。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个完整的、机器验证的关于恰好一次投递语义的理论框架，将所有实践中的经验性建议（重试、检查点、幂等键、围栏）统一到一个精确的信息论视角下。核心洞见是：恢复决策的信息来源决定了其正确性的上限，而保证的持久性取决于证据的存活时间。这为实践中验证和审计精确一次投递系统提供了理论依据。

Abstract: After a crash, a delivery process faces a question its own database cannot answer: did the other side already receive the effect? Transactional outboxes and change data capture remove the application's dual write, but the relay they introduce delivers and records its own progress as two separate durable acts, so the same decision reappears one stage later.
  Practitioners have handled this boundary for a decade with retries, checkpoints, idempotency keys, and fencing, and the operational advice is largely sound. What has been missing is a precise account of when it works: the exact event the guarantees refer to, the evidence they require, and how long that evidence lasts. This paper supplies the missing account as a machine-checked theory, developed in Isabelle/HOL.
  At its heart is an information bound. Two reachable post-crash states can agree on everything the crashed side durably knows and still differ in what the sink accepted, so any recovery decision computed from that side must duplicate a delivery or leave one owed. This is not a story about sloppy bookkeeping: a single deterministic deliver-then-checkpoint protocol, its own durable cursor included in what recovery reads, is defeated by crash timing alone. Reading the sink's accepted record escapes the bound exactly, under stated premises. The answer can then go stale -- an old request still in flight, a second recoverer racing the first -- and each hazard has its own proved fence at the sink's acceptance boundary, the cost of fencing itself a theorem. Finally, the guarantee has a lifetime: bounded deduplication memory and truncated source history each void it in a proved way. The resulting test for any exactly-once recovery claim is short: what did the sink accept, what can still change that answer, and how long will the evidence survive?

</details>

*Xufei Wu,Pengcheng Zhang,Yitong Song,Xiaobo Zhang,Anqi Liang,Kai Wang,Jijun Du,Yidi Xiong,Guangxu Cheng,Zhe Chen,Peng Chen,Guoliang Li,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了TEngineDB-V，一个OLAP原生的向量搜索系统，专为大规模k（k=10³–10⁵）的分析型向量搜索工作负载设计，通过将向量搜索解耦为关系表并融入OLAP优化，实现了最高145倍于StarRocks的加速和522倍的生产部署性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量搜索系统无法很好地支持大规模k的分析型向量搜索：专门的向量数据库通常限制k的规模以满足尾延迟约束且分析支持有限，而OLAP系统将向量索引作为黑盒嵌入，导致严重的读写放大且无法进行原生查询优化。随着LLM数据管理和广告分析等新兴工作负载的出现，这一问题日益突出。

Method: TEngineDB-V的方法包括：(1) 全局分段解耦的索引以关系表形式物化，消除scatter-gather执行，减少放大效应； (2) 将IVFPQ搜索分解为关系运算符，集成OLAP优化； (3) 提出DPPQ（方向感知量化+层次化残差细化）提高召回率并保持关系效率； (4) 引入索引感知的查询重写和分布式感知成本模型以支持高效分布式执行。

Result: 实验表明，TEngineDB-V相较于StarRocks等竞争系统实现了最高145倍的加速，在百亿规模的生产部署中性能提升高达52倍。

Conclusion: TEngineDB-V证明了将向量搜索作为OLAP引擎的一等公民（first-class primitive）是处理大规模k分析型向量搜索的有效路径，通过关系化索引和原生优化在性能上大幅超越现有方案。

Abstract: Vector search systems are essential infrastructure for modern data-driven applications. Large-$k$ analytical vector search, which retrieves $k=10^3$--$10^5$ results for analytics (e.g., aggregation, filtering, joins), is increasingly important for emerging workloads, including LLM data management and advertising analysis at Tencent. Existing systems remain inadequate: specialized vector databases often cap $k$ (e.g., $k \leq 10^4$) to satisfy tail-latency constraints and offer limited analytical support, while OLAP systems typically embed per-segment vector indexes as black boxes, causing severe read/compute amplification and preventing native query optimization.
  This paper presents TEngineDB-V, an OLAP-native vector search system for large-$k$ workloads. TEngineDB-V makes vector search a first-class analytical primitive in Tencent's OLAP engine through a global segment-decoupled index materialized as relational tables, eliminating scatter-gather execution, reducing amplification, and enabling native storage optimizations. It decomposes IVFPQ-based search into relational operators, integrates OLAP optimizations, and introduces DPPQ, which combines direction-aware quantization with hierarchical residual refinement to improve recall while preserving relational efficiency. TEngineDB-V further incorporates index-aware query rewriting and a distributed-aware cost model for efficient distributed execution. Experiments show that TEngineDB-V achieves up to a $145\times$ speedup over competitive systems such as StarRocks, and up to a $52\times$ improvement in 10-billion-scale production deployments.

</details>


### [52] [Using Lower-Bound Representations for Trajectory Similarity Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01039)
*Liwei Deng,Haotian Meng,Yupu Zhang,Yan Zhao,Torben Bach Pedersen,Kai Zheng,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出LB-TrajRep，一种统一的轨迹相似性下界表示框架，不依赖深度神经嵌入，通过构建单向量表示来提供多个经典轨迹距离（DTW、Hausdorff距离、DFD）的可容许且可解释的下界，显著提升top-k检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的轨迹相似性方法依赖嵌入训练来近似轨迹距离或排名，但缺乏对原始距离的保证，跨距离度量性能不稳定，且训练成本高昂。作者希望从下界表示的新视角解决这些问题。

Method: 提出LB-TrajRep框架，从一组下界组件构建单向量表示，支持DTW、Hausdorff距离和DFD等多种轨迹距离的可容许下界。具体实例化为点-枢轴（point-pivot）组件，兼容标准向量检索流程。同时提出两种数据驱动的枢轴选择策略：一种显式优化下界紧致性，另一种优先考虑困难近邻轨迹对以提升排序质量。

Result: 在真实轨迹数据集上的大量实验表明，LB-TrajRep在多种距离度量下持续优于最先进的神经轨迹嵌入方法，在Hausdorff距离和DFD上top-k排名准确率提升20%-60%，在DTW上提升15%-40%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，无需深度神经网络嵌入，通过精心设计的基于下界组件的单向量表示即可在轨迹相似性检索中获得更优且更可解释的性能，且具备可容许性保证。

Abstract: Trajectory similarity learning is fundamental to efficient trajectory retrieval under complex distance measures. Existing learning-based methods typically rely on embeddings trained to approximate trajectory distances or rankings, but they often lack guarantees with respect to the original distances, exhibit unstable performance across distance measures, and incur substantial training costs. We revisit trajectory similarity learning from a lower-bound representation perspective and propose LB-TrajRep, a unified lower-bound representation framework independent of deep neural embeddings. This framework constructs single-vector representations from a set of lower-bound components, enabling admissible and interpretable lower bounds for multiple classical trajectory distances, including Dynamic Time Warping (DTW), Hausdorff distance, and Discrete Fréchet Distance (DFD). Within this framework, we instantiate point-pivot components, which naturally support both metric and non-metric distances and remain compatible with standard vector-based retrieval pipelines. To improve ranking quality, we develop two data-driven pivot selection strategies that explicitly optimize lower-bound tightness and prioritize hard near-neighbor trajectory pairs, respectively. Extensive experiments on real-world trajectory datasets show that the proposed lower-bound representations are able to consistently outperform state-of-the-art neural trajectory embeddings across diverse distance measures, improving top-$k$ ranking accuracy by up to 20\%--60\% on the Hausdorff distance and DFD and by 15\%--40\% on DTW.

</details>


### [53] [Join Indices for Search Engines: a Prunable Parallel Semijoin over Lucene Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01173)
*Mikhail Khludnev*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Join-Index的查询时连接方案，将关系型数据库中的join-index技术移植到Lucene的LSM风格段存储中，通过物化序到序的join column避免了可变长度键的转换开销，实现了跨段、高性能、可组合的semijoin算法，在1M产品对10M SKU的基准测试中平均查询延迟降低5.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索引擎中的连接操作是"二等公民"：Lucene现有的查询时连接实现在性能或能力上有限制，迫使使用者在"单一索引内的快速连接"和"跨独立索引的慢速连接"之间做选择。需要一个既能高性能又能跨独立索引的连接方案。

Method: 1) 将Valduriez的join-index技术从关系系统移植到Lucene的flush-based (LSM-style)段存储中，为每对父子段物化append-only的序到序join-index列J[c]=p。2) 在基础上构建semijoin算法，按父段并行计算，无需全局屏障。3) 在三个层面进行剪枝：段级（免费获得）、先验min/max、文档级两阶段确认（含懒累积半读并集），使其能与任意引擎查询组合。

Result: 在Apache Solr查询解析器上实现的原型，针对1M产品连接10M SKU的基准测试中，平均查询延迟降低5.4倍（359.8ms vs 1934.6ms），且优势随负载单调扩大，在8并发时达到8.3倍。在4 vCPU上，基线在1.18 queries/s达到峰值后吞吐量下降，而join index仍在增长，达到8.04 queries/s（基线的6.8倍）。

Conclusion: 将join-index技术适配到Lucene的LSM段存储中，能够实现高性能的跨索引查询时连接，同时通过多层次剪枝策略保持与任意引擎查询的可组合性，显著优于Solr内置的查询时连接方案。

Abstract: Joins are second-class citizens in search engines: existing query-time join implementations in Lucene are limited either in performance or in capability, forcing a choice between fast joins scoped to a single index and slower joins that span independently managed indices. We carry Valduriez's join-index technique from relational systems to Lucene's flush-based (LSM-style) segment storage: for every pair of a parent and a child segment we materialize an append-only, ordinal-to-ordinal join-index column J[c]=p, avoiding any query-time translation of external variable-length keys. On top of this structure we build a semijoin algorithm that is computed per parent segment, in parallel, without a global barrier between stages; it prunes at three levels (segment-level, the first of which comes free from per-segment execution; a-priori min/max; and document-level two-phase confirmation with a lazily accumulated half-read union) so that it composes with arbitrary engine queries instead of wasting computation on matches that a sibling filter would later discard. A prototype implemented as an Apache Solr query parser, benchmarked on 1M products joined against 10M skus, cuts average query latency 5.4 times (359.8,ms vs. 1934.6,ms) relative to Solr's built-in query-time join, and the advantage widens monotonically with load, reaching 8.3 times at a concurrency of eight: on 4 vCPUs the baseline peaks at 1.18 queries/s and then loses throughput, while the join index is still gaining, at 8.04 - 6.8times the baseline's best.

</details>


### [54] [RaG-Tree: Combining R-Tree and HNSW for Multi-Attribute Range Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01255)
*Jiawei Liu,Xiang Zhang,Chao Zhang,Ju Fan,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出RaG-Tree，一种将R-tree与分区感知的HNSW图耦合的统一索引结构，用于解决多属性范围过滤的近似最近邻搜索（MR-ANNS）问题，在三个真实数据集上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MR-ANNS索引方法要么仅利用单一属性进行范围定位，要么沿单个属性递归划分对象，这些方法难以充分利用属性间的相关性来进行有效的范围剪枝，也难以利用属性-向量间的相关性来提升最近邻搜索效率。

Method: RaG-Tree将R树与分区感知的HNSW图耦合：利用R树的分层分区实现有效的范围剪枝，并根据每个分区内的局部向量分布自适应调整HNSW图的稀疏程度，从而实现轻量级索引和高效查询处理。同时提出基于代价的自适应搜索算法以减少不必要的图探索，以及高效的索引维护机制来增量更新受影响的分区感知HNSW图。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，RaG-Tree在查询性能上优于最先进的基线方法，同时实现了轻量级索引和快速的增量更新。

Conclusion: RaG-Tree通过将R树的空间分区能力与HNSW图的局部自适应图结构相结合，有效解决了MR-ANNS中属性相关性和属性-向量相关性的利用问题，在查询效率、索引开销和动态更新方面均取得了优异表现。

Abstract: Multi-attribute range-filtered approximate nearest neighbor search (MR-ANNS), which retrieves high-dimensional vectors satisfying multiple attribute constraints, is a fundamental operation in modern AI applications. Existing MR-ANNS indexes either exploit a single attribute for range localization or recursively partition objects along individual attributes, which may limit their ability to exploit attribute correlations for effective range pruning and attribute-vector correlations for efficient nearest-neighbor search. In this paper, we propose RaG-Tree, a unified index that couples an R-tree with partition-aware HNSW graphs for MR-ANNS. RaG-Tree leverages hierarchical R-tree partitions for effective range pruning and adapts the sparsity of each HNSW graph to the local vector distributions within its partition, enabling lightweight indexing and efficient query processing. To support efficient query processing and dynamic updates, we develop a cost-based adaptive search algorithm that minimizes unnecessary graph exploration, together with an efficient index maintenance mechanism for incrementally updating affected partition-aware HNSW graphs. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets show that RaG-Tree achieves superior query performance over state-of-the-art baselines, while also providing lightweight indexing and fast incremental updates.

</details>


### [55] [Six Dimensions of Benchmarking Time-Series Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01459)
*Jalal Mostafa,Sandro Melissano,Nicholas Tan Jerome,Suren Chilingaryan,Andreas Kopmann*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出SciTSv2，一个从六个工作负载维度（连接并行度、批量写入、时间序列规律性、多变量序列、混合工作负载和系统指标）系统评估时间序列数据库的基准测试框架，并利用该框架对四种不同存储引擎的TSDB进行了深入分析，揭示了传统基准测试难以发现的架构级性能特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据库采用不同的存储架构，分别针对特定工作负载特征进行优化，导致它们在不同场景下表现出截然不同的性能瓶颈。传统的单一维度基准测试方法无法全面揭示这些差异，系统架构师在选择数据后端时需要了解哪些存储引擎对其特定管线高效、哪些存在未来可扩展性风险。

Method: SciTSv2框架从六个维度评估时间序列数据库：（1）连接并行度（2）批量写入（3）时间序列规律性（4）多变量序列（5）混合工作负载（6）系统指标。论文选取四种代表性TSDB：InfluxDB（时间结构合并树）、TimescaleDB（关系数据库）、ClickHouse（列式存储）和DataLayerTS（专为规律时间序列优化），进行系统性评估。

Result: 研究发现每个测试维度都能揭示单一轴基准测试所掩盖的架构行为，包括：规律性相关的性能权衡、并发读写之间的争用，以及不同的CPU、I/O和磁盘带宽受限瓶颈。结合细粒度的系统指标，可以定位性能结果背后的架构成因。

Conclusion: SciTSv2为系统架构师提供了一种诊断工具，能够将性能结果追溯到底层架构原因，使存储引擎的选择基于经验性的、工作负载特定的实证证据，而非盲目依赖传统基准测试。

Abstract: Time-series databases (TSDBs) employ diverse storage architectures optimized for specific workload characteristics, leading to distinct performance profiles and bottlenecks that are often not apparent under conventional benchmarking approaches. System architects designing robust data backends must understand which storage engines are efficient for their particular pipelines and which exhibit the lowest risk of encountering future scalability constraints. This paper presents SciTSv2, a benchmarking framework that evaluates time-series databases across six workload dimensions: connection parallelism, batch ingestion, time-series regularity, multi-variate series, mixed workloads, and system metrics. We exploit SciTSv2 to systematically evaluate 4 TSDBs representing distinct storage engines: InfluxDB (Time-Structured Merge tree), TimescaleDB (based on relational databases), ClickHouse (columnar), and DataLayerTS (specialized in regular time-series). We show that each dimension surfaces architectural behavior that dedicated, single-axis benchmarks obscure, including regularity-dependent trade-offs, contention between concurrent reads and writes, and distinct CPU, I/O, and disk-bandwidth-bound bottlenecks. Paired with fine-grained system metrics, SciTSv2 gives architects a diagnostic tool for tracing performance outcomes back to their underlying architectural causes, supporting storage engine selections grounded in empirical, workload-specific evidence.

</details>


### [56] [Multimodal Embeddings for 3D Similarity Search in Semantic Web-of-Things Digital-Twin Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01852)
*Oussama Zaid,Romaric Gaudel,Hassan Thomas,Maria Massri,Philippe Raipin-Parv{é}dy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，将多模态嵌入层扩展到语义Web of Things（SWoT）平台，使本体类型化的3D点云、时间属性和语义标签实体被编码为潜在向量表示，与知识图谱共存，从而支持结合图过滤和相似性搜索的混合本体-向量查询，并在Orange Research的Thing'in平台上进行了可行性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWoT平台虽然能用知识图谱表达结构化和逻辑查询，但缺乏超越严格本体等价性的相似性表达机制。对于电信基础设施和工业物联网等领域的3D数字孪生，需要同时结合本体约束和异构场景数据的多模态相似性搜索，这一能力缺口是关键的。

Method: 提出一个框架，在SWoT平台上增加多模态嵌入层：将包含3D点云、时间属性和语义标签的本体类型化实体编码为潜在向量表示，与知识图谱一同存储，从而启用将图过滤与相似性搜索相结合的混合本体-向量查询。该框架基于Orange Research的Thing'in平台和Clock-G时间图数据库实现。

Result: 在S3DIS数据集上的可行性评估表明：图过滤在时间和关系约束下能有效缩小搜索范围；通用预训练编码器产生的表示足以用于相似性检索，并且可作为下游预测任务的初步编码步骤。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了在SWoT平台中集成多模态嵌入层的可行性，证明通用预训练编码器能够支撑相似性检索和预测任务，为3D数字孪生场景提供了混合查询能力的基础。

Abstract: Semantic Web of Things (SWoT) platforms model physical infrastructure as knowledge graphs typed against domain ontologies, enabling expressive structural and logical queries. However, they lack native mechanisms to express similarity beyond strict ontological equivalence, which represents a critical gap for 3D digital twins in domains such as telecom infrastructure and industrial IoT, where queries must combine ontological constraints with multimodal similarity search over heterogeneous, temporally-evolving scene data. We propose a framework that extends SWoT platforms with a multimodal embedding layer: ontology-typed entities comprising 3D point clouds, temporal attributes, and semantic labels are encoded into latent vector representations stored alongside the knowledge graph, enabling hybrid ontology-vector queries that combine graph-based filtering with similarity search. Implemented on Orange Research's Thing'in platform with the Clock-G temporal graph database, a feasibility evaluation on S3DIS demonstrates that graph filtering effectively restricts the search pool under temporal and relational constraints, and that general-purpose pretrained encoders produce representations sufficient for similarity retrieval and as a preliminary encoding step for downstream predictive tasks.

</details>

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对包含依赖（Inclusion Dependency）发现算法，提出了实现层面的优化技术。对经典算法Spider采用高效并行化技术（同时提升速度并降低内存消耗），对先进近似算法Faida应用四种优化（数据缓冲、SIMD执行、哈希表选择和并行化），并在开源C++数据剖析工具Desbordante中实现，相比Metanome实现了Spider高达5倍、Faida高达8倍的运行时间提升。

Method: 针对Spider算法提出高效并行化技术，在大幅加速的同时降低内存消耗；针对Faida算法应用四种优化技术：数据缓冲（data buffering）、SIMD指令集执行、哈希表精细选择以及并行化。所有算法在开源C++数据剖析器Desbordante中实现。

Result: 在Desbordante中实现了两种算法并评估多种选项。相较于Metanome（基于Java的数据剖析器），Spider实现了最高5倍的运行时间降低，Faida实现了最高8倍的运行时间降低，所有优化技术均验证有效。

Abstract: Inclusion dependency is a relation between attributes of tables that indicates possible Primary Key-Foreign Key references. Automatic discovery of inclusion dependencies is a relevant problem for both academic and industrial communities. The core concern for this problem is the efficiency of discovery process, since it is a computationally expensive task. However, existing studies only address the algorithmic side, while leaving out the implementation aspect. At the same time, engineering details are at least as important as the algorithmic ones for achieving good performance.
  In this paper, we describe techniques for efficient implementation of two algorithms for discovery of inclusion dependencies - Spider and Faida. The first one is a classic algorithm whose ideas lie in the foundation of many other inclusion dependency discovery algorithms. We propose an efficient parallelization technique, which greatly speeds up the algorithm while simultaneously reducing its memory consumption. The second one is the state-of-the-art approximate algorithm, which we approach by applying four types of optimizations: data buffering, SIMD-enabled execution, careful hash-table selection and parallelization.
  In order to experimentally evaluate our techniques, we have implemented these algorithms in Desbordante - an open-source science-intensive data profiler written in C++. For Spider, we have evaluated several different options, and in case of Faida we have demonstrated that all our optimization techniques yield results. We also compared our implementations with Metanome - a Java-based data profiler. Overall, we report up to 5x improvement in terms of run time reduction for Spider and up to 8x for Faida.

</details>


### [58] [Oasis: Hiding the Cost of Querying Parquet Files in the Datapath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02268)
*Jonas Dann,Luca Tagliavini,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Oasis是一种数据处理的SmartNIC，将Parquet解码卸载到网络数据路径中的自定义硬件加速器上，与DuckDB端到端集成，在最佳情况下几乎将DuckDB查询吞吐量翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生数据库系统通过分解计算和存储资源来提高成本效率，但生产数据仓库工作负载中扫描（含存储往返）约占查询运行时的一半。随着存储和网络带宽超越CPU性价比，CPU用于解码的周期日益削弱云的成本效率优势，因此需要在网络路径中实现硬件加速以消除这一瓶颈。

Method: Oasis将Parquet解码卸载到SmartNIC中的自定义硬件加速器上，包含三个关键组件：硬件解码器架构、软件抽象层以及与DuckDB的端到端集成。解码过程与网络数据路径重叠，从而隐藏解码成本。

Result: Oasis以最小开销将Parquet解码成本隐藏在网络数据路径之后，使扫描与查询执行的其余部分重叠，在最佳情况下几乎使DuckDB查询吞吐量翻倍。

Conclusion: 通过将Parquet解码卸载到SmartNIC硬件加速器，Oasis验证了在网络数据路径中实现数据解码专业化的可行性和显著性能收益，为云原生数据库系统的硬件加速提供了新方向。

Abstract: Cloud-native database systems disaggregate compute and storage resources to improve cost efficiency over traditional monolithic architectures through elasticity and resource pooling. Studies of production data warehouse workloads show that scans (including round trips to storage) account for roughly half of total query runtime. Data lakes and lakehouses amplify this bottleneck through per-query decoding of storage-optimized, compressed file formats such as Parquet. As storage and network bandwidth continue to outpace CPU cost-performance, the CPU cycles spent on decoding increasingly undermine the cloud's cost-efficiency promise. This has led to a wave of specialization across the stack with custom hardware at cloud-vendor scale at the extreme end. We build on this trend and present Oasis, a data-processing SmartNIC that offloads Parquet decoding into the network datapath as a custom hardware accelerator. Oasis features a hardware decoder architecture, software abstraction layer, and end-to-end integration with DuckDB. Our evaluation shows that Oasis hides the cost of Parquet decoding behind the network datapath with minimal overhead, overlapping the scan with the remainder of the query execution. In the best case, this almost doubles DuckDB query throughput.

</details>


### [59] [FastGFDs: Efficient Validation of Graph Functional Dependencies with Desbordante](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02321)
*Anton Chernikov,Yurii Litvinov,Kirill Smirnov,George Chernishev*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出FastGFDs算法，一种面向消费级PC的图函数依赖（GFD）验证算法。相比原有面向高性能服务器集群的并行方案，该算法采用顺序执行方式，结合Core-First Decomposition和Compact Path Index（CPI）技术，在真实图数据上实现了最高3倍（平均2.6倍）的性能提升，并将内存消耗降低了5倍。同时提供了首个公开可用的GFD验证开源实现（集成于Desbordante数据剖析工具中）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFD验证是计算代价极高的问题，其中子图定位占约99%的运行时间。原有算法专为高性能服务器集群设计，不适合普通消费级PC。作者希望将GFD验证能力推广到更广泛的用户群体，使其能在低端单节点环境（如个人电脑）上运行。

Method: 提出FastGFDs算法：1）采用顺序执行方式，在整张图上操作而非并行划分；2）核心创新在于使用Core-First Decomposition（核心优先分解）和Compact Path Index（CPI）两种技术，并引入近期发展的图匹配方法；3）与朴素顺序算法和原有并行方案进行对比，评估运行时间和内存消耗。

Result: 在真实图数据上的实验表明：相比原有并行方案，性能最高提升3倍，平均提升2.6倍；采用新的子图匹配算法使内存消耗降低5倍。此外，提供了Desbordante中首个公开可用的GFD验证开源实现。

Conclusion: 该研究是设计低端单节点环境下高效GFD验证算法的第一步，证明了通过精心设计的顺序算法和图匹配技术，可以在消费级硬件上实现优于高性能集群并行方案的性能。研究为GFD验证的普及化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Graph functional dependencies (GFD) are a recently-developed concept aimed at capturing both topological structures in graphs and functional dependencies between attributes. The process of verifying whether a given GFD holds over a particular graph is referred to as GFD validation. In this very computationally expensive problem, locating suitable subgraphs accounts for about 99% of the total run time. The concept's authors originally proposed a parallel scheme (algorithm), targeting specifically clusters of high-performance servers.
  The goal of this study is to open GFD validation to a broader public by making it possible to run it on a consumer class PC. Our initial experiments demonstrated that the existing algorithm may not be optimal for these purposes. Therefore, we propose FastGFDs - a GFD validation algorithm that employs a recently developed graph matching technique. In contrast to the parallel scheme, it is sequential and operates on the entire graph. Its novelty lies in the use of Core-First Decomposition and the Compact Path Index (CPI). We compare it with the naive sequential algorithm and the parallel scheme, evaluating run times and memory consumption. The current study is the first step towards designing an efficient algorithm for GFD validation in low-end single-node environments.
  We also provide an open-source implementation of GFD validation over large data graphs. To the best of our knowledge, this is the only publicly available implementation of an algorithm for this problem. It is developed in Desbordante - an open-source high-performance data profiler aimed at science-intensive tasks.
  Finally, our experiments on a real-life graph demonstrated up to three times performance (2.6x on average) improvement over the parallel scheme. Employing the new subgraph matching algorithm also reduced memory consumption by five times.

</details>


### [60] [Token-Native Storage: Read and Write in your Agent's Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02376)
*Kumar Shivendu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出"token-native存储"概念：将文本直接以BPE token ID形式存储，而非传统的UTF-8字节格式。该方法在紧凑性（压缩比最高3.30x）和读取速度（10-600x加速）上均优于现有方案，并呼吁AI社区标准化共享词表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着嵌入模型、重排序器和语言模型智能体成为文本的主要读写方，当前系统仍以UTF-8存储文本（为人类设计），每次访问都需在字符和token ID之间转换，造成不必要的开销。作者认为存储格式应适应模型的读取方式而非人类。

Method: 1) 将BPE token ID打包存储（如r50k ID用uint16），并与UTF-8及各种字节压缩编码器（含zstd字典）对比；2) 提出按频率而非合并顺序重新编号token，使streamvbyte等整数编码器恢复熵编码器的大部分压缩比且解码更快；3) 论证token-native存储直接传递token ID，避免重复分词。

Result: uint16打包在英文上压缩比UTF-8高2.25x，熵编码器达3.30x；在6种分词器、3种语料（英文/代码/印地语）上均匹敌或超越字节编码器；频率重排使streamvbyte恢复大部分压缩比且解码快~7x；token-native访问比重新分词快10-600x。

Conclusion: token-native存储更小更快，是未来的方向。主要障碍是跨模型词表不统一，因此作者呼吁AI实验室发布共享标准化词表，类似ASCII和UTF-8对文本的标准化作用。

Abstract: Search and database engines still store text as UTF-8, a format built for humans. But the systems that increasingly read and write that text (embedders, rerankers, and language-model agents) work in token IDs, not characters, so every access pays to translate between the two. As agents become the primary readers and writers of stored text, we argue for token-native storage: keep the text as the model's own byte-pair-encoding (BPE) token IDs. This is both smaller and faster. Packing r50k IDs as uint16 already beats UTF-8 by 2.25x on English with no compression, and an entropy coder reaches 3.30x. Across six tokenizers and three corpora (English, code, Hindi), compressing token IDs matches or beats every byte codec, even a corpus-trained zstd dictionary. Two findings sharpen the case. BPE numbers tokens by merge order, not frequency, and re-ranking by frequency lets a plain integer codec (streamvbyte) recover most of the entropy coder's ratio while decoding ~7x faster, a one-line change we ask AI labs to make when they publish vocabularies. And because a model reads token IDs, not text, a token-native store hands them over directly instead of re-tokenizing on every read, ~10-600x faster. The only barrier is that sharing token IDs requires a common tokenizer, which is not always true across model families yet, so we argue for standardization: a published, shared vocabulary, the way ASCII and UTF-8 standardized text.

</details>

### [61] [Pure-Strategy Equilibrium in the Generalized First-Price Auction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00334)
*Michael Ostrovsky,Andrzej Skrzypacz*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了广义一价拍卖（GFP）中纯策略纳什均衡是否存在这一经典问题。研究发现，当广告排名基于随机质量分数与出价的乘积（而非仅基于出价或出价与确定性质量分数的乘积）时，原有的结论可能发生逆转。此外，GFP在纯策略均衡下的预期收入可能显著超过广义二价拍卖（GSP），但在某些条件下这种关系也可能反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义一价拍卖（GFP）是赞助搜索广告中重要的拍卖机制，但其纯策略纳什均衡的存在性问题一直是经典研究课题。以往研究基于确定性质量分数或仅基于出价进行排名，得到的结论较为悲观。本文旨在探讨引入随机质量分数排名后，GFP均衡存在性及收入表现是否会发生根本性变化，从而为该拍卖机制在实际应用中的价值提供新的理论依据。

Method: 本文通过理论分析的方法，重新考察GFP拍卖中纯策略纳什均衡的存在性。具体而言，作者将广告排名规则从"仅基于出价"或"基于出价与确定性质量分数乘积"推广到"基于出价与随机质量分数乘积"，并在此框架下分析均衡的存在条件以及均衡时的预期收入，与GSP拍卖进行系统性比较。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 当排名基于随机质量分数与出价的乘积时，GFP中纯策略纳什均衡的存在性结论与此前经典结果相反，即原本不存在均衡的场景下可能出现纯策略均衡；(2) 在纯策略均衡下，GFP的预期收入通常可能显著高于GSP，但在某些特定条件下，GSP的收入反而可能更高。

Conclusion: 引入随机质量分数可以改变GFP拍卖的理论性质，使其在均衡存在性和收入表现上展现出与以往认知不同的特征。这一发现为GFP拍卖机制的应用前景提供了新的理论支持，同时也提示在实际设计中需要根据具体条件谨慎比较GFP与GSP的收入表现。

Abstract: We revisit the classic result on the (non-)existence of pure-strategy Nash equilibria in the Generalized First-Price Auction for sponsored search advertising and show that the conclusion may be reversed when ads are ranked based on the product of stochastic quality scores and bid amounts, rather than solely on the bids or on the product of bids and deterministic quality scores. Moreover, the expected revenue in the pure strategy equilibrium of the Generalized First-Price Auction may substantially exceed that of the Generalized Second-Price Auction, although under some conditions the relation may also be reversed.

</details>


### [62] [Understanding Federated Learning Through the Lens of Mechanism Design: The Role of Data Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00364)
*Lina Alkarmi,Po-Yen Chen,Mingyan Liu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习中的激励机制，对比了Shapley值机制(M^Shap)和外部性机制(M^E)在社交最优性、个体理性和公平/互惠性三个维度上的表现，发现M^Shap无法最大化社会福利而M^E可以，但M^E牺牲了互惠公平性，实证验证了两者之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习需要有效的激励机制来鼓励数据共享并防止搭便车行为。现有的Shapley值机制虽能保证互惠公平，但在现实世界中存在独立选项（如使用自己的数据独立训练）的情况下，其对社交最优性和个体理性的完整分析仍然缺失。

Method: 将经典的外部性机制M^E适配到联邦学习场景，并与Shapley值机制M^Shap在三个维度（社交最优性、个体理性、公平/互惠性）上进行系统对比分析，结合理论推导和实证模拟。

Result: 1) M^Shap的边际激励导致过贡献资源，不能最大化社会福利，而M^E设计上最大化社会福利；2) 在同质场景中两种机制都保证个体理性，但在异质场景下M^E在温和条件下仍能保证，而M^Shap不能；3) M^Shap设计上保持完美互惠公平，M^E则通过牺牲个体公平来最大化集体福利，仅在对称均衡下匹配Shapley贡献。

Conclusion: M^E在社交效率上优于M^Shap，但代价是牺牲互惠公平性；反过来M^Shap保证了公平但在异质性和独立选项现实条件下无法保证个体理性和社交最优性。两者之间本质上存在公平性与社交效率的权衡。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) requires effective incentive mechanisms to motivate data sharing and prevent strategic free-riding. Recent FL mechanisms such as the Shapley value mechanism M^Shap guarantee reciprocal fairness for agents. However, a complete analysis of how such mechanisms impact social optimality and individual rationality under realistic, standalone outside options remains unknown. In this paper, we address this gap by adapting the classical Externality mechanism M^E to the federated learning setting. We conduct a comparison of M^Shap and M^E across three dimensions: social optimality, individual rationality, and fairness/reciprocity. First, we establish that M^Shap generally does not maximize social welfare because its marginal incentives drive agents to over-contribute resources, while M^E maximizes social welfare by design. Second, we evaluate participation incentives through the individual rationality gap when considering agents' outside options as standalone training on their own data. We find that both mechanisms ensure individual rationality in homogeneous settings. We further show that under mild conditions, M^E maintains this guarantee under agent heterogeneity, whereas M^Shap does not. Third, we demonstrate that while M^Shap maintains perfect reciprocity by design, M^E generally does not, and only ensures that individual benefits match Shapley contributions at symmetric equilibria under homogeneity, as it sacrifices individual fairness to maximize collective welfare under heterogeneity. Empirical simulations validate our theoretical findings and illustrate a tradeoff between reciprocal fairness and social efficiency.

</details>


### [63] [Support Contraction for Well-Supported Nash Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00453)
*Zhengyang Liu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"支持收缩"(support contraction)的结构化方法，用于计算双矩阵博弈中的良支持纳什均衡(WSNE)。该方法在公共动作矩形上交替保留行支付矩阵的最大最小策略支持和列支付矩阵的最大最小策略支持。在稳定矩形处，通过两个最小最大策略的交叉得到保留子博弈的精确纳什均衡。该方法给出确定性多项式时间算法计算每个有理双矩阵博弈的1/2-WSNE，以及确定性O(ε⁻²log²n)比特的双方协议计算(1/2+ε)-WSNE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算良支持纳什均衡(WSNE)是博弈论与算法博弈论中的核心问题。已有的算法在近似精度与复杂度之间存在权衡，例如ε⁻⁴的查询复杂度依赖。作者希望设计一种结构性方法，能够以更优的复杂度保证计算WSNE，同时提供清晰的理论框架。

Method: 支持收缩过程：在公共动作矩形上，交替保留行支付矩阵的行最大最小策略支持与列支付矩阵的列最大最小策略支持。每次限制都保持选中该限制的矩阵博弈的值，且只能增加另一个值。在稳定矩形处，两个最大化策略的全支持与互补松弛条件使所有幸存动作变得紧（tight）。然后交叉两个最小最大策略，得到保留子博弈的精确纳什均衡，同时两个零和值界定对已删除动作的偏离。

Result: 1. 确定性多项式时间算法：计算所有支付在[0,1]的有理双矩阵博弈的1/2-WSNE，无附加松弛。2. 确定性O(ε⁻²log²n)比特的双方通信协议：计算(1/2+ε)-WSNE。3. 结合随机化单侧定位，得到O(ε⁻²n log n)支付查询算法，将原有ε⁻⁴依赖改进为ε⁻²。

Conclusion: 支持收缩提供了一种统一且高效的结构性框架，同时改善了时间复杂度和通信复杂度，将WSNE计算的查询复杂度依赖从ε⁻⁴降低到ε⁻²。该方法为双矩阵博弈中计算近似良支持纳什均衡提供了新的理论工具和算法范式。

Abstract: We introduce the support contraction, a structural procedure for computing well-supported Nash equilibria (WSNE) in bimatrix games. On a common action rectangle, the procedure alternately retains the support of a row maximin strategy for the row-payoff matrix and the support of a column maximin strategy for the column-payoff matrix. Each restriction preserves the value of the matrix game that selected it and can only increase the other value. At a stable rectangle, full support of the two maximizing strategies and complementary slackness make every surviving action tight. Crossing the two minimax strategies then gives an exact Nash equilibrium of the retained subgame, while the two zero-sum values bound deviations to deleted actions.
  Support contraction gives a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm that computes a $1/2$-WSNE of every rational bimatrix game with payoffs in $[0,1]$, with no additive slack, and a deterministic $O(ε^{-2}\log^2 n)$-bit two-party protocol for a $(1/2+ε)$-WSNE. The same support-contraction certificate, preceded by randomized one-sided localization, gives an $O(ε^{-2}n\log n)$ payoff-query algorithm for the same guarantee, improving the $ε^{-4}$ dependence to $ε^{-2}$.

</details>


### [64] [Order Auctions with Private Position Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00786)
*Ruijie Wang,Aviv Yaish*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究双位置拍卖中单位需求投标者的效率问题，发现标准一价拍卖无法在事后层面实现有效配置，其分布无关均衡福利保证为1/2；引入偏好表达位后可提升至1−1/e，并探讨了在优先服务拍卖和区块链交易排序中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拍卖理论通常假设竞价者对物品有同质偏好，但现实中投标者对位置（如广告位、排序位置）存在异质偏好——专家型仅看重第一位置，通才型则对两个位置无差异。作者旨在研究这类具有异质顺序偏好的拍卖中，效率配置能否实现及其均衡福利保障如何。

Method: 首先分析标准一价规则（将第一、二物品分配给最高和次高出价者），证明无论支付规则如何，不存在事后执行有效配置的策略组合；随后给出分布无关的均衡福利保证1/2。接着证明确定性单轮拍卖中，离散投标下实现有效配置需要每位投标者比其出价二进制表示多传达至少一个比特。最后提出在winner-pays-bid格式中利用该比特让投标者指定具体物品偏好，将福利保证提升至1−1/e。

Result: 标准一价拍卖在异质偏好下无法事后有效配置，均衡福利保证为1/2；允许投标者表达物品偏好后，该保证提升至1−1/e（约0.632）。

Conclusion: 多分配一个比特的信息表达（物品偏好）能显著提升拍卖机制的效率保证，该结论适用于优先服务拍卖和区块链交易排序等现实场景。

Abstract: We study auctions where two positions are sold to unit-demand bidders with private heterogeneous order preferences: some are specialists who value only the first position, while others are generalists who are indifferent between the two. First, we consider a standard first-price rule which allocates the first and second items to the highest and second-highest bidders, respectively. We show that no strategy profile ex-post implements the efficient allocation at every type profile, irrespective of payments, and provide a distribution-free equilibrium welfare guarantee of $\frac{1}{2}$. To augment this result, we prove that for deterministic one-round auctions and discrete bids, the efficient allocation requires each bidder to communicate at least one bit more than its bid's binary representation. We next ask what the same bit accomplishes in winner-pays-bid formats where bidders can also specify specific item preferences. In particular, we show that this strengthens our distribution-free equilibrium welfare guarantee to $1-\frac{1}{e}$. Finally, we discuss our results' applicability to priority service auctions and blockchain transaction sequencing.

</details>


### [65] [Freemium Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00823)
*Maxim Bichuch,Aviv Yaish*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个生成式AI（GenAI）服务提供商在免费访问与服务质量改进之间的权衡。提供商需要在保持付费用户隐私的同时，利用免费请求产生的数据来提升模型质量，并给出了最优定价与质量策略的闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免费提供GenAI服务需要昂贵的计算资源，但免费请求产生的数据又可以改善未来服务质量。这种数据-成本权衡关系尚未被系统研究，提供商需要理解如何在付费用户隐私保护与免费用户数据利用之间做出最优决策。

Method: 构建了一个服务需求模型，其中用户根据请求价值和隐私偏好存在差异。付费请求保持隐私，免费请求数据用于模型改进，且模型质量若不用数据更新会退化为基线水平。作者刻画了服务需求，证明了在任意平稳策略下质量收敛于唯一稳态，并分析了最优定价与质量策略，证明了最优策略可由两个内生用户价值阈值表达。对于均匀分布的用户价值，推导出闭式最优策略，并刻画了提供免费服务的充分条件与推理成本的关系。

Result: 证明了任意平稳服务策略下质量收敛至唯一稳态；最优定价和质量策略可用两个用户价值阈值表达；对均匀价值分布给出了闭式最优服务策略；提供了提供免费服务的充分条件，该条件取决于推理成本。

Conclusion: GenAI服务的免费提供决策本质上是数据收益与推理成本之间的权衡，最优策略可以通过阈值结构简洁刻画，推理成本是决定是否提供免费服务的关键因素。

Abstract: Free access to a generative AI (GenAI) service requires costly compute, yet can also produce data that improve future service quality. We study a service provider whose users differ in request value and privacy preference, under the constraint that paid requests are kept private, while free requests can be used to improve model quality which otherwise reverts toward a baseline. In particular, we characterize service demand and prove that quality converges to a unique steady state for every stationary service strategy. Next, we analyze optimal pricing and quality policies and show that these can be expressed using two endogenous user value thresholds. For uniform values, we provide a closed-form optimal service strategy and characterize the sufficient conditions for offering free services as dependent on inference cost.

</details>


### [66] [MixedComplementarityProblems.jl: A Fast, Batched, Open-Source Interior Point Solver for Mixed Complementarity Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00959)
*David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MixedComplementarityProblems.jl，一个开源的纯Julia实现的内点法求解器，用于求解参数化混合互补问题（MCPs）。该求解器在标准基准上媲美PATH求解器的可靠性，原生支持批量并行处理（CPU多线程或NVIDIA GPU），并支持对问题参数的高效自动微分。在多智能体换道轨迹博弈中，CPU多线程批量求解比顺序调用PATH快约100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合互补问题（MCPs）在机器人的多智能体轨迹优化中广泛出现，但当前最主流的求解器PATH是闭源的，这限制了其可扩展性、可定制性和批量并行能力。本文旨在提供一个开源、高性能且支持批量并行和自动微分的替代方案。

Method: 采用内点法求解参数化MCPs，通过抽象层设计使单一求解器实现可不加修改地运行在不同线性代数后端上（稠密、批量稀疏和单大规模），原生支持CPU多线程和GPU批处理，并集成了对解的自动微分支持。

Result: 在标准基准上可靠性媲美PATH；在多智能体换道轨迹博弈中，CPU多线程批量求解比顺序调用PATH快约100倍；GPU后端也能远超PATH但未优于CPU多线程，仅在每个实例的KKT系统足够大时GPU才占优，并刻画了这一领域依赖关系。

Conclusion: MixedComplementarityProblems.jl为机器人和优化社区提供了一个开源、高性能、并行友好的MCP求解器，在批量场景下大幅超越PATH，同时具备自动微分能力，为多智能体轨迹优化等问题提供了更灵活和可扩展的工具。

Abstract: Mixed complementarity problems (MCPs) arise as the first-order optimality conditions of nonlinear programs and noncooperative games, and provide a natural formulation for multi-agent trajectory optimization problems that appear throughout robotics. The dominant solver for problems of this form is PATH, which offers strong performance on robotics problems but remains closed-source. We present MixedComplementarityProblems.jl, an open-source, pure Julia implementation of an interior point method for parametric MCPs that: (i) matches PATH's reliability on standard benchmarks, (ii) natively supports batched, parallel processing of many parameter instances, either across CPU threads or on an NVIDIA GPU, and (iii) supports efficient automatic differentiation of solutions with respect to problem parameters. On a multi-agent lane-change trajectory game representative of robotics planning problems, our CPU-multithreaded batched solver clears a batch of parametric instances ~100x faster than sequential calls to PATH. A GPU backend, running the same solver implementation unmodified, also clears these batches far faster than PATH, but does not outperform the multithreaded CPU on this problem; the GPU pulls ahead only once each per-instance KKT system grows large, and we characterize this regime dependence. We describe the solver's interior point formulation, the abstraction that lets a single solver implementation run unmodified across dense, batched-sparse, and single-large linear-algebra backends, and report benchmarks against PATH on both randomly generated quadratic programs and trajectory games.

</details>


### [67] [Improved Last-iterate Convergence Properties for the FLBR-MWU Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01170)
*Michail Fasoulakis,Evangelos Markakis,Giorgos Roussakis,Christodoulos Santorinaios*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了近年前瞻最佳响应乘法权重更新（FLBR-MWU）算法的收敛速度，首次为其对偶间隙建立了几何收敛率O(c^t)，并通过实验证明其性能与最优方法OGDA相当甚至更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLBR-MWU算法此前仅被证明具有渐近最后迭代收敛性，但缺乏显式的收敛速率，这是Fasoulakis等人留下的开放问题。作者希望回答这一问题，以更深入地理解该算法在零和博弈中的理论保证。

Method: 作者通过分析算法的雅可比矩阵的最大特征值等博弈参数，为FLBR-MWU建立对偶间隙的几何收敛率O(c^t)，同时进行实验比较，与OGDA方法在零和博弈中进行性能对比。

Result: 建立了FLBR-MWU的显式几何收敛速率O(c^t)，其中c<1依赖于时间无关的博弈参数；实验表明FLBR-MWU的性能与OGDA相当，某些情况下甚至优于OGDA。

Conclusion: 成功解决了Fasoulakis等人提出的开放问题，证明了FLBR-MWU不仅具有渐近收敛性，还具备几何收敛速率，且在实践中与最优的OGDA方法相比具有竞争力。

Abstract: We revisit a variant of Multiplicative Weights Update (MWU), defined recently by Fasoulakis et al. [AISTATS; 2022], and denoted as Forward Looking Best Response MWU (FLBR-MWU). These dynamics are based on the approach of extra-gradient methods, with the tweak of using different learning rates in the intermediate step and the actual update step. So far, it has been proved that this algorithm attains asymptotic last-iterate convergence but no explicit rate has been known. We answer the open question from Fasoulakis et al. by establishing a concrete convergence rate for the duality gap. In particular, we show a geometric convergence rate, of the form $O(c^t)$, where $c<1$ is independent of time but dependent on game parameters, such as the maximum eigenvalue of the Jacobian matrix. We also complement our theoretical analysis with an experimental comparison to OGDA (Optimistic Gradient Descent-Ascent), which ranks among the best last-iterate methods for solving zero-sum games. We demonstrate that the performance of the FLBR-MWU method matches or, in some cases, outperforms OGDA.

</details>


### [68] [Individual Fairness in Budget Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01228)
*Xiaohui Bei,Felix Brandt,Matthias Greger,Jannik Peters,Erel Segal-Halevi,Warut Suksompong*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究预算聚合问题（将n个个体分布聚合为集体分布），提出了两种个体公平份额保障，证明在ℓ_t（t≥1）度量下这两种保障可与帕累托效率同时满足且可多项式时间计算；但ℓ_1和ℓ_2效用下，帕累托效率、策略证明性与单目标正份额公平性存在不可能性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预算聚合文献中的公平性概念通常不能保证对个体代理人的公平性，作者希望填补这一空白，定义个体层面的公平份额保障，并系统研究其与效率、策略证明性等性质的兼容性。

Method: 定义了两种个体公平份额保障版本，利用ℓ_t度量下的凸优化/算法设计构造满足公平性与帕累托效率的分配规则；通过构造反例证明ℓ_1和ℓ_2效用下的不可能性定理，并针对小参数情形（n,m≥3）设计了满足全部公理的规则。

Result: （1）ℓ_t（t≥1）度量下，两种个体公平份额保障均可与帕累托效率同时满足，且可在多项式时间内计算；（2）ℓ_1效用下，帕累托效率、策略证明性和单目标正份额三者不可同时满足（n,m≥3），小参数情形下提供了满足规则的方案；（3）ℓ_2效用下得到了类似的不可行性结果。

Conclusion: 个体公平份额保障在ℓ_t度量下是可实现的，但在ℓ_1和ℓ_2（即常用的曼哈顿和欧几里得距离）效用下，公平性、效率与策略证明性之间存在根本性的权衡矛盾。

Abstract: We consider the problem of aggregating $n$ individual distributions over $m$ alternatives into a collective distribution, also known as budget aggregation. Existing fairness notions in this literature typically do not guarantee fairness to individual agents. To address this, we define two versions of individual fair share guarantees. We show that when agents' utilities are derived from $\ell_t$ metrics for any $t\geq 1$, both these guarantees can be satisfied along with Pareto efficiency, and the corresponding distributions can be computed in polynomial time. On the other hand, for $\ell_1$ utilities, we prove that Pareto efficiency, strategyproofness, and a very weak fairness notion called single-minded positive share are not always compatible for $n,m \ge 3$. For smaller parameters, we provide rules that satisfy these three axioms. We also establish similar impossibility results for $\ell_2$ utilities.

</details>


### [69] [Governance Outruns Analysis: Incidence and Manipulability of Credential-Gated Reciprocal Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01476)
*Jinming Xing,Charlotte Brian*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文形式化了学术会议中基于凭证的互惠审稿制度（credential-gated reciprocal review），证明了其三个结构性缺陷：阶梯式审稿义务在线性需求下导致非单调的负担分布、可转移的凭证门槛使防灌水配额设计失效、单作者义务加单论文惩罚使制裁风险在作者组合内完全相关。在19,814篇投稿、64,648位作者、75,859条审稿记录的真实数据上验证后，提出三部分修改方案可将负担分布拉平至近似均匀并消除灌水激励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式工具降低了投稿成本但未降低审稿成本，学术会议因此通过投稿上限、按投稿量挂钩的审稿义务、以及以发表记录为门槛的资格限制等治理手段应对。这些规则约束数万名研究者，却在公布前缺乏对其激励性质的分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，对这类制度进行正式分析。

Method: 将这类制度形式化为"凭证门槛互惠审稿"（credential-gated reciprocal review）家族，证明三个结构性定理：(1) 阶梯形义务对线性需求会导致非单调的负担分布；(2) 可转让凭证门槛击败防灌水配额设计，因为门槛赋予可行性且无单作者定价能抵消可行集的跳跃；(3) 单作者义务加单论文惩罚使制裁风险在作者组合内完全相关。随后基于完整的前一轮投稿图（19,814篇投稿、64,648位作者、75,859条实际审稿）进行实证评估，并提出三部分修改方案在同一固定图上验证。

Result: 实证结果显示：该义务覆盖了84.2%的实际需求，81.4%的作者无需承担任何义务，负担最重的作者所承担的审稿劳动量是其产生劳动量的4.45倍，而负担最轻的仅为其0.45倍——表明负担分布极不均衡。提出的三部分修改方案在同一固定图上将负担分布拉平至近似均匀，并消除了灌水激励。

Conclusion: 现有学术会议治理的凭证门槛互惠审稿制度在激励结构上存在根本性缺陷：负担分配不均、可被灌水策略利用、制裁风险过度集中。通过三部分修改（拉平负担至近似均匀并消除灌水激励），可以为这类制度提供更公平、更稳健的设计方案。

Abstract: Generative tools have lowered the cost of producing a submission without lowering the cost of evaluating one, and venues have responded with governance: submission caps, reviewing obligations tied to submission count, and eligibility gates keyed to prior publication. These rules bind tens of thousands of researchers and are typically announced without published analysis of their incentive properties. We formalise the family as credential-gated reciprocal review and prove three structural results: a step-shaped obligation against linear demand yields incidence that is not monotone in consumption; a transferable credential gate defeats padding-resistant quota design, since the gate confers feasibility and no per-author price offsets a jump in the feasible set; and a per-author obligation with a per-paper penalty makes sanction risk perfectly correlated within an author's portfolio. Evaluating against a full prior-cycle submission graph (19,814 submissions, 64,648 authors, 75,859 observed reviews), the obligation covers 84.2% of realised demand, 81.4% of authors owe nothing, and the most burdened agents owe 4.45 times the review labour they generate against 0.45 times for the least. A three-part modification, evaluated on the same fixed graph, flattens incidence to near-uniform and removes the padding incentive.

</details>


### [70] [What Multichoice Values Cannot See: The Information Content of Anonymous Values for Games with Graded Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01527)
*Matthew Fried*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究带分级参与的博弈（multichoice games、带弃权投票、分级特征归因等），精确刻画了所有线性、玩家对称的值/权力指数/重要性度量的可观测性与盲区。核心发现是：匿名值的联合信息只占据多项式维度的分量（相对于m^n维博弈空间），且存在非零的盲空间——三玩家即可隐藏信息，首次出现的不见成分是手性（chirality）而非相关性。审计规避条件为c-⌈c/m⌉≥d+1，意味着三个及以上层级时三人联盟即可对任意基于值的支付方案进行不可见重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分级参与博弈（如multichoice games、投票弃权、分级特征归因）中，已有大量线性、玩家对称的值和权力指数被提出，但缺乏统一的框架来理解这些度量"能看到什么、看不到什么"。作者旨在对所有m和n一次性精确刻画整个匿名值家族的可见信息与盲区，揭示该框架的根本局限。

Method: 利用表示论（对称群与分支法则）：计算单个玩家回报的泛函在匿名值下对其它n-1个玩家置换不变，由分支法则可知此类不变量仅存在于(C^m)^{⊗n}的Specht构成分量(n)和(n-1,1)中。基于此推导盲空间的维度、结构（±1博弈生成）、阶d交互探测的可见性边界，以及审计规避的条件。所有维度与秩的论断均经计算验证。

Result: (1) 所有匿名值的联合信息是维度关于n多项式大小的分量，经典二值理论是m=2的退化情形；(2) m≥3时n=3即出现非零盲空间，首个不可见成分是手性而非相关性（由两个不同的单调弃权-投票规则实现）；(3) n≥4时盲空间由每个含4个profile的±1博弈张成；(4) 阶d交互探测恰好看到第一行外至多d个cell的分划，完全恢复需d=n-⌈n/m⌉；(5) 审计规避条件c-⌈c/m⌉≥d+1，三或更多层级时三人即可规避所有基于值的支付方案；(6) 可见部分是多项式大小的可廉价估计的sketch，而精确计算全支撑值需全部m^n-1次非零查询。

Conclusion: 分级参与博弈中的匿名值族存在根本性的信息盲区，且此盲区比经典二值理论更大（审计更容易被规避）。这为multichoice值、弃权投票权力指数和分级特征归因的应用提供了重要的理论边界警示：盲目依赖匿名线性值可能遗漏关键的"手性"信息，设计支付方案或归因系统时需意识到这些不可见成分的存在。

Abstract: In a cooperative game with graded participation, each of $n$ players acts at one of $m$ ordered levels; multichoice games, voting with abstention, and graded feature attribution all take this form. We determine exactly what the entire family of linear, player-symmetric values, power indices, and importance measures proposed for this setting, present and future, can and cannot see, for all $m$ at once. The mechanism is short: the functional computing any one player's payoff under an anonymous value is invariant under permutations of the other $n-1$ players, and by the branching rule such invariants exist only in the Specht constituents $(n)$ and $(n-1,1)$ of $(\mathbb{C}^m)^{\otimes n}$. Consequences: the joint information of all anonymous values is a component of dimension polynomial in $n$ against an $m^n$-dimensional game space, with the classical binary theory as the $m=2$ shadow. Three players can hide: for $m\ge3$ the blind space is nonzero already at $n=3$, and the first invisible constituent is not a correlation but a chirality, realized by two distinct monotone abstention-voting rules on three voters that every anonymous power index scores identically. For $n\ge4$ the blind space is spanned by $\pm1$ games on four profiles each. Order-$d$ interaction probes see exactly the partitions with at most $d$ cells outside the first row, with full recovery only at $d=n-\lceil n/m\rceil$. Audit evasion gets easier than in the binary theory: a coalition of $c$ players evades every order-$d$ audit iff $c-\lceil c/m\rceil\ge d+1$, so with three or more levels, trios can restructure invisibly to every value-based payment scheme. Algorithmically, the visible part of a game is a polynomial-size, cheaply estimable sketch, while exact computation of any full-support value requires all $m^n-1$ nonzero queries. All dimension and rank claims are verified computationally.

</details>


### [71] [Conjugation invariants determine the metacommutation permutation only up to relabelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01610)
*Matthew Fried*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究Hurwitz四元数上的元交换（metacommutation）置换。Cohn-Kumar和Leite-Machiavelo此前用共轭不变数据（q和迹）计算了这些置换的符号、不动点数和完整循环结构。本文证明这正是此类不变量所能承载的极限：任何共轭不变函数都无法确定π_Q作为标号置换，甚至无法确定单个特定类的像。证明结合了等变恒等式与一个极小显式反例（p=3, q=5），并进一步讨论了PGL₂(F_p)挠子结构和陪集刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前文献已证明某些重要的置换性质（符号、不动点数、循环结构）仅依赖于Q的共轭不变数据。自然的问题是：共轭不变函数究竟能决定多少信息？本文旨在精确划定这类不变量能力的边界，证明其不足以确定置换本身或单个类的像。

Method: 1) 证明等变恒等式π_{uQu⁻¹} = ρ_u π_Q ρ_u⁻¹，揭示元交换置换在单位共轭下的共轭作用结构。2) 构造极小显式反例：(p,q)=(3,5)时，四个素数2+i, 2+j, 2+k, 2-i形成单一单位共轭轨道，因此在任何共轭不变函数下不可区分，但它们诱导四个两两不同的4-循环。3) 利用H/pH ≅ M₂(F_p)的同构构成PGL₂(F_p)挠子的事实，说明不存在规范的射影标号。4) 通过轨道-稳定子定理，将单个目的类π_Q(C)刻画为PGL₂(F_p)/G_C中的一个陪集。5) 附录列出全部16种重构分解，并经两条独立机器验证。

Result: 证明了共轭不变数据（q和迹）确实无法确定元交换置换作为标号置换的信息，也无法确定任何指定类的像。通过(p,q)=(3,5)的显式反例严格验证了这一边界性结论，并给出了PGL₂(F_p)陪集层面的精确刻画。

Conclusion: 共轭不变函数的能力极限已被精确划清：它们能捕获循环结构的形状（如符号、不动点数、循环型），但无法捕获特定类的具体去向。这是此类问题中"不变量信息量"的完整答案，为理解元交换置换的深层结构提供了全新视角。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{H}$ be the Hurwitz quaternions, $p$ an odd prime, and $Q \in \mathcal{H}$ a prime of norm $q \neq p$. Metacommutation $PQ = Q'P'$ induces a permutation $π_Q$ of the $p+1$ left-associate classes of primes of norm $p$. Cohn and Kumar compute its sign and fixed-point count, and Leite and Machiavelo its full cycle structure, by formulas depending only on conjugation-invariant data of $Q$ (namely $q$ and $\mathrm{tr}\,Q$). We prove this is exactly the boundary of what such invariants can carry: no quantity $I(Q)$ invariant under unit conjugation determines $π_Q$ as a labelled permutation of the intrinsic class set, or even the image of a single specified class. The proof combines an equivariance identity $π_{uQu^{-1}} = ρ_u π_Q ρ_u^{-1}$ with a minimal, fully explicit witness at $(p,q) = (3,5)$: the four primes $2+i$, $2+j$, $2+k$, $2-i$ form a single unit-conjugacy orbit, hence agree under every conjugation-invariant function, yet induce four pairwise distinct $4$-cycles of the same four classes. We further observe that isomorphisms $\mathcal{H}/p\mathcal{H} \to M_2(\mathbb{F}_p)$ form a torsor under $\mathrm{PGL}_2(\mathbb{F}_p)$, so no projective labelling of the classes is canonical, and that by orbit-stabilizer the datum of one destination $π_Q(C)$ is exactly a coset $g_Q G_C$ in $\mathrm{PGL}_2(\mathbb{F}_p)/G_C$. All sixteen refactorizations in the witness are listed in the appendix and have been verified by machine along two independent routes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差盈余最大化是机制设计中的核心问题，它衡量机制产生的效用减去代理人信息租金后的剩余价值。在多维机制设计环境中（代理人具有任意非负估值），如何在保证真实性和个体理性的同时最大化残差盈余一直是一个开放问题。此前的已知结果存在缺口：Hartline 和 Roughgarden (2008) 关于燃钱机制的能力在 k-单位拍卖之外的场景尚未解决，Fotakis 等人 (2015) 只给出了依赖于结果空间大小的 O(log|O|) 非紧致保证，而 Ezra 等人 (2025) 的更广泛价值类问题也悬而未决。本文旨在填补这些理论空白，给出最优的、紧致的近似保证。

Method: 作者设计了基于 VCG 机制的通用框架。核心思想是：在保持普遍诚实性（universal truthfulness）和事后个体理性（ex-post IR）的前提下，通过适当的燃钱（money burning）或收费方案来逼近最优社会福利。机制在对社会福利最大化的 VCG 机制可以多项式时间计算的所有环境中都能高效运行（如总替代估值、多单位估值和多种单参数可行性约束）。关键的比率 H_n 来源于调和数结构，通过精巧的证据构造证明了该保证在常数层面是最坏情况最优的——即使放宽到单一物品拍卖、已知 i.i.d. 先验和贝叶斯激励相容的较弱要求下，该常数也不可改进。

Result: 作者给出了一个普遍诚实且事后个体理性的机制，其期望残差盈余至少为最优社会福利的 1/H_n 倍。该近似比在常数层面是最坏情况最优的，即使在最简单的单物品拍卖、已知 i.i.d. 先验和贝叶斯激励相容条件下也不可改进。机制的时间复杂度与 VCG 机制相同（当 VCG 是多项式时间时），覆盖了总替代、多单位估值和多种单参数可行性约束等重要环境类别。

Conclusion: 本文以紧致且最优的因子 H_n 完成了多维环境残差盈余最大化问题的理论研究，解决了两个长期开放的问题，并将先前依赖于结果空间的保证提升为依赖于代理人数的紧致保证，同时强化了诚实性要求。这一结果标志着对燃钱机制能力（power of money burning）的理论理解达到了一个新的完备水平，为后续机制设计研究提供了重要的基准结果。

Abstract: We settle the worst-case approximability of residual-surplus maximization in general multidimensional mechanism-design environments. For $n$ agents with arbitrary nonnegative valuations over a finite outcome space, we give a universally truthful and ex-post individually rational mechanism whose expected residual surplus is at least $W(N)/H_n$, where $W(N)$ is the optimal social welfare and $H_n$ is the $n$-th harmonic number. This guarantee is worst-case optimal, including its constant, even for a single-item auction with a known i.i.d. prior and under the weaker requirement of Bayesian incentive compatibility.
  Our result resolves the welfare-approximation aspect of the open question of [Hartline and Roughgarden 2008] on the power of money burning beyond $k$-unit auctions, as well as an open question of [Ezra et al. 2025] concerning optimal guarantees for broader valuation classes. It also replaces the outcome-dependent $O(\log|\mathcal{O}|)$ guarantee of [Fotakis et al. 2015] by the tight agent-dependent factor $H_n$, while strengthening truthfulness in expectation to universal truthfulness.
  The mechanism is polynomial-time whenever welfare-maximizing VCG is polynomial-time, yielding efficient mechanisms for gross-substitutes and multi-unit valuations and for several natural single-parameter feasibility constraints.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的安全博弈场景中，攻击者的攻击强度、数量和类型会随时间变化，而现有研究大多假设这些参数是静态的。需要设计能够适应时变环境的在线学习算法，以在动态安全场景中实现无遗憾决策。

Method: 1) 扩展安全博弈：允许攻击者同时选择多个目标，并在乐观平局规则下推导出精确的混合整数线性规划(MILP)预言机。2) 全信息反馈：将预言机与Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader(FTPL)算法集成，处理非预期序列中时变的追随者数量、攻击强度和攻击者类型。3) Bandit反馈：针对多个追随者共享固定攻击者类型的场景，使用重心跨度(barycentric-spanner)构造来从聚合攻击观测中重建效用估计。

Result: 在全信息反馈下实现期望O(√T)遗憾；在bandit反馈下实现期望O(T^(2/3))遗憾。大量仿真实验验证了该方法在完整和部分信息反馈下的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将在线学习框架成功扩展到具有时变攻击强度的多目标Stackelberg安全博弈中，在全信息和bandit反馈两种设定下均提供了理论遗憾保证，并通过仿真验证了实际有效性，为动态安全场景中的防御决策提供了理论基础和可行算法。

Abstract: This work studies no-regret online learning in Repeated Stackelberg Security Games with time-varying attack intensities. We formulate an extended security game in which an attacker may select multiple targets and derive an exact mixed-integer linear programming oracle under a optimistic tie-breaking rule. Under full-information feedback, the oracle is integrated with Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader and yields expected $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret against non-anticipating sequences with time-varying follower numbers, attack intensities, and attacker types. Under bandit feedback, we consider multiple followers sharing a fixed attacker type and use a barycentric-spanner construction to reconstruct utility estimates from aggregate attack observations, obtaining expected $\mathcal{O}(T^{2/3})$ regret. Extensive simulations demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our approach under full and partial information feedback.

</details>
