<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年研究表明多种事前比例保证可以与强事后代表公理同时实现，但尚不清楚事前弱帕累托最优等基本性质是否能与事后正当代表兼容，需要系统性地厘清分数代表与两全其美可实现性之间的边界。

Method: 通过构造不相容性证明（弱PO与事后JR的不兼容、AJR与事后JR的不兼容），并证明分数事前层面的多种代表保证可以同时满足，从而系统分离"分数代表"与"两全其美可实现性"两类性质。

Result: 证明了事前弱帕累托最优与事后JR不相容（由此分数核心也与事后JR不相容）；事前AJR与事后JR不相容；但在分数事前层面，分数核心、群体资源比例、AJR（及AJR+）和帕累托最优可同时满足。

Conclusion: 分数公平委员会即使无法通过与JR委员会的随机化来实现，其事前层面本身仍可高度公平；研究揭示了公平随机委员会选择的基本局限性，并将分数代表与两全其美可实现性作出了清晰分离。

Abstract: We study the best-of-both-worlds fairness in approval-based multi-winner elections, asking whether ex-ante guarantees for a fractional outcome can be implemented while every realized committee satisfies an ex-post representation axiom. Recent work has shown that several ex-ante proportionality guarantees can be achieved together with strong ex-post representation axioms. We first prove that ex-ante weak Pareto optimality (weak PO), which requires that no other fractional outcome makes every voter strictly better off, is incompatible with ex-post justified representation (JR). Since fractional core stability implies weak PO, this also rules out the possibility of combining ex-ante fractional core, a central fairness notion for fractional committees, with ex-post JR. We further show that ex-ante AJR is incompatible with ex-post JR, even though AJR is a much stronger average-representation analogue of JR. On the positive side, we show that the fractional ex-ante side itself remains highly compatible: several natural ex-ante representation guarantees can be satisfied simultaneously, including fractional core, group-resource proportionality, AJR (as well as its strict strengthening AJR+), and Pareto-optimality. Hence, highly fair fractional committees may exist even when they cannot be implemented by randomization over JR committees. Our results separate fractional representation from best-of-both-worlds implementability and identify fundamental limitations of fair randomized committee selection.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出"弥散分析"（diffuse analysis）框架，解释自动出价均衡在最坏情况下是PPAD完全的、但在实践中却快速收敛的矛盾。当竞标者价值分布是非原子的（符合现实市场），自动出价均衡成为分离单调的广义纳什均衡（GNE），作者给出了首个具有最后迭代线性收敛的求解器，证明均衡具有多项式弥散复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动出价均衡的计算在最坏情况下是PPAD完全的，但实际市场中广告商运行简单的去中心化学习策略却能快速收敛，这之间存在明显矛盾。作者希望弥合理论最坏情况困难性与实践快速收敛之间的鸿沟。

Method: 引入"弥散分析"这一超越最坏情况的框架，研究竞标者价值来自一般非原子分布时的均衡计算。在该框架下，自动出价均衡被转化为分离单调的广义纳什均衡（GNE），并为其设计了具有最后迭代线性收敛性质的求解器。该框架将预算节奏（budget pacing）和节流（throttling）均衡作为特殊情形涵盖，支付规则为第一价和第二价的凸组合。

Result: 证明了最坏情况下的硬度依赖于原子性（atomicity），当价值分布为非原子时硬度消失。给出了首个针对该GNE的具有最后迭代线性收敛的求解器，表明均衡具有多项式弥散复杂度，与现实市场的收敛行为一致。

Conclusion: 通过弥散分析框架，成功解释了理论最坏情况困难性与实际快速收敛之间的不一致，为自动出价均衡计算提供了超越最坏情况的理论保证，并统一了预算节奏与节流均衡等特殊情况。

Abstract: Computing certain autobidding equilibria is PPAD complete in the worst case. Yet such instances rarely arise in practice, where advertisers running simple, decentralized learning strategies usually converge quickly. We show there is no contradiction: the hardness requires atomicity and vanishes once the value distribution is nonatomic, as it is in real world markets.
  To bridge worst case hardness and practical convergence, we introduce diffuse analysis, a beyond worst case framework that studies equilibrium computation when bidder values are drawn from general nonatomic distributions. Under this framework, the autobidding equilibrium becomes a separately monotone generalized Nash equilibrium (GNE). For this GNE, we give the first solver with last iterate linear convergence. Thus, the equilibrium has polynomial diffuse complexity, matching the convergence observed in real-world markets.
  Concretely, our framework subsumes the budget pacing and the throttling equilibrium as special cases when the payment rule is a convex combination of first and second price.

</details>
