TL;DR: 本文在Monderer和Shapley的精确势博弈理论基础上，研究了当对称性条件（玩家交叉差分两两一致）不成立时的情况。作者提出了MS-势，它由代表博弈共同利益元素的二阶差分构造而成，在精确势博弈类上可恢复势函数（至多差可分离收益项），并通过最小二乘方法推广到所有有限博弈，得到MS-分解：每个有限博弈可分解为共同利益MS-势博弈和吸收个人主义收益的残差部分。核心结果为：当所有玩家具有相同数量的行动时，MS-势的一个增广形式与Candogan等（2011）的势函数在加法常数意义下一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确势博弈的势函数依赖于玩家激励之间严格的对称性条件（交叉差分两两一致），但现实中许多博弈并不满足这一对称性。作者希望探究当对称性失败时，能否从这些特征出发构建有意义的结构，以刻画博弈中的共同利益元素。

Method: 论文从二阶差分中构造MS-势，该势在可分离收益项意义下唯一，其存在性由高阶MS-条件刻画。在精确势博弈类上，MS-势恢复出除掉个体主效应后的势函数。随后利用最小二乘构造将MS-势扩展到所有有限博弈，从而产生MS-分解，将博弈分解为共同利益势博弈与个人主义残差两部分。

Result: 核心结果为一条恒等式：当所有玩家行动数相同时，MS-势的某种增广形式与Candogan、Menache、Ozdaglar和Parrilo（2011）的势函数在加法常数意义下完全一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为对称性失败情形下的博弈结构分析提供了统一框架，建立了MS-势与既有势分解理论之间的等价联系，扩展了势博弈方法论的适用范围。

Abstract: Monderer and Shapley (1996) showed that a game is an exact potential game exactly when the players' cross-differences agree pair by pair, a symmetry condition on how any two players' incentives interlock. This paper asks what can be built from these characteristics when symmetry fails. The resulting MS-potential is constructed from the second differences that represent the game's common-interest elements. The MS-potential is unique up to separable payoff terms and it exists precisely when a higher-order MS-condition holds. On the class of exact potential games it recovers the potential up to the players' individualistic main effects. A least-squares construction subsequently extends the MS-potential to the class of all finite games. The construction induces the MS-decomposition: every finite game splits into a common-interest MS-potential game and a residual that absorbs every player's individualistic payoffs.
  The paper's central result is an identity: for games in which all players have equally many actions, an augmentation of the MS-potential coincides --- up to the additive constant --- with the potential of Candogan, Menache, Ozdaglar and Parrilo (2011).

</details>


### [77] [Intention Inference Under Execution Noise: Separating Aleatoric and Epistemic Uncertainty in Social Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02440)
*Kival Mahadew,Jonathan Shock*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）的框架，用于在含噪社会困境中推断对手意图，并通过主动推理（AIF）框架求解，将成本函数分解为认知性和实用性地两个部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含噪社会困境中，执行动作会被随机破坏，观察到的背叛可能源于敌意或动作误差。标准MDP将执行动作视为状态，无法区分这两种情况，导致系统性过度报复。

Method: 将对手意图编码为潜在状态，执行动作编码为噪声观测，构建POMDP模型；在主动推理框架中求解，成本函数分解为认知部分（推断当前意图）和实用部分（学习意图的演变）。

Result: 在对称噪声的迭代囚徒困境中推导出临界噪声阈值，该阈值控制合作崩溃，并与学习先验的固定点条件相关联。实验表明意图推断的价值依赖上下文：POMDP在面对条件合作对手时具有一致优势，但充分噪声下相互意图推断会产生相关的信念驱动崩溃。

Conclusion: 意图推断的优势特定于意图归因对决策相关的博弈场景，并非在所有社会困境中普遍有益。

Abstract: In noisy social dilemmas, intended actions are stochastically corrupted before execution, so an observed defection may reflect hostile intent or action error. Standard Markov Decision Process (MDP) formulations treat executed actions as states, structurally precluding this distinction and causing systematic over-retaliation. We introduce a Partially Observable MDP (POMDP) formulation encoding opponent intentions as latent states and executed actions as noisy observations, solved within the active inference (AIF) framework with a cost function that decomposes into epistemic and pragmatic components that jointly address inferring current intent and learning how intent evolves. In the Iterated Prisoner's Dilemma with symmetric noise, we derive a critical noise threshold governing cooperation collapse, connecting it to a fixed-point condition on learned priors. Experiments reveal that the value of intention inference is context-dependent: the POMDP provides consistent advantages against conditionally cooperative opponents, but mutual intention inference under sufficient noise produces correlated belief-driven collapse. The advantage is specific to games where intent attribution is decision-relevant.

</details>


### [78] [House-monotone multi-level apportionment has logarithmic quota discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02559)
*Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了多层比例分配（multi-level apportionment）中配额（quota）与单调性（house monotonicity）能否同时满足的问题，给出了否定答案，并通过对二叉梳状结构（binary comb）的分析，确定了最坏情况误差的最优阶为 Θ(log D)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（Schmidt-Kraepelin 等）已经证明在固定议院规模下，下配额与上配额可以同时满足，且存在分别满足某一配额的 house-monotone 规则，但一个自然且核心的开放问题是：是否存在单一规则能同时满足下配额、上配额和 house monotonicity。本文旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 作者利用全二叉梳（full binary comb）结构进行分析。对于 D 个等权叶子的梳状分组，任何 house-monotone 分配序列会产生一个座位接收者序列；配额约束迫使所有网格对齐的前缀差异（grid-aligned prefix discrepancy）小于 1。通过中点嵌入（midpoint embedding）将区间差异（interval discrepancy）约束在该量加 1/2 的范围内，与 Schmidt 的对数下界矛盾。反之，构造了二叉 van der Corput 座位调度，证明其在梳上的前缀误差至多为 log D/(3log2)+1。由此得出最优最坏情况误差为 Θ(log D)。

Result: 对二叉梳状分组，不存在同时满足下配额、上配额和 house monotonicity 的规则。最优最坏情况误差为 Θ(log D)。论文还揭示了静态-动态鸿沟（static-dynamic gap）：每个固定议院规模存在配额可行的分配，但这些可行分配无法被嵌入到一条单调路径中。

Conclusion: 本文否定了单条规则同时满足下配额、上配额与 house monotonicity 的可能性，给出了精确的 Θ(log D) 误差界，并在量化语言中刻画了嵌入式量化惩罚（embedded-quantization penalty）的阶，为渐进式独热舍入（progressive one-hot rounding）提供了理论界限。

Abstract: Multi-level apportionment allocates integer seats through a hierarchy of groups. Schmidt-Kraepelin, Suksompong, and Wijaya proved that, at every fixed house size, lower and upper quota can be satisfied simultaneously; they also constructed house-monotone rules satisfying either quota separately. They left open whether one rule can satisfy lower quota, upper quota, and house monotonicity together, even when quota is required only relative to the root. We give a negative answer. For a full binary comb with $D$ equally entitled leaves, every house-monotone allocation sequence induces a sequence of seat recipients. Quota for the nested comb groups would force every grid-aligned prefix discrepancy to be below one. A midpoint embedding then bounds the full interval discrepancy by this quantity plus $1/2$, contradicting Schmidt's logarithmic lower bound. Conversely, a binary van der Corput seat schedule has comb-prefix error at most $\log D/(3\log 2)+1$. Thus the optimal worst-case error on the comb is $Θ(\log D)$, and for sufficiently large finite $D$ no house-monotone quota rule exists. The proof isolates a static--dynamic gap: each house size admits a quota-feasible allocation, but the feasible allocations cannot be embedded into one monotone path. In quantization language, the result characterizes the order of the embedded-quantization penalty for progressive one-hot rounding on the comb.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [79] [Hierarchical BM25: Lexical Search at Billion-Document Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00229)
*Umesh Deshpande,Swaminathan Sundararaman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Hierarchical BM25，一种分层BM25索引方法，通过常驻小规模粗索引（约4.4GB）选择约1K个主题分组，再对选中的组进行穷举搜索，在保持固定内存和延迟上限的同时实现大规模（10亿文档）的词法检索。查询延迟约300ms，吞吐量较扁平索引提升4.7-5.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扁平BM25索引在10亿文档规模下需要约400GB内存或4-12秒/查询的磁盘延迟，无法在交互式延迟预算内实现精确top-k词法检索，因此需要一种牺牲部分精度但可控内存和延迟的检索方案。

Method: 采用分层结构：一个常驻的粗粒度索引将约10亿文档组织成约1K个主题均衡的分组。查询时使用两个信号选择要访问的分组：(1)查询词在组内的总词频；(2)对于在组间分布过于稀疏的信息性词条，检查其是否在同一文档中共现。选中的分组进行穷举搜索，使用约100KB的全局统计信息打分，确保返回分数与扁平索引完全一致，近似仅局限于分组选择环节。

Result: 常驻内存约4.4GB（与语料大小无关）；10亿文档上的16词查询约300ms返回，吞吐量为扁平多线程索引的4.7-5.6倍；预热缓存可持续约32查询/秒（扁平索引仅不到3）。在50万文档配置下，访问5-10%的聚类可恢复穷举结果分数的0.83-0.92。

Conclusion: Hierarchical BM25通过将近似限制在选择环节而非打分环节，在固定内存和延迟约束下实现了大规模词法检索的显著性能提升。10亿规模下的召回率以及与文档重排BlockMax-WAND的直接对比仍有待进行。

Abstract: A flat BM25 index over one billion documents occupies about 400 GB. Holding it in memory requires DRAM proportional to corpus size. Serving it from disk takes 4-12 seconds per query. Exact top-k lexical retrieval at this scale is therefore impractical within an interactive latency budget.
  Hierarchical BM25 gives up exact ranking in exchange for fixed bounds on memory and latency. A resident coarse index selects which of ~1K topical, size-balanced document groups a query visits, using two signals: the total frequency of each query term within a group, and, for informative terms spread too thinly across groups for frequency totals to reflect, whether several of them appear together in one document. Selected groups are then searched exhaustively and scored against ~100 KB of global statistics. Every returned score therefore equals the flat index's score, and the approximation is confined to selection alone. The resident footprint is ~4.4 GB, independent of corpus size. Sixteen-term queries over one billion documents return in ~300 ms (4.7x to 5.6x the throughput of a flat multi-threaded index), and a warmed cache sustains ~32 queries per second versus under 3 for flat indexing. At a 500K-document configuration, visiting 5-10% of clusters recovers 0.83-0.92 of the exhaustive result score. Billion-scale recall and a direct comparison against document-reordered BlockMax-WAND remain open.

</details>


### [80] [CeQe: Grounding Lexical Retrieval in Semantic Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00452)
*Adam Kahirov,Umesh Deshpande,Swaminathan Sundararaman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CE-QE（Cross-Encoder Query Expansion）是一种混合检索中的查询扩展方法，利用交叉编码器的逐词相关性归因，从语义检索结果中提取关键词并加入BM25查询中，弥合词法检索与语义检索之间的词汇鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BM25词法检索无法处理语义词汇鸿沟问题：当相关文档用不同措辞回答查询时，BM25永远不会检索到该文档，且下游重排序或融合无法恢复候选集中不存在的文档。经典的伪相关反馈复用BM25自身可能错误的结果，导致查询漂移；生成式查询扩展（如HyDE、Query2doc）依赖LLM的幻觉文本，可能引入语料库中不存在的词汇。

Method: 1) 对语义检索的top结果应用交叉编码器，提取每token的相关性归因分数；2) 选择交叉编码器判定为决定性的关键术语；3) 将这些术语逐字追加到BM25查询中。所有扩展词均从检索到的段落中逐字复制，不产生新词汇。同时提出评分融合变体（SESF）。

Result: 在7个BEIR数据集上：1) 在查询与答案词汇分歧的场景下显著提升词法召回率（如NQ的Recall@100从0.32提升至0.47）；2) SESF变体在Recall@100上比交叉编码器评分融合提升2.5%；3) 在nDCG@10上比SPLADEv2提升5.3%、比ColBERTv2提升4.6%；4) 不修改底层BM25索引。

Conclusion: CE-QE通过从语义检索结果中提取词汇来扩展BM25查询，以近乎零额外成本（复用混合流水线中已有的交叉编码器）弥合了词法与语义检索之间的词汇鸿沟，避免了经典PRF的查询漂移和生成式扩展的幻觉问题，在保持词法检索优点（可解释性、无需索引修改）的同时显著提升检索性能。

Abstract: Lexical retrieval (BM25) captures exact keyword matches and weights terms by corpus-wide significance, but it is blind to the semantic vocabulary gap: when a relevant document phrases an answer differently from the query, BM25 never retrieves it, and no amount of downstream reranking or fusion can recover a document that was never in the candidate set. We present Cross-Encoder Query Expansion (CE-QE), which reads the per-token relevance attributions of a cross-encoder applied to top semantic search results, selects the terms the cross-encoder treats as decisive, and appends them to the BM25 query. Unlike classical pseudo-relevance feedback, which reuses BM25's own (possibly wrong) top results, CE-QE seeds expansion from the semantic retriever's results, avoiding self-reinforcing query drift. Unlike recent generative query expansion (HyDE, Query2doc), which prompts a large language model to hallucinate text from its parametric knowledge, every CE-QE expansion term is copied verbatim from a retrieved passage, so it cannot introduce vocabulary the corpus does not contain, and its only added cost is attribution extraction on a cross-encoder a hybrid pipeline already runs for reranking. On seven BEIR datasets, CE-QE improves lexical recall substantially where query and answer vocabulary diverge (e.g., NQ Recall@100 from 0.32 to 0.47), and its score-fusion variant (SESF) beats cross-encoder score fusion by 2.5% on Recall@100 and beats SPLADEv2 and ColBERTv2 by 5.3% and 4.6% on nDCG@10, while leaving the underlying BM25 index completely unmodified.

</details>


### [81] [A Context-Aware Cultural Heritage Guide Powered by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00549)
*Liliana Ardissono,Fabio Ferrero,Angelo Geninatti Cossatin,Claudio Mattutino,Noemi Mauro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了对文化遗产Web应用Triangolazioni的扩展，通过松耦合、与LLM无关的架构，将策展内容与由大型语言模型提供的外部上下文相关信息进行丰富，支持上下文相关信息的搜索与展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产Web应用中策展内容通常静态且缺乏上下文相关的外部信息，需要借助LLM的能力来动态丰富内容，同时保持系统架构的灵活性与LLM无关性。

Method: 采用松耦合架构扩展Triangolazioni应用，使系统能够接入任意LLM，支持上下文依赖的信息搜索与展示功能，架构设计上与具体LLM实现解耦。

Result: 成功实现了对Triangolazioni的扩展，能够在保持系统与特定LLM无关的前提下，为策展内容动态补充上下文相关的外部信息。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在文化遗产领域利用LLM增强内容展示的一种可行方案，其松耦合架构保证了系统的可扩展性和对不同LLM的兼容性。

Abstract: We present an extension of Triangolazioni (a Cultural Heritage webapp) to enrich curated content with context-dependent, external information provided by Large Language Models (LLMs) within a loosely-coupled architecture agnostic to the LLM. The system supports context-dependent information search and presentation within an architecture agnostic to the exploited LLM.

</details>


### [82] [PHA-Net: Prototype-based Hierarchical Alignment Network for Text-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00551)
*Xiaolun Jing,Kezhao Yin,Xinxing Yang,Genke Yang,Jian Chu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于原型的分层对齐网络（PHA-Net），用于文本-视频检索。通过引入模态共享原型作为桥梁来高效对齐个体/局部/全局层次特征，并设计原型支持的token合并模块来增强强语义token、抑制弱语义token，同时提出原型对比损失确保文本和视觉原型聚焦于不同语义信息。在四个基准数据集上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本-视频检索最佳方法通常需要在个体、局部和全局多个层次上同时对齐跨模态语义，但简洁文本与丰富视频之间存在内在语义不匹配问题。传统方法将多个语言-视频注意力模块集成到分层框架中，仅优化视觉表示且计算成本高昂，需要更高效的跨模态对齐方法。

Method: 1) 引入多个模态共享原型作为桥梁，高效优化文本和视频表示以实现跨模态对齐；2) 提出原型支持的token合并模块，利用原型语义引导增强强语义token并抑制弱语义token，解决聚类token中语义分布不平衡的问题；3) 设计原型对比损失，确保同原型下的文本和视觉原型相似度高于不同原型，促使原型聚焦于不同的语义信息。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上验证了PHA-Net的有效性，所有召回率之和相比基线有显著提升：MSR-VTT提升8.8%、ActivityNet提升19.2%、VATEX提升0.7%、Charades提升4.9%。

Conclusion: PHA-Net通过原型驱动的方式实现了高效且有效的多层次跨模态对齐，显著提升了文本-视频检索性能，并提供了公开可用的代码以供复现和进一步研究。

Abstract: With the emergence of large-scale image-text pre-training models, e.g., CLIP, text-video retrieval has experienced substantial advances in recent years. Existing best-performing methods involve aligning cross-modal semantics at individual, local, and global levels simultaneously, raising concerns about the intrinsic semantic mismatch between concise texts and rich videos. A canonical approach is to integrate multiple language-video attention modules into the hierarchical framework while this paradigm only optimizes visual representations with prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose a new prototype-based hierarchical alignment network (PHA-Net) to align individual/local/global level representations across modalities. Concretely, we introduce multiple modality-shared prototypes as the bridge to efficiently optimize text and video representations for cross-modal alignment. Then, we argue that the imbalanced semantic distribution in clustered tokens may undermine retrieval performance, as tokens with weak semantics are of little interest. To reduce the impact of these tokens, a proposed prototype-supported token merge module is responsible for enhancing tokens with strong semantics and suppressing others with weak semantics via prototype semantics guidance. Moreover, we devise a prototype contrastive loss to encourage textual and visual prototypes to focus on different semantic information. The idea of this auxiliary loss is to ensure higher similarity between textual and visual prototypes from the same prototype than those from different prototypes. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks confirm the effectiveness of our PHA-Net, which achieves significant improvements in the sum of all recalls on MSR-VTT (8.8%), ActivityNet (19.2%), VATEX (0.7%), and Charades (4.9%). Code is available at https://github.com/JingXiaolun/PHA-Net.

</details>


### [83] [GARDRec: Decision-Level Graph Grounding for Large Language Model Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00669)
*Yong Wang,Hongliang Sun,Jinlan Liu,Hua Zhang,Dianbo Sui,Dianhui Chu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了GARDRec框架，通过图增强的自适应推理和决策感知方法，将知识图谱的结构信息与LLM结合，用于下一项物品推荐排序任务。该方法在三个公开基准上优于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图增强LLM推荐系统通常仅将知识图谱作为提示级别的证据，导致排序决策缺少结构化用户-物品关系的约束。这对于下一项推荐尤为重要，因为模型需要在相同用户上下文下比较候选物品，同时保持时间偏好、协同信号和属性匹配。

Method: GARDRec包含四个核心组件：(1) 从文本节点特征和图传播构建语义-结构物品表示；(2) 从时间加权历史和一跳邻域推导个性化图上下文；(3) 通过连续多模态提示将图表示与冻结的LLM对齐；(4) 通过后期决策分支注入显式交互和匹配特征，并利用候选间注意力和受限生成似然进行最终排序。

Result: 在三个公开基准上使用多种LLM骨干网络进行实验，GARDRec在候选排序性能上普遍优于代表性基线。消融和诊断分析验证了图投影、邻域检索、显式决策特征、排序损失和生成校准各组件的作用。

Conclusion: GARDRec通过将图结构信号深度融入LLM推荐流程，有效解决了图增强LLM推荐中排序约束不足的问题，验证了多模态提示和对齐策略在冻结LLM推荐场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities for recommendation by interpreting item descriptions, user instructions, and external knowledge through natural-language prompts. However, existing graph-augmented LLM recommenders often use knowledge graphs mainly as prompt-level evidence, leaving ranking decisions weakly constrained by structured user-item relations. This is problematic for next-item recommendation, where the model must compare candidates under the same user context while preserving temporal preference, collaborative signals, and attribute matches. To address this issue, we propose \emph{GARDRec}, a Graph-grounded Adaptive Reasoning and Decision-aware Recommendation framework for LLM-based next-item ranking. GARDRec constructs semantic-structural item representations from textual node features and graph propagation, derives personalized graph contexts from temporally weighted histories and first-order neighborhoods, and aligns graph-derived representations with a frozen LLM through continuous multimodal prompts. Explicit interaction and matching features are injected through late-stage decision branches, while inter-candidate attention and restricted generative likelihood support final ranking. Experiments on three public benchmarks with multiple LLM backbones show that GARDRec generally improves candidate-ranking performance over representative baselines. Ablation and diagnostic analyses verify the contributions of graph projection, neighborhood retrieval, explicit decision features, ranking loss, and generative calibration.

</details>


### [84] [A Triple-Robustness Analysis of Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Multi-Hop Requirements Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00705)
*Meftun Akarsu,Burak Özdemir,Doğancan Büyükçolak,Recep Kaan Karaman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对GraphRAG与向量RAG的对比结论进行了三重鲁棒性分析，固定五管线架构矩阵，变化嵌入器、语料库和评判器，共执行超4400次主矩阵运行和600次跨语料库运行。研究发现：检索集与引文集的评测点不同会颠覆架构排名；答案级引文胜者依赖语料库但稳健于嵌入器；忠实性依赖语料库；单一LLM评判器对忠实性的判断极不稳定（kappa低至0.137-0.14）。作者主张RAG架构声明应在该级别的鲁棒性验证下进行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对GraphRAG与向量RAG的优劣结论存在分歧，且证据通常绑定于单一语料库、嵌入器和评判器，缺乏系统性验证。作者发现分歧部分源于引文质量的测量位置差异，因此需要进行多维度鲁棒性分析来厘清真实结论。

Method: 构建固定五管线架构矩阵，系统变化三个维度：嵌入器（e5-small与Azure text-embedding-3-small）、语料库（DO-178C需求文档与Wikipedia段落链MuSiQue）、评判器（GPT-5.4与GPT-4.1）。共进行2×4,440次主矩阵运行、600次跨语料库运行和超5,000次忠实性判断。同时训练基于稠密嵌入的学习路由器进行跳数分类。

Result: 三类关键发现：(1) 检索集作为归因集会颠覆架构排名（GraphRAG检索精度0.12-0.23但引文精度0.48-0.65）；(2) 答案级引文胜者依赖语料库但稳健于嵌入器（GraphRAG在短跳DO-178C上与vanilla持平，赢下所有MuSiQue层次）；(3) 单一评判器LLM忠实性判断极不稳定，GPT-5.4自我kappa仅0.137，重审kappa≤0.14。学习路由器达到宏F1 0.86。

Conclusion: RAG架构声明（包括对引文测量点的鲁棒性）必须在本研究提供的鲁棒性测试级别下验证后方可采信，否则基于单一语料库、嵌入器或评判器的结论不可靠。

Abstract: Reported verdicts on GraphRAG versus vector RAG disagree, and the evidence is typically tied to a single corpus, embedder, and judge -- and, we show, to where citation quality is measured. We present a triple-robustness analysis that holds a five-pipeline architecture matrix fixed and varies embedder (local e5-small vs. Azure text-embedding-3-small), corpus (DO-178C typed-edge requirements vs. Wikipedia paragraph chains via MuSiQue), and judge (paired GPT-5.4 x GPT-4.1 on both corpora), over 2x4,440 main-matrix runs, 600 cross-corpus runs, and over 5,000 faithfulness judgments. (C2a) GraphRAG's graph walk floods the context window at precision 0.12-0.23, but the synthesizer cites selectively at precision 0.48-0.65; scoring the retrieved set as the attribution set inverts the architecture ranking, which reconciles part of the disagreement in prior reports. (C1) Answer-level citation winners are corpus- and stratum-conditional but embedder-robust: GraphRAG ties vanilla on short-hop DO-178C queries and wins every MuSiQue stratum, while agentic pipelines lead only on 3+-hop requirements queries. (C2b) Faithfulness is corpus-conditional: on DO-178C it declines with hop distance (trend p<0.05 in three of four judge x embedder combinations); on Wikipedia chains neither judge shows a collapse. (C3) Single-judge LLM faithfulness is fragile to retrieval state: GPT-5.4's self-kappa across embedders is 0.137 (41% verdict change) against a same-day test-retest floor of 0.76, and re-judging frozen inputs eleven weeks later gives kappa <= 0.14 for both judges. A learned router on dense embeddings alone reaches macro-F1 0.86 on hop classification (C4). We argue that RAG architecture claims should be tested at this level of robustness -- including robustness to the citation-measurement point -- before they are trusted.

</details>


### [85] [Hierarchical Residual Policy Optimization for Generative Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00750)
*Kaifeng Guo,Yiming Yang,Jingtong Gao,Guolei Zeng,Fukang Yang,Yukang Liang,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HRPO（层次残差策略优化）框架，用于生成式推荐系统的后训练优化。HRPO将物品级别的离线反馈转化为稠密的、与语义标识符（SID）token对齐的学习信号，通过前缀级效用估计、残差token信用分解和残差回报策略优化，解决了传统方法在token级信用分配上的稀疏性和高方差问题，并在公开数据集和线上A/B测试中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器通过自回归解码语义标识符（SIDs）来选择物品，其token位置形成从粗到细的物品层级结构。然而，现有SID解码器通过监督式下一token预测训练，只是模仿记录轨迹而非直接优化下游效用。虽然可以通过结果反馈进行后训练，但日志反馈仅对最终曝光物品可观测，导致大多数后训练方法在物品层面操作，将相同的终端信号广播到所有SID token上，使得token级信用分配稀疏、高方差且依赖层深度。

Method: HRPO包含三个核心步骤：(1) 通过基于特征用户聚类的分组奖励平滑，估计SID前缀级别的效用；(2) 将这些效用分解为残差token信用，并累积为credit-to-go信号；(3) 残差回报策略优化（RRPO）使用裁剪更新、组归一化优势和KL正则化来优化残差信用，以保持训练稳定性。

Result: 在公开数据集和大型商业系统的在线A/B测试中，HRPO在会话级效用和关键业务指标上均取得了一致的提升，证明了该方法在实际场景中的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: HRPO通过将物品级结果反馈转化为稠密、token对齐的学习信号，实现了保守的token级策略改进，有效解决了生成式推荐后训练中的信用分配难题，并提供了可复现的源代码和归档工件。

Abstract: Generative recommenders select items by autoregressively decoding semantic identifiers (SIDs), whose token positions induce a coarse-to-fine hierarchy over the item space. In practice, SID decoders are trained via supervised next-token prediction, which imitates logged trajectories rather than directly optimizing downstream utility. This motivates post-training with outcome feedback to guide decoding toward higher utility. However, logged feedback is only observed for the final exposed item, causing most post-training methods to operate at the item level and broadcast the same terminal signal across all SID tokens. As a result, token-level credit assignment becomes sparse, high-variance, and layer-dependent. To this end, we propose Hierarchical Residual Policy Optimization (HRPO), a post-training framework that converts item-level outcomes into dense, token-aligned learning signals for conservative token-wise improvement. Specifically, HRPO first estimates SID prefix-level utilities via group-wise reward smoothing over feature-based user clusters. It then decomposes these utilities into residual token credits and accumulates them into credit-to-go signals. Finally, Residual-Return Policy Optimization (RRPO) optimizes the residual credits using clipped updates, group-normalized advantages, and KL regularization to preserve stability. Experiments on a public dataset and an online A/B test in a large-scale commercial system show consistent gains in session-level utility and key business metrics. Source code and the archived artifact are available for reproduction.

</details>


### [86] [Exponential Reward Weighting for Fine-Tuning Generative Recommenders under Sparse and Noisy Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00816)
*Keertana Chidambaram,Sanath Kumar Krishnamurthy,Qiuling Xu,Ko-Jen Hsiao,Moumita Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Exp-RSFT方法，通过对登录交互按指数奖励加权进行微调，避免生成式推荐系统中奖励过度优化问题。理论证明其次优性分解为覆盖成本和噪声成本，温度参数λ平衡两者。实验验证性能随λ呈倒U型趋势，优于PPO和DPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中用户反馈稀疏且带噪声，后训练生成式推荐器面临两个挑战：从登录交互训练得到的奖励模型难以泛化，直接优化不完美的奖励会导致奖励过度优化。

Method: Exp-RSFT（指数奖励加权微调），每个登录交互按exp(r/λ)加权，直接优化登录奖励，用温度λ正则化噪声。理论上证明次优性分解为覆盖成本（logging policy局限）和噪声成本（反馈不完美），λ平衡两者的最优权衡。

Result: 在三个公开基准和大型工业数据集上，性能随λ呈倒U型趋势；PPO和DPO常过度优化不可靠的奖励模型而降低推荐质量；Exp-RSFT无需在线探索或偏好数据即可持续提升排序性能。

Conclusion: Exp-RSFT提供了一种无需在线探索或偏好数据的生成式推荐器训练方案，通过λ参数实现对高奖励行为利用与噪声鲁棒性之间的最优权衡。

Abstract: In recommendation systems, users interact with only a small fraction of a vast item catalog, producing feedback that is both sparse and noisy. This challenges post-training generative recommenders: reward models trained from logged interactions often fail to generalize, while directly optimizing imperfect rewards can lead to reward over-optimization. We propose Exponential reward-weighted fine-tuning (Exp-RSFT), where each logged interaction is weighted by $\exp(r/λ)$, avoids this failure by optimizing directly on the logged rewards, with the temperature $λ$ regularizing against their noise. We theoretically show that Exp-RSFT's suboptimality decomposes into two costs: a coverage cost arising from limitations of the logging policy and a noise cost from imperfect feedback. The temperature $λ$ balances these competing effects, yielding an optimal tradeoff between exploiting high-reward behavior and robustness to noise. Across three public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset, we verify this theoretical prediction: performance follows an inverted-U trend as a function of $λ$, while PPO and DPO often over-optimize unreliable reward models and degrade recommendation quality. Exp-RSFT consistently improves ranking performance without requiring online exploration or preference data.

</details>


### [87] [Tevatron Meets Megatron: Expert-Parallel LLM Reranker Training on an Academic Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00916)
*Zhichao Xu,Xueguang Ma,Shengyao Zhuang,Luyu Gao,Wenqian Ye,Yu Wang,Jamie Callan,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍Tevatron 3.0，将Megatron-Core训练后端集成到Tevatron重排序框架中，以支持大规模MoE（混合专家）模型的训练。新后端在保持数据管线和评估流程不变的同时，支持专家并行，使得30B参数的Qwen3-30B-A3B MoE重排序器训练成为可能（此前用PyTorch FSDP1不可行）。实验表明MoE重排序器在激活参数不到一半的情况下，质量与密集8B模型相当，且推理吞吐量更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代重排序方法（十亿级跨编码器、MoE骨干、对强教师蒸馏）已远超大多数学术团队的训练基础设施。现有Tevatron重排序训练依赖Hugging Face Trainer配合DeepSpeed或PyTorch FSDP1，但这些后端缺乏对大规模MoE训练的高效支持，亟需新的训练后端。

Method: 将Megatron-Core训练后端集成到Tevatron 3.0，保留原有数据管线、评估流程和Hugging Face兼容检查点；支持LoRA和全参数微调；利用专家并行训练30B参数的Qwen3-30B-A3B MoE重排序器。

Result: Megatron与FSDP在数据并行设置下重排序质量相当且训练效率相当；推荐单节点配置下提速高达22%；MoE重排序器在激活参数不到一半时匹配密集8B模型质量，推理吞吐量显著更高。同时报告了Hugging Face和vLLM的推理吞吐量。

Conclusion: 该框架使得学术团队也能训练大规模MoE重排序器，并揭示MoE重排序器在参数效率和推理效率上的优势。框架和训练检查点将开源发布。

Abstract: Modern reranking recipes---billion-scale cross-encoders, mixture-of-experts (MoE) backbones, and distillation against strong teachers---have outpaced the training infrastructure available to most academic groups. Existing Tevatron reranker training relies on the Hugging Face Trainer with DeepSpeed or PyTorch FSDP1, but these backends lack efficient support for large-scale MoE training. We present Tevatron 3.0, which integrates a Megatron-Core training backend into Tevatron while preserving its data pipeline, evaluation workflow, and Hugging Face-compatible checkpoints. We benchmark existing distributed training configurations against the new backend, showing that Megatron matches FSDP reranker quality and training efficiency under comparable data-parallel settings, is up to 22% faster in the recommended single-node configuration, and supports both LoRA and full-parameter fine-tuning. Crucially, expert parallelism enables training a 30B-parameter Qwen3-30B-A3B MoE reranker, which is infeasible with PyTorch FSDP1. Using this framework, we conduct a controlled comparison of MoE versus dense models, LoRA versus full-parameter tuning, and distillation versus contrastive training on BEIR-15 with three first-stage retrievers, and report serving throughput for Hugging Face and vLLM. We find that the MoE reranker matches dense 8B quality while activating less than half as many parameters and achieving substantially higher inference throughput. We will release the framework and trained checkpoints.

</details>


### [88] [GRACE: Generative Recommender Acceleration Engine for Real-Time Ads Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00938)
*Zhou Fang,Yuhang Huang,Ang Zhang,Yihan He,Ruichao Xiao,Chao Li,Yavuz Yetim,Sibyl Yang,Xiaohan Wei,Fei Tian,Liang Wang,Liyuan Li,Nathan Yan,Gaoxiang Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GRACE，一个用于广告生成式检索（generative recommenders）的在线服务系统，针对高吞吐、实时广告检索场景中的两个核心挑战：广告资格（eligibility）和计算成本。GRACE通过Generative Target Matching (GTM)解决资格问题，将输出从23.55%提升到40.42%的通过率；通过对encoder-decoder Transformer的注意力内核、KV cache和beam search进行针对宽波束、短序列场景的重新设计，在GH200上实现交叉注意力68倍、自注意力23.4-25.8倍的延迟提升，整体decoder延迟降低11.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的广告生成式检索面临两大服务挑战：一是资格（eligibility），即每个生成的广告必须满足广告主的受众定向规则；二是计算成本，需要在严格的延迟和GPU成本约束下，通过宽波束解码（wide-beam decoding）每秒生成数千条广告。现有系统无法同时满足这两个要求。

Method: GRACE包含两大核心设计：1) Generative Target Matching (GTM)——将目录约束解码（catalog-valid constrained decoding）扩展为基于Semantic ID (SID)前缀的个性化过滤，使用由定向规则导出的bitmask和Bloom filter匹配器实现；2) 面向encoder-decoder Transformer的解码器重构——针对宽波束、短序列场景重新设计注意力内核、KV cache和beam search优化，选择比LLM更轻量的encoder-decoder架构。

Result: SID级别的GTM将最终广告级目标匹配通过率从23.55%提升到40.42%（相比仅用约束解码）。在NVIDIA GH200上，相比FlashAttention-2和FlashAttention-3中更快者，GRACE将交叉注意力延迟提升68.0倍，自注意力延迟提升23.4-25.8倍（跨解码步骤）。综合这些优化，decoder延迟整体降低11.1倍，使广告生成式检索满足延迟和计算要求。

Conclusion: GRACE通过GTM和针对性的解码器优化，成功解决了广告生成式检索生产化中的资格与计算两大挑战，在保持广告合规性的同时实现了显著的性能提升，证明了生成式检索在真实广告场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Productionizing generative recommenders for high-volume, real-time ads retrieval creates two serving challenges: eligibility, ensuring that each generated ad is eligible for the request under the advertiser's audience targeting rules, and compute, which requires meeting strict latency and GPU cost requirements while remaining capable of generating thousands of ads per request with wide-beam decoding. This paper presents GRACE, a serving system for ads generative retrieval that addresses both challenges. For eligibility, GRACE introduces Generative Target Matching (GTM), which extends catalog-valid constrained decoding with personalized filtering over Semantic ID (SID) prefixes using bitmask and Bloom filter matchers derived from targeting rules. SID-level GTM improves final ad-level target matching pass rate from 23.55% to 40.42% over constrained decoding alone. For compute-cost and latency, GRACE targets encoder-decoder Transformers, which are more lightweight than LLMs. It redesigns the decoder around the wide-beam, short-sequence regime, covering attention kernels, KV cache, and beam search optimizations. On NVIDIA GH200, compared with the faster of FlashAttention-2 and FlashAttention-3 baselines, GRACE improves cross-attention latency by 68.0 times and self-attention latency by 23.4-25.8 times across decode steps. Together, these changes reduce decoder latency by 11.1 times, keeping ads generative retrieval within latency and compute requirements.

</details>


### [89] [UniHEAR: Unified Heterogeneous-Source Attentive Retrieval for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01147)
*Ganzhong Luo,Yang Ren,Hanyong Wang,Shuyu Zheng,Menglong Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniHEAR是一个统一的轻量级框架，用于异构来源的实体检索和重排序，通过粗检索描述符、检索引导的注意力模态门控和熵加权源融合，解决了KB-VQA中单一来源检索瓶颈和检索源盲重排序两个关键问题，在E-VQA和InfoSeek上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KB-VQA的检索增强系统存在两个关键限制：(1)依赖单一检索模态导致"单一来源检索瓶颈"，无法获取仅通过互补来源才能访问到的ground-truth实体；(2)双塔逐点重排序器存在"检索源盲重排序"问题，忽略了检索来源和候选级检索先验，导致模态依赖冗余。

Method: UniHEAR提出了统一的轻量级异构来源实体检索与重排序框架，包括三个核心组件：(1)为每个候选实体构建粗检索描述符；(2)引入检索引导的注意力模态门控，根据描述符条件化模态注意力权重；(3)采用熵加权源融合整合粗检索先验。同时使用对比学习与辅助模态保持损失的混合训练策略，在单一模型中统一实体级和章节级检索。

Result: 在E-VQA和InfoSeek数据集上的大量实验表明，UniHEAR在检索和VQA性能上均达到SOTA，Recall@1相比最强基线分别提升6.7和1.2个百分点，同时保持了轻量级的重排序架构。

Conclusion: UniHEAR通过统一的多源检索与重排序框架有效解决了KB-VQA中的来源选择和模态依赖问题，证明了轻量级架构也能在复杂检索任务中取得优异性能。

Abstract: Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) requires retrieving relevant entity knowledge from external sources to answer visually grounded questions. Existing retrieval-augmented systems suffer from two critical limitations. First, relying on a single retrieval modality creates a Single-Source Retrieval Bottleneck, missing ground-truth entities that are only accessible through complementary sources. Second, dual-tower pointwise rerankers suffer from Retrieval-Source-Blind Reranking, as they overlook retrieval origins and candidate-level retrieval priors, leading to redundant modality reliance. To address these challenges, we propose UniHEAR, a unified lightweight framework for heterogeneous-source entity retrieval and reranking. UniHEAR constructs a Coarse Retrieval Descriptor for each candidate entity, and introduces Retrieval-Guided Attentive Modality Gating to condition modality attention weights on this descriptor, further complemented by Entropy-Weighted Source Fusion of coarse retrieval priors. A hybrid training strategy combining contrastive learning with an auxiliary modality-preserving loss unifies entity-level and section-level retrieval within a single model. Extensive experiments on E-VQA and InfoSeek demonstrate that UniHEAR achieves state-of-the-art retrieval and VQA performance, improving Recall@1 by 6.7 and 1.2 points over the strongest baselines while maintaining a lightweight reranking architecture. Code and model are available at https://github.com/iven-luo/UniHEAR.

</details>


### [90] [Auditing Semantic Gains in Sequential Recommendation: A Lightweight Recovery Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01260)
*Kong Wang,Zhongke He,Xiang Chen,Hongwei Zeng,Kai Deng,Long Wang,Kehua Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LIME-Rec通过轻量级可审计的恢复测试来厘清语义/生成式推荐器性能提升的来源，该方法结合SASRec序列专家、ItemCF共现专家和冻结语义嵌入专家，通过可审计的分数级融合与历史校准，在亚马逊三个数据集上取得7%-12%的R@10提升，并证明增益来自真实的物品-文本对应关系而非模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语义和生成式检索推荐器声称比ID-only基线有显著提升，但这些提升究竟来自语言模型推理、语义ID生成、端到端语义架构、更强的离线物品表示，还是语义与协同信号的互补，尚不清楚。作者希望厘清这种归因模糊性。

Method: LIME-Rec构建三个独立专家：SASRec序列专家（协同信号）、ItemCF共现专家（协同信号）和基于冻结BAAI/bge-base-en-v1.5物品嵌入的语义专家。三个专家的全目录分数按用户归一化，通过可审计的分数级融合（fusion gate）结合，再进行有界历史校准（calibration head）。融合门和校准头仅用验证集拟合，不需要服务时语言模型推理，每个专家贡献可单独检查。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty、Toys和Sports上，LIME-Rec的R@10分别为0.0996、0.1105和0.0593，比最强基线高7.0%-12.0%。三专家融合不管有无历史校准都优于校准后的SASRec，证明增益不单来自校准。随机置换物品-文本嵌入使R@10降低13.6%-17.5%，表明增益依赖真实物品-文本对应关系而非额外表示容量。

Conclusion: 在将提升归因于服务时语言建模、语义ID生成或更重的语义机制之前，应首先排除轻量级恢复（离线物品表示+透明融合）的贡献。这为推荐系统领域提供了一种低成本、可审计的消融基线方法。

Abstract: Recent semantic and generative-retrieval recommenders report substantial improvements over ID-only sequential baselines, but it remains unclear whether these gains arise from language-model reasoning, semantic-ID generation, end-to-end semantic architectures, stronger offline item representations, or complementary semantic and collaborative signals. We investigate this attribution ambiguity through LIME-Rec, a lightweight and auditable recovery test. LIME-Rec combines three independent experts: a SASRec sequential expert, an ItemCF co-occurrence expert, and a semantic expert based on frozen BAAI/bge-base-en-v1.5 item embeddings. Their full-catalog scores are normalized per user and combined through auditable score-level fusion followed by bounded history calibration. The fusion gate and calibration head are fitted on validation data only, require no serving-time language-model inference, and keep each expert contribution separately inspectable. On Amazon Beauty, Toys, and Sports, LIME-Rec achieves R@10 scores of 0.0996, 0.1105, and 0.0593, outperforming the strongest comparison baseline by 7.0%-12.0%. Three-expert fusion without history calibration consistently outperforms calibrated SASRec, showing that calibration alone does not explain the recovery. Randomly permuting item-text embeddings across item IDs reduces R@10 by 13.6%-17.5%, indicating that the gains depend on genuine item-text correspondence rather than additional representation capacity. These results suggest that lightweight recovery from offline item representations and transparent fusion should be ruled out before improvements are attributed to serving-time language modeling, semantic-ID generation, or heavier semantic machinery.

</details>


### [91] [Collaborative Memory Augmentation for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01315)
*Enze Liu,Zhen Tian,Wayne Xin Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OMEGA是一个为生成式推荐（GR）设计的协作记忆增强框架，通过引入外部协作记忆库来弥补现有GR模型仅依赖内部参数空间、缺乏跨用户协作信号的不足。它采用潜在上下文压缩、目标感知检索和上下文感知门控融合机制，显著提升了推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式推荐框架主要关注单个用户序列的内部参数化建模，未能显式利用跨用户的协作信号。这一局限阻碍了模型从全局行为模式中获益，因此需要一种能够桥接隐式参数知识与显式协作信号的机制。

Method: OMEGA包含三个核心组件：(1) 潜在上下文压缩方法，利用可学习查询令牌将序列用户行为蒸馏为紧凑表示以降低存储开销；(2) 协作记忆库，聚合压缩表示作为全局行为模式的显式存储库；(3) 轻量级目标感知检索机制，同时考虑序列级和目标级相似性来识别相关记忆，并采用带门控交叉注意力的上下文感知集成模块自适应融合检索到的记忆与局部用户上下文，同时缓解噪声模式干扰。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实证评估表明，OMEGA显著优于现有先进的生成式推荐模型，验证了外部记忆作为生成范式补充的潜力。

Conclusion: 外部协作记忆能够有效补充生成式推荐范式，OMEGA证明了显式利用跨用户协作信号可以进一步提升生成式推荐的性能，为该领域提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has exhibited great potential by modeling item transitions as a sequence-to-sequence task. Despite the success of GR, existing frameworks primarily focus on modeling individual user sequences within a constrained internal parametric space, failing to explicitly leverage cross-user collaborative signals. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{OMEGA}, a cOllaborative MEmory augmentation framework for Generative recommendAtion. OMEGA bridges the gap between implicit parametric knowledge and explicit collaborative signals. We first introduce a latent context compression method that utilizes learnable query tokens to distill sequential user behavior into compact representations, significantly reducing storage overhead. These compressed representations are aggregated into a collaborative memory bank, serving as an explicit repository of global behavioral patterns. To ensure precise knowledge acquisition, we design a lightweight and target-aware retrieval mechanism that identifies pertinent memories by considering both sequence-level and target-level similarities. Furthermore, a context-aware integration module, equipped with a gated cross-attention mechanism, is employed to adaptively fuse the retrieved collaborative memories with the local user context while mitigating the interference of noisy patterns. Empirical evaluations on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that OMEGA significantly outperforms existing advanced GR models, validating the potential of external memory as a complement to the generative paradigm.

</details>


### [92] [Real-Time Hybrid Retrieval in Hyperbolic Space for Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01450)
*Aradhya Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个完全在双曲几何Lorentz模型中运行的混合文档检索系统，用于检索增强生成（RAG）。系统通过学习的HyTE-H变换将预训练词嵌入投影到双曲空间，采用BM25词法评分与双曲相似度评分两阶段流水线检索，在五个BEIR基准数据集上仅凭词嵌入即取得有竞争力的NDCG@10结果，且无需微调神经编码器，支持边缘设备上的实时索引与交互式查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的稠密检索器局限于欧几里得空间，难以充分利用自然语言中固有的层次化组织特性。双曲几何具有指数体积增长特性，更契合语言数据的层级结构，因此作者希望探索在双曲空间中构建检索系统的可能性。

Method: 系统采用两阶段检索管道：首先通过BM25进行词法评分初筛，然后使用Lorentzian内积相似度对候选文档重新排序。通过可调参数α融合BM25分数与双曲相似度分数。文档被分割为重叠块，以Lorentz嵌入进行索引。系统仅使用预训练词嵌入经HyTE-H变换投影到双曲空间，无需微调神经编码器或交叉注意力重排器。

Result: 在BEIR基准的五个数据集（SciFact、NFCorpus、ArguAna、SciDocs、FiQA）上分别取得NDCG@10分数0.654、0.304、0.342、0.150和0.217，仅凭词嵌入实现。系统支持实时索引和资源高效的查询，可在边缘设备上以交互延迟运行。

Conclusion: 该工作证明双曲空间检索可以作为轻量级、资源高效的RAG方案，无需大规模微调即可获得合理性能，并能在边缘设备上实时运行，为部署受限环境中的检索增强生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid document retrieval system designed for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that operates entirely within the Lorentz model of hyperbolic geometry. Unlike conventional dense retrievers confined to Euclidean space, this system projects pretrained word embeddings into hyperbolic space through a learned HyTE-H transformation, whose exponential volume growth suits the hierarchical organization of natural language. Documents are segmented into overlapping chunks, indexed by their Lorentz embeddings, and retrieved through a two-stage pipeline that first applies BM25 lexical scoring, then re-ranks candidates using Lorentzian inner-product similarity. A tunable parameter $α$ blends the BM25 score with the hyperbolic similarity score. The system was evaluated on five datasets from the BEIR benchmark suite, SciFact, NFCorpus, ArguAna, SciDocs, and FiQA, achieving NDCG@10 scores of 0.654, 0.304, 0.342, 0.150, and 0.217 respectively with word embeddings alone, without fine-tuned neural encoders or cross-attention rerankers. The system supports real-time indexing of user-supplied documents and resource-efficient querying over tens of thousands of moderately sized documents, so hyperbolic retrieval can run on edge devices at interactive latencies.

</details>


### [93] [Floor, Ceiling, and the Fusion Gap: How Much of Crowd Reading Attention Can Machines Predict?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01704)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文围绕"预测众包读者在网页文档中高亮的句子"这一任务，构建了性能的上下界（朴素截断为下限，分裂半集市为上限），系统分析了上下界之间差距的来源（语义性），评估了前沿语言模型的零样本表现，并提出跨供应商融合策略达到上限的60%，最后通过知识蒸馏将融合模型压缩为8B开源学生模型，保留90%的融合优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数若无参照系则毫无意义——不知道平凡方法能达到什么水平，也不知道最优方法的上限在哪里。作者旨在为"众包读者高亮句子预测"这一罕见拥有真实标注的任务建立上下界，并系统理解上下界之间差距的结构性来源。

Method: 1) 以朴素截断（lead）作为下界，以分裂半集市（split-half oracle）作为上界；2) 通过位置和长度特征分析差距的语义性；3) 评估前沿语言模型的零样本表现及LLMLingua-2压缩器；4) 提出无权重跨供应商融合（五个前沿模型排名+位置先验）；5) 进行预注册复制验证；6) 将融合模型蒸馏为8B开源学生模型并比较全文与局部上下文的差异。

Result: 上下界差距为+0.2028 AP；位置和长度特征仅恢复5%差距（语义性）；前沿模型零样本达到35-53%，远超经典基线但远低于众包水平，LLMLingua-2低于下界与随机无异；跨供应商融合达到60%，比最佳单模型提升+0.0159（预注册复现+0.0179，p=0.042）；蒸馏的8B全文学生模型保留90%融合优势并与最强单模型统计持平，局部上下文学生仅保留63%。

Conclusion: 众包的信号蕴含在文档级结构中而非局部上下文；跨供应商模型融合是已知最廉价的性能提升手段；基准评估必须同时报告上下界才能真正理解模型能力；蒸馏可以高效保留融合模型的优势。

Abstract: A benchmark score means nothing without knowing what a trivial method achieves and what the best possible method could achieve. We construct both bounds for a task with a rare kind of ground truth: predicting which sentences a crowd of readers -- highlighting for their own purposes, unpaid, uninstructed, and blind to each other -- marked in 120 web documents. The floor is naive truncation (lead); the ceiling is a split-half oracle: half the crowd predicting the other half. The gap between them is +0.2028 AP [+0.1698, +0.2342, domain-clustered], and three findings structure it. First, the gap is semantic: position and length features recover 5% of it. Second, frontier language models reach 35-53% of it zero-shot -- far above classical baselines, far below the crowd; a state-of-the-art prompt compressor (LLMLingua-2) lands below the floor, indistinguishable from random selection. Third, an unweighted cross-vendor fusion of five frontier rankings plus a position prior reaches 60%, beating the best single model by +0.0159 [+0.0044, +0.0269; Holm p=0.019] -- a gain that survives ablation of its best member, split-half arm selection, prompt paraphrase, and label, gate, and seed perturbations, and was CONFIRMED by a pre-registered replication on 217 independent documents (+0.0179, Holm p=0.042). Finally, the bracket compresses: distilling the fusion into one open-weight 8B student that reads the whole document retains 90% of the fusion's edge and reaches statistical parity with the strongest single frontier model (+0.0070 [-0.0068, +0.0200]), where a local-context student retains only 63% -- the crowd's signal lives in document-level structure, and the cheapest known improvement is to ask several different models and average.

</details>


### [94] [MODE: Mutual Optimality in Direct Effects of Reciprocal Recommendations in Matching Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01731)
*Yoji Tomita*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对匹配平台（如招聘和交友平台）中的双向推荐系统（RRS）问题，提出了"直接效应最优性"概念，并设计了MODE方法，在考虑其他用户推荐结果的前提下，为每个个体用户计算相互最优的推荐列表，兼顾了双边偏好和机会集中度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配平台的成功关键在于设计合适的双向推荐系统，需要同时考虑双边用户的偏好并防止机会过度集中在少数热门用户上。然而，过度强调集中度缓解会导致部分个体用户获得不满意的推荐结果，造成用户流失。现有方法难以在个体满意度和全局集中度控制之间取得平衡。

Method: 论文形式化了"直接效应最优性"的概念，即在给定其他用户推荐结果的前提下，衡量个体用户推荐列表的最优程度。基于这一概念，提出了一种名为MODE（Mutually Optimal Direct Effects）的新方法，能够计算在直接效应方面相互最优的推荐列表，实现个体级和全局级的双向优化。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，MODE在相互直接效应最优性方面优于现有方法，具有更快的处理速度，并能实现更高的预期匹配数量。

Conclusion: MODE通过形式化直接效应最优性并为个体用户计算相互最优的推荐，有效解决了匹配平台中个体满意度与机会集中度缓解之间的权衡问题，在效果和效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Matching platforms such as job posting services and online dating platforms have become widely used over the past decade. For a matching platform to be successful, it is crucial to design appropriate reciprocal recommendation systems (RRSs) that consider the preferences of users on both sides (job candidates and employers) and prevent opportunities from being concentrated too heavily on a few popular users. However, prioritizing concentration mitigation too much can lead to recommending undesirable results to some individual users, resulting in their dissatisfaction. In this paper, we formulate the concept of ``optimality of direct effects'' of the recommendation list for an individual user, given the recommendations to other users. Furthermore, we propose a novel method, MODE, that computes mutually optimal recommendations in direct effects. Experiments with synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that MODE surpasses other existing methods in terms of mutual optimality of direct effects, exhibits faster processing speeds, and enables a higher expected number of matches.

</details>


### [95] [X-KGRank: A Knowledge Graph RAG Framework for Explainable Recommendations via Pattern Mining and LLM Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01732)
*Meenakshi Rajpurohit,Jainish Patel*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: X-KGRank是一个知识图谱检索增强框架，统一了协同过滤和LLM解释生成。它从MovieLens-1M构建异构知识图谱，用LightGCN进行排序，并通过选择性路由策略为长尾物品生成知识图谱路径增强的解释，在推荐指标和解释质量上均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统产生用户无法质询的预测。协同过滤捕获行为信号但缺乏推理能力，LLM能生成流畅解释但容易产生幻觉且缺乏用户历史基础。需要一种统一两种优势的方法来提供可解释、有根据的推荐。

Method: 从MovieLens-1M构建包含9,762节点和999,264条边的异构知识图谱（三种关系：RATED、HAS_GENRE、CO_RATED），存储在Neo4j中。训练带内容感知SBERT初始化和评分加权BPR目标的LightGCN排序器，采用流行度选择性路由策略：长尾物品用知识图谱路径接地，流行物品用预训练知识服务，减少约50%的KG增强生成。

Result: 在99样本协议下，NDCG@10=0.2956，Recall@10=0.5371，较流行度基线提升17.1%；NDCG@20提升15.6%，MRR提升14.6%。16个案例中，Qwen2.5-1.5B在启发式解释质量上（0.97 vs 0.94）甚至匹配更大的Mistral-7B，但定性分析显示小模型更易产生事实性编造。

Conclusion: X-KGRank证明知识图谱检索增强能有效统一结构化协同过滤与LLM解释，在保持推荐性能的同时提供有根据的解释。然而小模型存在事实性编造问题，说明模型规模与生成可靠性的权衡仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems produce predictions that users cannot interrogate. The two dominant improvements, collaborative filtering and LLM-based reasoning, each fall short: collaborative filtering captures behavioural signals but offers no reasoning, while large language models (LLMs) generate fluent explanations but hallucinate and are poorly grounded in a user's history. We present X-KGRank, a knowledge graph retrieval augmented framework that unifies structural collaborative filtering with LLM-based explanation. From the MovieLens-1M dataset (6,040 users, 3,704 items, 988,129 interactions) we construct a heterogeneous knowledge graph of 9,762 nodes and 999,264 edges spanning three relation types (RATED, HAS_GENRE, and CO_RATED) persisted in Neo4j. We train a LightGCN ranker with content-aware SBERT initialization and a rating weighted BPR objective, and apply a popularity selective routing strategy that grounds long-tail items (1,855 of 3,704) in knowledge-graph paths while serving popular items from pre-trained knowledge, reducing KG-augmented generations by roughly 50%. On the MovieLens-1M test set under a 99-sample protocol, X-KGRank achieves NDCG@10 = 0.2956 and Recall@10 = 0.5371, improving over a strong popularity baseline by 17.1% on both metrics, by 15.6% on NDCG@20 (0.3449 vs. 0.2983), and by 14.6% on MRR (0.2435 vs. 0.2124). Across three LLM backbones evaluated on 16 cases, a 1.5-billion-parameter model (Qwen2.5-1.5B) matches a 7-billion-parameter model (Mistral-7B) on heuristic explanation quality (0.97 vs. 0.94), yet qualitative analysis shows the smaller model is more prone to factual fabrication.

</details>


### [96] [SPEAR: Selection-aware Personalized End-to-end Adaptive Rewriting and Retrieval for Community Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01738)
*Wenbin Wu,Yuzhong Wu,Yufan Xu,Kuan Fang,Xing Xu,Cheng Ye,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPEAR框架，一种选择感知的端到端自适应改写与检索系统，针对电商搜索中查询改写与检索分离导致的"通用词主导效应"问题，通过双嵌入主干、乘法门控聚合器和动态改写选择器三个组件，实现了改写质量与检索效果的结构对齐，离线与在线实验均验证了其有效性，并已部署于Dewu社区搜索平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) 生产系统中的查询改写优化与检索效果优化是分离的，导致两阶段结构不对齐；2) 路径式端到端架构虽能统一两者，但最初为个性化设计，未将相关性作为显式约束；3) 直接迁移会引发"通用词主导效应"——模型偏好通用改写而偏离用户原始查询意图。

Method: SPEAR包含三个针对性组件：(1) 双嵌入主干+辅助损失+梯度隔离，保护召回侧语义不被CTR驱动的排序信号侵蚀；(2) 乘法门控聚合器，仅在改写置信度和条目相关性都强时给予高分，消除通用词捷径；(3) 动态改写选择器，联合生成请求特定的改写权重和用户-查询条件化的缩放与偏置项，使改写偏好和相关性校准适应每个请求。

Result: 离线评估（100K工业搜索会话）：改写语义相似度@10提升+18.2，点击召回@10提升+99.5（相对生产基线）；在线A/B测试：查询视图CTR提升+0.259，平均阅读深度提升+0.733；系统自2025年起已在Dewu社区搜索平台全面部署。

Conclusion: SPEAR通过结构化的端到端设计解决了电商搜索中改写与检索的结构性错位问题，证明了改进的改写选择能转化为更强的检索效果和更深的用户参与度，并为该领域提供了可复现的开源实现。

Abstract: Query reformulation bridges user intent and retrieval in e-commerce search, yet production systems optimize rewrite quality and retrieval effectiveness separately, leaving the two stages structurally misaligned. Path-based architectures unify them end-to-end but were designed for personalization, where relevance is not an explicit constraint-search additionally requires the rewrite to remain faithful to the user's stated query intent. Transplanted directly, these models learn a shortcut we term the generic-word dominance effect: they favor generic rewrites that score well on paths but drift from query intent. To address this, we propose SPEAR (Selection-aware Personalized End-to-end Adaptive Rewriting and Retrieval), which integrates three components that each target one failure mode: (1) a dual-embedding backbone with auxiliary loss and gradient isolation that shields recall-side semantics from being eroded by CTR-driven ranking signals; (2) a multiplicative gating aggregator that lets a rewrite score high only when both its confidence and item relevance are strong, eliminating the generic-word shortcut; (3) a Dynamic Rewrite Selector that jointly generates request-specific rewrite weights and user-query-conditioned scale and bias terms, allowing both rewrite preference and relevance calibration to adapt to each request. Offline evaluation on 100K held-out industrial search sessions shows that the proposed framework improves rewrite semantic similarity@10 by +18.2 and click recall@10 by +99.5 over the production baseline. In online A/B testing, SPEAR achieves +0.259 in query-view CTR and +0.733 in average reading depth, confirming that improved rewrite selection translates into stronger retrieval and deeper user engagement. The proposed SPEAR system has been fully deployed in Dewu's community search platform since 2025. Our code is available at https://github.com/mallocagi1-cell/spear.

</details>


### [97] [HyperAgent4POI: Dynamic Semantic Message Passing on Multi-Agent Hypergraphs for Missing-Modality Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01846)
*Jinze Wang,Yuze Liu,Tiehua Zhang,Jiong Jin,Zhu Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HyperAgent4POI是一种基于超图的POI推荐方法，通过动态语义消息传递（DSMP）在超图层内完成模态补全和软关联度细化，利用冻结的Llama骨干网络和角色特定适配器生成节点到超边的消息，在模态缺失场景下显著提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实POI服务中文本和视觉内容常不完整，缺失模态会削弱POI表示，减少排序所需的语义证据，并导致用户-POI高阶交互建模不可靠，因此需要处理模态缺失问题。

Method: 提出HyperAgent4POI，核心是动态语义消息传递（DSMP），在每个超图层内同时进行模态补全和软关联度细化。利用共享冻结Llama骨干的持久节点代理，配合角色特定适配器生成节点到超边的消息；由这些消息形成的语义超边模式（motifs）指导软关联度评分和模态补全；最终节点表示被缓存用于在线排序，无需LLM调用。

Result: 在三个真实LBSN数据集上，HyperAgent4POI在多种模态缺失率下一致优于15个基线。在60%模态缺失率下，三个数据集上NDCG@20平均比最强基线提升8.2%，且缓存推理保证了实际在线效率。

Conclusion: HyperAgent4POI通过超图内的动态语义消息传递机制有效解决了模态缺失问题，在推荐质量和在线效率之间取得了良好平衡，为POI推荐在真实场景下的鲁棒性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation benefits from textual and visual content that describes venue semantics, yet such content is often incomplete in real-world services. Missing modalities weaken POI representations and reduce the semantic evidence available for ranking. The resulting representations also provide unreliable evidence for modeling higher-order user--POI interactions. We propose HyperAgent4POI, which uses Dynamic Semantic Message Passing (DSMP) to perform modality completion and soft incidence refinement within each hypergraph layer. Persistent node agents share a frozen Llama backbone and use role-specific adapters to produce node-to-hyperedge messages. Semantic hyperedge motifs formed from these messages guide soft incidence scoring and modality completion. Final node representations are cached for online ranking without LLM calls. Experiments on three real-world LBSN datasets show consistent ranking gains over 15 baselines across modality-missing rates, while cached inference provides practical online efficiency. Under a 60% modality-missing rate, HyperAgent4POI improves NDCG@20 over the strongest baseline by 8.2% on average across the three datasets.

</details>


### [98] [A Self-Triggered Agentic Push Recommendation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01949)
*Zhao-Yu Zhang,Qingying Chen,Chunyuan Zheng,Jing Zhou,Jian Sun,Siqi Chen,Leiying Chen,Chuan Zhou,Huiyou Jiang,Xin Tao,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: STEPS是一个已在抖音全面部署、服务超过10亿用户的主动式自触发端到端智能推送推荐系统。它将推送推荐重构为自触发的智能体过程，系统不仅决定是否发送推送，还决定何时再次调用自身，从而在实时性和效率间形成闭环平衡。系统由两个基于决策Transformer的智能体（规划智能体和执行智能体）及一个轻量级过滤智能体组成。在线A/B测试显示，该系统使活跃天数提升0.2843%，推送权限关闭率降低1.9089%，计算开销减少79.42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推送系统存在两大被动范式：预规划频率方法（离线建模分配推送时间，缺乏实时适应性）和固定间隔触发方法（周期性轮询系统，在计算开销与最优时机捕捉之间存在严格两难）。此外，多阶段框架严重受限于局部最优解。这些局限促使研究者设计更主动、端到端的推送推荐系统。

Method: STEPS将推送推荐重构为自触发智能体过程，包含三个核心组件：(1) 规划智能体——基于决策Transformer，使用门控序数回归方法调度下一次系统调用时机；(2) 执行智能体——基于决策Transformer，根据轨迹奖励决定是否发送推送；(3) 轻量级过滤智能体——控制计算开销并为不合理的规划行为提供安全保障。

Result: 在线A/B测试表明：STEPS显著提升用户活跃天数0.2843%，降低推送权限关闭率1.9089%，过滤智能体减少计算开销79.42%。该系统已全面部署于拥有超10亿用户的抖音平台。

Conclusion: STEPS通过自触发智能体范式解决了推送系统在实时性与效率之间的根本矛盾，在工业级大规模场景中验证了其有效性，为推送推荐系统设计提供了新的主动式端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Push notification is a critical recommendation scenario on large-scale platforms, allowing the system to proactively reach users outside the application to improve long-term re-engagement. However, designing an optimal push system requires handling a complex action space for the "whether and when" delivery problem under strict system resource constraints. Existing solutions typically fall into two passive paradigms: pre-planned frequency methods that allocate delivery times via offline modeling, limiting real-time adaptability; and fixed-interval triggering methods that periodically poll the system, creating a strict dilemma between excessive computational overhead and diminished optimal timing capture. Furthermore, such multi-stage frameworks severely suffer from local optima. To overcome these limitations, in this paper, we propose STEPS, a proactive, Self-Triggered End-to-end Agentic Push Recommendation System, which is already fully deployed at Douyin with over 1 billion users. STEPS reformulates push recommendation as a self-triggered agentic process in which the system decides not only whether to send a push, but also when to invoke itself again, thereby forming a closed loop that balances real-time effectiveness and efficiency. Specifically, STEPS consists of two decision transformer-based agents: a planning agent that schedules the next system invocation using a gated ordinal regression method, and an execution agent that decides whether to send a push based on trajectory rewards. Furthermore, we introduce a lightweight filtering agent to both control computational overhead and act as a crucial safeguard against unreasonable planning behaviors. Online A/B testing demonstrates that STEPS significantly increases user active days by 0.2843% and reduces the push permission disablement rate by 1.9089%, while the filtering agent reduces computational overhead by 79.42%.

</details>


### [99] [SmartGR: Hierarchy and Beam-Aware Knowledge Distillation for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02048)
*Ziheng Zhang,Yu Cui,Bohao Wang,Yong He,Chao Yu,Chuan Yuan,Wujie Sun,Can Wang,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SmartGR是一个面向生成式推荐（GR）模型的知识蒸馏框架，通过层级感知的SID蒸馏和波束感知的排序蒸馏，解决了GR场景中蒸馏难度不均衡和波束搜索前缀剪枝错误两个特有挑战，在四个基准数据集上平均提升8.6%性能并实现2.39倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐模型在扩展规模时面临推理成本急剧增加的问题。知识蒸馏是有效的解决方案，但现有蒸馏方法未考虑GR模型的两个特有挑战：语义ID层级中蒸馏难度不均衡，以及波束搜索过程中错误的前缀剪枝。

Method: 提出SmartGR框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Hierarchy-Aware SID Distillation，在语义ID层级结构中传递教师模型的建模能力；(2) Beam-Aware Ranking Distillation，在波束搜索过程中蒸馏教师的排序偏好。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，SmartGR在平均提升8.6%的推荐性能的同时，实现了2.39倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: SmartGR通过针对GR特有挑战设计的蒸馏方法，有效平衡了模型性能与推理效率，验证了在生成式推荐场景中专用知识蒸馏框架的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm for recommender systems. Scaling up GR models can improve recommendation performance, but it also substantially increases inference cost. Knowledge distillation provides a practical solution by transferring knowledge from a large GR model to a lightweight one. However, existing distillation methods do not account for two GR-specific challenges: imbalanced distillation difficulty across the semantic ID (SID) hierarchy and incorrect prefix pruning during beam search. To address these challenges, we propose SmartGR, a novel distillation framework that utilizes Hierarchy-Aware SID Distillation to transfer the teacher's modeling capability across the hierarchy and leverages Beam-Aware Ranking Distillation to distill the teacher's ranking preferences during beam search. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of SmartGR, improving the performance by 8.6% while achieving a 2.39$\times$ inference speedup on average.

</details>


### [100] [Douyin Multimodal Embedding Model Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02148)
*Haonan Chen,Chu Li,Zhicheng Wang,Yuanwei Liu,Yuanjiang Wang,Shaohua Jiang,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出抖音多模态嵌入模型（DME），通过两阶段训练策略，结合大规模对比预训练和两种训练时机制（证据锚定类型化潜在推理与跨条件重建），在不增加服务端开销的前提下，同时实现多模态嵌入的高效检索能力和细粒度判别能力，在MMEB-v2基准上达到SOTA，并在抖音生产环境中取得显著效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入模型难以同时满足大规模工业场景的两大需求：对比模型高效但依赖粗粒度的配对级监督，难以进行细粒度硬样本匹配；基于CoT的模型通过显式生成提升判别力但推理开销过大，无法在线部署。作者希望同时兼顾效率与细粒度判别能力。

Method: DME采用两阶段训练：（1）第一阶段进行大规模对比预训练，建立统一的多模态嵌入空间，覆盖广泛的模态与任务；（2）第二阶段通过两种训练时机制补充"语义充分性"——证据锚定类型化潜在推理（将检索证据组织为隐空间潜在推理）和跨条件重建（通过跨方向自回归重建强制执行对端语义）。两种机制仅在训练时生效，推理时只增加极少的查询端开销，因此DME与标准对比编码器同样高效。

Result: 在MMEB-v2基准上，DME-2B和DME-9B分别达到74.8和78.4的SOTA成绩，尤其在视频和视觉文档任务上表现突出。生产中在抖音内部离线评估集上获得2.92%相对提升，已部署于生成式、图片和AI搜索等场景，在线A/B测试中抖音搜索的Lifetime（LT）提升0.1%。

Conclusion: DME验证了通过训练时机制（而非推理时生成）可以有效弥合对比模型与CoT模型之间的差距，在保持对比编码器推理效率的同时获得接近精细推理的判别能力，为工业级多模态检索提供了可行的新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning is a cornerstone of modern AI. By encoding multimodal queries and targets into vectors, it powers industrial search and recommendation and underpins modern agents. Real-world platforms with complex modalities and massive-scale content, such as Douyin, Xiaohongshu, and YouTube, demand both efficiency under billion-scale indexing and fine-grained discrimination for hard matching. Existing MLLM embedding models rarely satisfy both. Contrastive models are efficient but rely on pair-level supervision too coarse for fine-grained distinctions, while CoT-based models improve discrimination through explicit generation impractical to serve online. We present Douyin Multimodal Embedding (DME), a model trained in two stages to combine both strengths. Stage 1 performs large-scale contrastive pre-training that establishes a unified multimodal embedding space with broad modality and task coverage. Stage 2 supplements semantic sufficiency, the property that an embedding is grounded in retrieval-relevant evidence and preserves fine-grained counterpart-side semantics, via two mechanisms. Evidence-Grounded Typed Latent Reasoning organizes retrieval evidence through hidden-space latent reasoning, and Cross-Conditional Reconstruction enforces counterpart-side semantics through cross-directional autoregressive reconstruction. Both act only during training and add only marginal query-side overhead, so DME serves as efficiently as a standard contrastive encoder. On MMEB-v2, DME reaches state-of-the-art results at comparable scales for its 2B and 9B variants (74.8 and 78.4), with especially strong video and visual-document tasks. In production, DME delivers a 2.92% relative gain on Douyin's in-house offline evaluation set, is deployed across Douyin scenarios such as generative, image, and AI search, and yields a 0.1% Lifetime (LT) gain in online A/B testing on Douyin search.

</details>


### [101] [Disentangled Contrastive Learning for Zero-Shot Multilingual Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02189)
*Chao Huang,Yufeng Chen,Changhao Guan,Guang Yang,Dongze Chen,Kaiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦对比学习方法(DCL)，将多语言稠密检索中的表示分离为语义子空间和语言子空间，通过分层语义对齐和语言去偏对比学习减轻语言特征对语义匹配的干扰，实现从英语监督到多语言检索的稳定零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言稠密检索面临低资源语言标注数据稀缺的挑战。现有方法将高资源监督迁移到低资源语言时，共享表示中语义特征与语言特征相互纠缠，会干扰语义相关性优化。因此需要解耦语义和语言特征以提升检索效果。

Method: 提出解耦对比学习(DCL)方法，将多语言表示分离为语义和语言两个子空间。设计基于分层语义对齐（句子级和词元级）和语言去偏对比学习的解耦优化目标，并与检索目标联合优化，以促进从英语监督到多语言稠密检索的稳定零样本迁移。

Result: 在mMARCO和MIRACL基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在多个强基线上持续取得更优性能，验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将语义与语言特征解耦到不同子空间，可以减少语言引起的干扰，从而提升多语言稠密检索的跨语言迁移能力，尤其是在低资源语言场景下表现优异。

Abstract: Multilingual dense retrieval aims to handle queries and documents across different languages based on a unified retriever model. The challenge lies in enabling robust retrieval transfer to low-resource languages where annotated retrieval data is often scarce. Although previous studies transfer high-resource supervision to low-resource languages in multilingual semantic representation learning, the shared representation often entangles semantic and linguistic features, which may interfere with optimizing semantic relevance for retrieval. Different from existing methods that focus on learning language-agnostic semantic features under such entanglement, we propose a disentangled contrastive learning~(DCL) method for multilingual dense retrieval by separating multilingual representations into semantic and linguistic subspaces. Specifically, we design disentangled optimization objectives based on hierarchical semantic alignment and language debiasing contrastive learning. By aligning retrieval-relevant semantics across languages at both sentence and token levels while capturing language-specific variations in the linguistic subspace, these objectives reduce language-induced interference in semantic matching. We jointly optimize them with the retrieval objective to facilitate stable zero-shot transfer from English supervision to multilingual dense retrieval. Extensive experiments on mMARCO and MIRACL show that our method consistently outperforms several strong baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness and generalization ability.

</details>


### [102] [Advancing Relevance Measurement with Vision-Language Models for Web-Scale Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02446)
*Han Wang,Alex Whitworth,Pak Ming Cheung,Zhenjie Zhang,Krishna Kamath,Xi Chen,Roberto Konow,Kurchi Subhra Hazra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出并部署了一个基于VLM（视觉语言模型）的自动化相关性评估流程，用于Pinterest搜索的在线A/B实验中，以替代传统人工标注方式。实验证明VLM生成的判断与人类标注高度一致，在保证可靠性的同时大幅提升评估效率，并解锁了更大规模查询集扩展和更优采样设计的可能性，最终降低了实验中的最小可检测效应（MDE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的人工标注方式进行相关性评估成本高昂且周期长，限制了其可扩展性，无法满足个性化搜索系统在线实验对评估效率和规模的需求。

Method: 构建了一个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动化相关性评估管道，并部署在Pinterest搜索的在线A/B实验中。通过严格验证VLM生成的判断与人工标注之间的一致性，证明VLM可以提供可靠的相关性测量。

Result: VLM生成的评估与人类标注高度一致，能够提供可靠的相关性测量并大幅提高评估效率。该方法还支持扩展查询集、优化采样设计、更高效地评估更广泛的搜索体验，最终提高了相关性指标质量并显著降低了在线实验测量中的最小可检测效应（MDE）。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的自动化相关性评估是一种可行且高效的替代方案，能够在保证评估质量的同时突破人工标注的可扩展性瓶颈，为大规模在线搜索实验提供更可靠和更敏感的度量基础。

Abstract: Relevance evaluation plays a crucial role in personalized search systems, serving as a guardrail alongside user engagement metrics to ensure that search results align with user queries and intent. While human annotation is the traditional method for relevance evaluation, its high cost and long turnaround time limit its scalability. In this work, we present a VLM-based automated relevance evaluation pipeline deployed within Pinterest Search for online A/B experiments. We rigorously validate the alignment between VLM-generated judgments and human annotations, demonstrating that VLMs can provide reliable relevance measurement for experiments while greatly improving the evaluation efficiency. Leveraging VLM-based labeling further unlocks opportunities to expand the query set, optimize sampling design, and efficiently assess a wider range of search experiences at scale. This approach leads to higher-quality relevance metrics and significantly reduces the Minimum Detectable Effects (MDEs) in online experiment measurements.

</details>


### [103] [Syntax Meets Semantics: Understanding Scientific Formulae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02457)
*Yuni Susanti,Moritz Schubotz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了学术文献中数学公式的句法（结构）与语义两种模态之间的对应关系，发现其原始表示空间存在显著的表示不匹配（弱可观察对应但强潜在相关）。通过基于图的句法编码器和基于文本的语义编码器，结合对比学习进行表示对齐，可显著提升跨模态检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学公式是学术交流的基本组成部分，其结构化句法与语义载体的双重属性在学术信息检索中尚未被充分探索。尽管已有研究表明联合建模句法和语义模态能提升检索性能，但两种模态底层表示之间的关系从未被系统研究过，因此需要实证检验二者之间的对应性。

Method: 首先实证分析公式句法表示空间与语义表示空间之间的对应关系（衡量可观察对应性和潜在相关性）；然后使用基于图的编码器表示句法结构、基于文本的编码器表示语义信息，并应用对比学习技术诱导共享表示空间，以评估标准表示学习和对齐技术能否缩小两模态间的表示差距。

Result: 研究发现公式的句法与语义原始表示空间之间存在极弱的可观察对应关系，但存在很强的潜在相关性，表明两种模态之间存在实质性的表示不匹配。经过对比学习对齐后，跨模态检索性能得到显著提升，说明显式的表示学习能够恢复原始表示空间中所缺失的对应关系。

Conclusion: 标准表示学习与对齐技术（如对比学习）能够有效弥合公式句法与语义模态之间的表示鸿沟，显式学习可以恢复原始空间中不存在的跨模态对应关系，从而显著改善学术文献中的公式跨模态检索效果。

Abstract: Scientific formulae are a fundamental component of scholarly communication, yet their dual nature -- as structured syntax and carriers of semantics -- remains underexplored in scholarly information retrieval. Although prior studies show that jointly modeling syntactic and semantic modalities improves retrieval performance, the relationship between their underlying representations has not been systematically investigated. In this work, we empirically study cross-modal correspondence between formula syntax and semantics. We find that their native representation spaces exhibit extremely weak observable correspondence despite strong latent correlation, indicating a substantial representation mismatch between the two modalities. We further evaluate whether this mismatch can be reduced using standard representation learning and alignment techniques. We represent syntactic structure using graph-based encoders and semantic information using text-based encoders, then apply contrastive learning to induce a shared representation space. Results show that the learned alignment substantially improves cross-modal retrieval, suggesting that explicit representation learning can recover correspondence absent from the original representation spaces.

</details>


### [104] [Unpaired Modality-Agnostic Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02477)
*Weihao Shen,Wei Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Meng Yuan,Yuqin Lan,Guojun Liu,Qingsong Hua,Wei Lin,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UnpairGR 提出了一种无需配对样本的多模态生成式推荐方法，通过将模态特定处理限制在轻量输入投影中、共享 Transformer 和残差码本，从而在配对的图文观测和不配对的单模态观测中学习统一的语义 ID 空间，并在三个基准数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态生成式推荐（GR）方法依赖逐项配对的视觉和文本观测来构建语义 ID，这限制了 tokenization 只能在模态交集上进行。而将未配对观测纳入学习又很困难，因为微小的表征偏移可能跨越量化边界，产生不兼容的标识符序列，从而阻碍了利用更丰富的单模态数据。

Method: UnpairGR 将模态特定的处理限制在轻量输入投影层中，共享后续的 Transformer 和残差码本。配对观测用于建立可靠性引导的跨模态共识，而单模态观测直接精化相同的表征和码字。学习后的 tokenizer 被固定，为单一的自回归推荐器提供稳定的训练目标，无需特征插补、模态特定码本或回退映射。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，UnpairGR 在完全观测和观测不完整的设置下均一致地提升了推荐性能。

Conclusion: UnpairGR 成功解决了多模态 GR 中未配对数据的利用问题，通过统一语义 ID 空间的设计，在无需配对样本的前提下有效融合了多模态信息，为生成式推荐提供了更鲁棒和泛化的方案。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation over discrete semantic identifiers (IDs). Although recent multimodal GR methods improve semantic ID construction with visual and textual information, they typically require item-level paired observations, restricting tokenization to the intersection of modality availability. Moreover, incorporating unpaired observations is nontrivial because small representation shifts may cross quantization boundaries and produce incompatible identifier sequences. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{Unpair}ed Modality-Agnostic \textbf{G}enerative \textbf{R}ecommendation (UnpairGR), which learns a unified semantic-ID space from paired, image-only, and text-only observations. UnpairGR confines modality-specific processing to lightweight input projections while sharing the subsequent Transformer and residual codebooks across all observation conditions. Paired observations establish a reliability-guided cross-modal consensus, whereas unimodal observations directly refine the same representations and codes. The learned tokenizer is then fixed to provide stationary targets for a single autoregressive recommender, without feature imputation, modality-specific codebooks, or fallback mappings. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that UnpairGR consistently improves recommendation performance under both fully observed and incomplete-observation settings.

</details>


### [105] [Requirement--Evidence Alignment for Compositional E-Commerce Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02500)
*Weihao Shen,Wei Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Meng Yuan,Yuqin Lan,Guojun Liu,Qingsong Hua,Wei Lin,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出REAlign框架，一种面向组合式电商查询的需求-证据对齐重排序方法，通过区分满足/违反/不支持条件、构建需求定向对比并采用需求感知分组相对策略优化，在固定池电商基准上超越强基线，减少违规并提升浅层排名效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商重排序模型将多个查询需求折叠为单一相关性分数，导致无法正确处理组合式查询（多个需求须同时满足），常常将主题近似但不可行的商品排在可行商品之前，缺乏对需求满足状态的显式建模。

Method: REAlign框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 将类型化查询需求与可见证据显式对齐，区分满足、违反、不支持三种状态；(2) 构建需求定向对比样本以暴露失败模式；(3) 通过需求感知分组相对策略优化（Requirement-Aware Group-Relative Policy Optimization）优化去重部分排名，其列表效用同时考虑相关性、需求满足度、证据支持、实质性违规和输出有效性。

Result: 在两个固定池电商基准上的实验表明，在匹配训练预算下，REAlign一致优于强监督基线和策略优化基线，顶部候选排名中违规更少，浅层排名增益更大。受控消融实验确认了需求建模、证据基础和分解优化的互补价值。

Conclusion: 显式建模查询需求与商品证据的对齐关系、并采用需求感知的列表级优化，是提升电商组合式查询重排序效果的有效途径，能为后续研究提供新的框架基础。

Abstract: Compositional e-commerce queries express multiple requirements that must hold jointly, yet existing rerankers collapse these constraints into aggregate relevance and often promote topical near misses over feasible products. In this paper, we introduce REAlign, a novel requirement-evidence-aligned reranking framework that explicitly connects typed query requirements with visible evidence. REAlign distinguishes satisfied, violated, and unsupported conditions, constructs requirement-targeted contrasts that expose failure modes, and optimizes duplicate-free partial rankings through Requirement-Aware Group-Relative Policy Optimization. Its list utility preserves relevance while incorporating requirement satisfaction, evidence support, material violations, and output validity. Experiments on two fixed-pool e-commerce benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong supervised and policy-optimization baselines under matched training budgets, with fewer violations among top-ranked candidates and larger gains at shallow ranks. Controlled ablations confirm the complementary value of requirement modeling, evidence grounding, and decomposed optimization.

</details>


### [106] [Between-User Collapse Under Popularity-Biased Feedback: A Centered-Covariance Theorem and Computable Phase Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02548)
*Sahil Medepalli*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了流行度偏置的BPR训练如何重塑协同过滤嵌入的用户间几何结构。作者证明在流行度偏置反馈和固定物品条件下，均值中心化的用户协方差C收敛到与物品噪声协方差Q成正比的稳态，导致用户间分布坍缩到噪声基底。文章推导了一个可计算的相变边界，并发现该效应在部署规模下虽真实存在但很小，对推荐指标无实际影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作关注未中心化的二阶矩，而作者认为均值中心化的用户协方差C才是衡量用户间可区分性的正确对象。理解流行度偏置BPR训练如何影响用户嵌入的几何分布，对于判断推荐系统是否陷入"强坍缩"状态至关重要，且需要可计算的方法来判断部署系统是否处于危险区域。

Method: 采用均值中心化的用户协方差C=(1/n)UᵀHU作为分析对象，通过数学证明在流行度偏置反馈下C收敛到与物品噪声协方差Q成正比的稳态。推导出训练超参数(α, λ_neg, γ, d)间的闭式可计算相变边界，区分嵌入空间的收缩与扩张行为，并在MovieLens-25M上验证方向性预测。

Result: 在MovieLens-25M上验证了相变边界的预测方向。发现部署规模正则化下预测的收缩虽真实存在且由策略驱动，但幅度很小，未反映在任何推荐级别指标上。α驱动的各向异性坍缩机制仅在正则化强度损害推荐器时起作用。理论推导的部署时恢复干预未能改善推荐质量。

Conclusion: 相变边界可从训练好的模型嵌入、物品交互计数和训练超参数计算，使实践者无需模拟反馈循环即可检查部署系统是否处于强坍缩区域。实验表明可部署设置远未达到该区域，说明流行度偏置BPR的用户间坍缩效应在实际场景中影响有限。

Abstract: We study how popularity-biased BPR training reshapes the between-user geometry of collaborative-filtering embeddings. We work with the mean-centered user covariance $C=\tfrac1n U^\top H U$, the object that measures how distinguishable users are from one another, as opposed to the uncentered second moment used in prior work. We prove that under popularity-biased feedback with stationary items, $C$ converges to a steady state proportional to the item-noise covariance $Q$. Thus between-user spread collapses toward a noise floor. We derive a closed-form, computable phase boundary in the training hyperparameters $(α,λ_{neg},γ,d)$ separating contraction from expansion, and validate both directional predictions on MovieLens-25M. We then examine the limits of the effect. At deployment-scale regularization the predicted contraction is real and policy-driven but small, and it is not reflected in any recommendation-level metric we measured. The $α$-driven anisotropic-collapse mechanism operates only at regularization strengths that degrade the recommender. A deployment-time restoration intervention derived from the theory does not improve recommendation quality. The boundary is computable from a trained model's embeddings, item interaction counts, and training hyperparameters, so a practitioner can check whether a deployed system sits in the strong-collapse regime without simulating the feedback loop. In our experiments the boundary places deployable settings far from that regime.

</details>


### [107] [Beyond the Final Prompt: Measuring the Effect of Within-Conversation Context on AI Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02556)
*Benjamin Tannenbaum*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究系统性地评估了AI系统中被忽略的多轮对话上下文对回答的影响。通过对180个英语多轮对话进行比较，发现完整对话与仅用最后一条消息的回答在44.7%的情况下存在实质性差异，而加入压缩前缀（最多160词）可将差异率降低至30.8%。压缩前缀虽能显著缩小满意度差距（从0.49分降至0.01分），但仍无法完全替代完整对话上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统评估中，通常将孤立的最终用户消息视为查询，忽略了对话中分散在前序轮次的可操作请求。这种评估方式可能掩盖实际部署中的性能缺陷，因此需要直接检验遗漏的对话内上下文是否改变回答质量。

Method: 从受管控的商业语料库和公开的PRISM数据集中采样180个多轮英语对话。对每个对话，保持最终用户消息和请求的回答模型不变，生成三种回答：(1)完整角色标记对话、(2)仅最终消息、(3)最终消息加上限160词的仅前缀重建。使用独立的评判模型在随机标签下评估回答，并以逆概率加权到符合条件的群体。主要终点为可能导致用户行为改变的材料性差异。

Result: 完整对话与孤立最终消息的回答在44.7%的情况下存在材料性差异（95% Bootstrap CI 33.8%-56.1%）。完整对话回答在0-4分的请求满意度量表上平均高0.49分（0.32-0.67）。加入压缩前缀后，材料性差异率降至30.8%（20.2%-42.1%），减少13.9个百分点（4.9%-24.1%），平均满意度差距降至0.01分（-0.12至0.13）。但近三分之一的回答仍存在材料性差异。顺序交换重复实验显示91.7%的一致性和k=0.83的kappa系数。

Conclusion: 多轮对话的前序上下文对AI回答有实质影响，忽略它将导致约45%的情况产生材料性差异。压缩前缀是有效但不完美的替代方案——它能消除满意度差距但无法完全复现完整上下文的回答。研究局限在于仅涉及同一对话内的前序轮次，未测试跨对话的持久记忆。

Abstract: An isolated final user message is often treated as the query in evaluations of AI systems. In a conversation, however, the actionable request may be distributed across preceding turns. We directly test whether that omitted within-conversation context changes answers. For each of 180 English multi-turn conversations sampled from a governed commercial corpus and the public PRISM dataset, we hold the final user message and requested answer model constant while generating three answers: one from the full role-labelled conversation, one from the final message alone, and one from the final message plus a prefix-only reconstruction capped at 160 words. A separately requested judge model evaluates answers under randomized labels. The prespecified primary endpoint is a material difference that could change what the user does, rather than a difference in style or detail. After inverse-probability weighting to the eligible cohorts, the full-conversation and isolated-final answers differ materially in 44.7% of cases (95% bootstrap CI 33.8% to 56.1%). Full-conversation answers score 0.49 points higher on a 0 to 4 request-satisfaction scale (0.32 to 0.67). Adding the compressed prefix reduces the material-difference rate to 30.8% (20.2% to 42.1%), a 13.9-point reduction (4.9% to 24.1%), and reduces the mean satisfaction gap to 0.01 points (-0.12 to 0.13). Yet compression is not equivalent to the complete dialogue context: almost one third of answers remain materially different. An order-swapped repeat on 48 cases yields 91.7% agreement and kappa = 0.83 for the primary decision. The study concerns preceding turns in the same conversation and does not test persistent memory across separate conversations.

</details>
